/** * OWH Properties — Apps Script backend (owh-properties) * ------------------------------------------------------------------ * ONE read endpoint: props_get_all. The sheet does all the P&L math; * this only reads computed values and shapes them as JSON for the app. * Contract: OWH_props_get_all_spec.md · Rules: OWH_Data_Sources_and_Rules.md * * DEPLOY (Sarah): * 1. Extensions → Apps Script (this is bound to "OWH Properties"). * 2. Paste this file as Code.gs. Save. * 3. Deploy → New deployment → Web app. * Execute as: Me · Who has access: Anyone with the link * (so it can read the private sheet as owner). * 4. Copy the /exec URL into the app config. * 5. Triggers (clock icon) → add a time-driven trigger for keepWarm(), * every 5 minutes (beats cold starts; pairs with the client's 1-retry wrapper). * * TEST in the browser: ?fn=props_get_all&user=sarah * ------------------------------------------------------------------ * v1 scope: meta · fx · flats · targets · pnl · portfolio (Close screen, §7 build order). * Not yet: today, thisWeek, pace, internet, stats, stays, drill-down lines — added per screen. */ // ---- config ------------------------------------------------------- var API_TOKEN = ''; // optional read secret; '' = off. If set, client must pass &token=... // Writes, diagnostics and password changes now require a valid ADMIN SESSION — no shared secret can // authorise them. 'OWH-26-KS' is BURNED: it was published in the WeShare front-end (public GitHub // Pages), so anyone could read it. Never put a secret in a file served to a browser. var SYNC_TOKEN = 'OWH-SYNC-5dOHMVw5ihUeCxt0S1NhKYgHMN7X'; // ONLY authorises fn=props_sync_all (idempotent, returns no data). // READ-ONLY debugging access. Lets a diagnosis happen without handing over an admin session // (which would carry write access to the P&L). It can ONLY call the read functions in DIAG_FNS — // never a write, never set_password, never the user/hash dump (diag_users). // // NO TOKEN LIVES IN THIS FILE. It is generated on demand and stored in Script Properties, so it is // switched on and off from the sheet menu — "The Hub → Debug access" — with **no code edit and no // redeploy**. Each grant mints a fresh token and **expires automatically**, so access lapses even // if nobody remembers to revoke it. "Turn off now" kills it instantly. // ⚠️ While on, it can read the full P&L. Grant only for as long as a diagnosis needs. // Exactly the endpoints wired to accept it — all read-only. Keep this list and the wiring in step: // listing an fn here does nothing unless that endpoint also checks diagOk_. var DIAG_FNS = ['props_get_all', 'diag_getall', 'diag_tabs', 'diag_repairs', 'diag_smoobu_raw', 'stays_row', 'diag_tab', 'diag_triggers']; // Keep it server-side (WeShare's Code.gs), never in an .html file. var TZ = 'Africa/Cairo'; // business timezone — 'today' and all dates use this var VERSION = 138; // bump on every paste so the live /exec can be identified /* ---- Emad notifications (Sarah 2026-08-01: "if i submit a new schedule or a new repair, Emad is instantly notified"). Two channels per event: 1. dcTouch_() stamps the DC workbook's Meta!A1 — Emad's app compares the stamp per request and rebuilds its cached panel the moment it differs, so the app is fresh on his NEXT open. 2. Telegram, via the same OWH bot WeShare already uses for Sarah/Khaled. Messages go to everyone in TG_CHAT_IDS — Sarah asked that she and Khaled get the same alerts. An empty id is skipped silently: Emad's stays empty until he has pressed Start on the bot (Telegram forbids a bot from messaging first); fill it and redeploy. */ var TG_HUB_BOT_TOKEN = '8941013191:AAHB1giDmXw_BWl7Wvp2m7TSFnrYMKjRvFw'; // @Owh_hub_bot — sender reads "The Hub - OWH" var TG_CHAT_IDS = { sarah: '8657440374', khaled: '8606311586', emad: '1162238856' // @OmdaHM — read from getUpdates 2026-08-02 after his /start + «أنا عماد» }; /* Every line gets an invisible RIGHT-TO-LEFT MARK: Telegram aligns each line by its first strong character, so "17A: ..." rendered LTR next to Arabic lines rendered RTL — the zigzag Sarah saw. The mark makes every line RTL regardless of what it starts with. */ function tgRtl_(text) { return String(text).split('\n').map(function (l) { return '‏' + l; }).join('\n'); } /* Telegram text must not mix Arabic and Latin LETTERS (Sarah 2026-08-04) — flat codes become Arabic inside the sender so every message is covered in one place. Screens keep 17A/8A. */ function tgArabizeFlats_(t) { return String(t).replace(/\bZayed 2000\b/g, 'زايد 2000').replace(/\b17A\b/g, '١٧أ') .replace(/\b17B\b/g, '١٧ب').replace(/\b28A\b/g, '٢٨أ').replace(/\b8A\b/g, '٨أ'); } function tgHubNotify_(text, opts) { text = tgRtl_(tgArabizeFlats_(text)); var out = {}; for (var who in TG_CHAT_IDS) { if (!TG_CHAT_IDS[who]) { out[who] = 'skipped'; continue; } if (opts && opts.noEmad && who === 'emad') { out[who] = 'excluded'; continue; } // rule: financial content never reaches Emad if (opts && opts.only && who !== opts.only) { out[who] = 'excluded'; continue; } // single-recipient variant (Emad's operations-only copy) try { UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.telegram.org/bot' + TG_HUB_BOT_TOKEN + '/sendMessage', { method: 'post', muteHttpExceptions: true, payload: { chat_id: TG_CHAT_IDS[who], text: text } }); out[who] = 'sent'; } catch (e) { out[who] = String(e && e.message || e); } } return out; } /* Sends a test line to every configured chat and SHOWS the per-person result — because a Telegram failure is silent by design in production (a notification must never break a booking write), and that silence once hid "chat not found" for days. */ function tgHubTest() { var res = tgHubNotify_('🔧 اختبار من «الهاب» — لو وصلتك الرسالة دي يبقى كله تمام ✅'); var lines = []; for (var who in res) lines.push(' ' + who + ': ' + res[who]); SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Telegram test (Hub bot)\n\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n\n"sent" = accepted by Telegram. "chat not found" = that person never pressed Start on @Owh_hub_bot.' + '\nEmad "skipped" = his chat id is not filled in yet.'); } /* ---- Alerts spec C3 — the Thursday 20:30 pre-scheduling digest (Sarah+Khaled). Everything open going into the weekly planning session, one message. Sarah sets the time trigger once: editor → Triggers → weeklyDigest → Time-driven → Week timer → Thursday → 8-9pm. */ function weeklyDigest() { /* Audience is Sarah AND Khaled — imperatives in these messages are plural or noun-based, never gendered singular (the first draft said تعملي and read wrong on Khaled's phone). */ // Last-run stamp (2026-08-07): the Thursday reminder silently didn't arrive and there was no way // to tell "trigger never fired" from "fired but Telegram failed". diag_triggers reads this back. try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('WEEKLY_DIGEST_LAST', Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')); } catch (eWD) {} var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), lines = ['📋 قبل جدولة الأسبوع:']; try { var reps = buildRepairs(ss).filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'open'; }); if (reps.length) { var oldest = null; reps.forEach(function (r) { if (r.date && (!oldest || r.date < oldest)) oldest = r.date; }); lines.push('🔧 ' + reps.length + ' بلاغ صيانة مفتوح' + (oldest ? ' (أقدمهم ' + oldest + ')' : '')); } } catch (e) {} try { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var pend = parseInt(sp.getProperty('TG_LAST_PENDING') || '0', 10); if (pend > 0) lines.push('💳 ' + pend + ' مصروف مستني تأكيد في WeShare'); var dec = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('ELEC_DECISIONS') || '[]'); if (dec.length) lines.push('⚡ ' + dec.length + ' فاتورة كهرباء مستنية قرار'); var m8 = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('ELEC_METER8A') || 'null'); if (m8 && m8.balance != null) lines.push('⚡ كارت 8A: ~' + Math.round(m8.projectedBalance) + ' ج' + (m8.readAgoDays > 5 ? ' (آخر قراءة من ' + m8.readAgoDays + ' يوم — محتاجة تحديث)' : '')); } catch (e) {} try { // stock shortages, from the الكنترول workbook var inv = SpreadsheetApp.openById(DRYCLEAN_ID).getSheetByName('InvItems'); if (inv && inv.getLastRow() > 1) { var iv = inv.getRange(2, 1, inv.getLastRow() - 1, 11).getValues(), short = 0; iv.forEach(function (r) { var active = String(r[8]).toLowerCase() !== 'false' && r[8] !== false; var noR = r[10] === true || String(r[10]).toLowerCase() === 'true'; var par = Number(r[6]) || 0, cur = Number(r[7]) || 0; if (active && !noR && par > 0 && cur < 0.6 * par) short++; }); if (short) lines.push('🧴 ' + short + ' صنف ناقص في المخزون (تحت ٦٠٪)'); } } catch (e) {} try { // internet bundles under 20% (weekly line until §5.4 lands) var net = buildInternet(ss), lastByFlat = {}; (net.readings || []).forEach(function (r) { lastByFlat[r.flat] = r; }); // readings are appended in order (net.bundles || []).forEach(function (b) { var rd = lastByFlat[b.flat]; if (rd && b.quota > 0 && rd.remaining != null && rd.remaining < 0.2 * b.quota) lines.push('📶 ' + b.flat + ': باقي ' + rd.remaining + ' جيجا من ' + b.quota); }); } catch (e) {} if (lines.length === 1) lines.push('كله تمام ✨ مفيش حاجة معلقة.'); tgHubNotify_(lines.join('\n'), { noEmad: true }); return { lines: lines.length - 1 }; } /* The Thursday digest never fired on 2026-08-06 — it relied on a trigger created BY HAND in the editor, and a manual step that was never done fails silently forever. This installs it idempotently from the menu, same pattern as every other installer here. */ function weeklyDigestInstallTrigger() { ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'weeklyDigest') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('weeklyDigest').timeBased().onWeekDay(ScriptApp.WeekDay.THURSDAY).atHour(20).create(); return 'installed (Thursday 8-9pm)'; } function weeklyDigestInstallPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var r = weeklyDigestInstallTrigger(); var last = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('WEEKLY_DIGEST_LAST'); ui.alert('Thursday digest\n\n' + r + '\nlast actually ran: ' + (last || 'never') + '\n\nIt lists everything open before the weekly scheduling session (repairs, pending WeShare, electricity decisions, stock, internet).'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Install failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* ============================================================ * §5.4 A6 — INTERNET DEPLETION WATCH. The occupancy-aware pace model, server-side: * rate priority = current guest's own measured GB/occupied-day (across cycle renewals — a renewal * resets the bundle, not the guest) → flat history → portfolio → quota/30. Vacant days burn at the * flat's measured idle trickle (Zayed's real data: ~2 GB/day), never at the guest rate. Projection * walks the BOOKED calendar. Alert = ONE DAY before projected depletion (Sarah's spec), announce- * once per bundle cycle. KEEP IN STEP with netCalc/netHistory in internet.html — same model. * ============================================================ */ function netWatchCandidates_() { try { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var net = buildInternet(ss), stays = buildStaysM_(ss); // memoized — 3 alert paths shared one sync var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), DAY = 86400000; var live = stays.filter(function (s) { var st = (s.status || '').toLowerCase(); return s.portal !== 'Blocked' && st.indexOf('cancel') < 0 && s.checkIn && s.checkOut; }); function occ(flat, ymdStr) { for (var i = 0; i < live.length; i++) { var s = live[i]; if (s.flat === flat && s.checkIn.slice(0, 10) <= ymdStr && ymdStr < s.checkOut.slice(0, 10)) return flat + '|' + (s.guest || s.id); } return null; } var pd = function (t) { var d = new Date(String(t).replace(' ', 'T')); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d; }; var G = {}, F = {}, V = {}, P = { gb: 0, days: 0 }; var byFlat = {}; (net.bundles || []).forEach(function (b) { var cs = pd(b.cycleStart); if (!cs) return; (byFlat[b.flat] = byFlat[b.flat] || []).push({ b: b, cs: cs }); }); Object.keys(byFlat).forEach(function (flat) { var bs = byFlat[flat].sort(function (a, z) { return a.cs - z.cs; }); bs.forEach(function (x, bi) { var ceN = bs[bi + 1] ? bs[bi + 1].cs : null; var reads = (net.readings || []).filter(function (r) { var t = pd(r.at); return r.flat === flat && t && t >= x.cs && (!ceN || t < ceN); }) .map(function (r) { return { t: pd(r.at), rem: r.remaining }; }) .sort(function (a, z) { return a.t - z.t; }); var pts = [{ t: x.cs, rem: x.b.quota }].concat(reads); /* THE HOUSE CLOCK (2026-08-07) — keep in step with internet.html netHistory. Ownership was per DAY at noon, so on a changeover day the incoming guest owned the whole date: the outgoing guest's last morning plus the empty 12:00→15:00 gap landed on someone who had just walked in (Iman: 8.3 GB after 49 minutes), and that phantom became their "measured" pace. A stay occupies 15:00 check-in day → 12:00 check-out day; intervals are sliced at those boundaries and the changeover gap burns as vacant idle. */ var occs = live.filter(function (s) { return s.flat === flat; }).map(function (s) { return { key: flat + '|' + (s.guest || s.id), s0: new Date(s.checkIn.slice(0, 10) + 'T15:00:00').getTime(), s1: new Date(s.checkOut.slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').getTime() }; }).filter(function (o) { return o.s1 > o.s0 && !isNaN(o.s0) && !isNaN(o.s1); }); var ownerAt = function (T) { for (var oi = 0; oi < occs.length; oi++) if (T >= occs[oi].s0 && T < occs[oi].s1) return occs[oi].key; return null; }; for (var i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) { var a = pts[i], z = pts[i + 1], delta = Math.max(0, a.rem - z.rem), tot = z.t - a.t; if (tot <= 0) continue; var cuts = [a.t.getTime(), z.t.getTime()]; occs.forEach(function (o) { if (o.s0 > a.t.getTime() && o.s0 < z.t.getTime()) cuts.push(o.s0); if (o.s1 > a.t.getTime() && o.s1 < z.t.getTime()) cuts.push(o.s1); }); cuts.sort(function (p, q) { return p - q; }); for (var c2 = 0; c2 < cuts.length - 1; c2++) { var s1 = cuts[c2], s2 = cuts[c2 + 1]; if (s2 <= s1) continue; var gb = delta * (s2 - s1) / tot, df = (s2 - s1) / DAY; var k = ownerAt((s1 + s2) / 2); if (k) { (G[k] = G[k] || { gb: 0, days: 0 }); G[k].gb += gb; G[k].days += df; (F[flat] = F[flat] || { gb: 0, days: 0 }); F[flat].gb += gb; F[flat].days += df; P.gb += gb; P.days += df; } else { (V[flat] = V[flat] || { gb: 0, days: 0 }); V[flat].gb += gb; V[flat].days += df; } } } }); }); var cands = []; Object.keys(byFlat).forEach(function (flat) { var bs = byFlat[flat].sort(function (a, z) { return a.cs - z.cs; }); var cur = bs[bs.length - 1]; var reads = (net.readings || []).filter(function (r) { var t = pd(r.at); return r.flat === flat && t && t >= cur.cs; }) .map(function (r) { return { t: pd(r.at), rem: r.remaining }; }).sort(function (a, z) { return a.t - z.t; }); var last = reads.length ? reads[reads.length - 1] : { t: cur.cs, rem: cur.b.quota }; var gk = occ(flat, today); var rate = (gk && G[gk] && G[gk].days >= 0.5) ? G[gk].gb / G[gk].days : (F[flat] && F[flat].days >= 3) ? F[flat].gb / F[flat].days : (P.days >= 3) ? P.gb / P.days : cur.b.quota / 30; var idle = (V[flat] && V[flat].days >= 1) ? Math.min(4, V[flat].gb / V[flat].days) : 1.5; var rem = last.rem, depl = null; var walk0 = new Date(String(Utilities.formatDate(last.t, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd')) + 'T12:00:00'); for (var k2 = 0; k2 < 45 && rem > 0; k2++) { var dk = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(walk0.getTime() + k2 * DAY), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); rem -= occ(flat, dk) ? rate : idle; if (rem <= 0) { depl = dk; break; } } var tomorrow = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Date.now() + DAY), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); if (depl && depl <= tomorrow) cands.push({ key: 'net|' + flat + '|' + Utilities.formatDate(cur.cs, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), text: '📶 إنترنت ' + flat + ' هيخلص تقريباً ' + depl + ' — محتاج تجديد النهارده. (المعدل ~' + Math.round(rate * 10) / 10 + ' جيجا/يوم)' }); }); return cands; } catch (e) { return []; } } /* ---- announce-once (Alerts spec A3/A5): a Needs-you flag becomes ONE Telegram when it first appears, never a repeat on every sync. Announced keys persist in Script Properties; pruned so the store can't grow without bound. */ function tgAnnounceNew_(candidates, noEmad) { if (!candidates.length) return 0; var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), seen = {}; try { seen = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('TG_ANNOUNCED') || '{}'); } catch (e) {} var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), sent = 0; candidates.forEach(function (c) { if (!c.key || seen[c.key]) return; tgHubNotify_(c.text, { noEmad: noEmad !== false }); seen[c.key] = today; sent++; }); var keys = Object.keys(seen); if (keys.length > 300) { // prune oldest ~third by recorded date keys.sort(function (a, b) { return seen[a] < seen[b] ? -1 : 1; }) .slice(0, keys.length - 200).forEach(function (k) { delete seen[k]; }); } sp.setProperty('TG_ANNOUNCED', JSON.stringify(seen)); return sent; } /* Needs-you → Telegram candidates, computed after a sync (Alerts spec A3 + A5). */ function tgNeedsYouCandidates_(elecStep, weshareStep) { var cands = [], sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); // electricity decisions (deferred big bills) ((elecStep && elecStep.deferred) || []).forEach(function (d) { cands.push({ key: 'dec|' + d.flat + '|' + d.billMonth + '|' + d.amount, text: '⚡ ' + d.flat + ' — فاتورة كهرباء محتاجة قرار: ' + Math.round(d.amount).toLocaleString() + ' ج تغطي ' + d.covers.replace('..', ' → ') + '. القرار من قائمة الشيت: apply new bills أو restate one bill.' }); // noun-based, no gendered address }); // 8A card depletion (computed and cached by the same sync) try { var m8 = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('ELEC_METER8A') || 'null'); if (m8 && m8.alert) cands.push({ key: 'm8a|' + m8.depletion, text: '⚡ كارت كهرباء 8A قرب يخلص: باقي ~' + Math.round(m8.projectedBalance) + ' ج، هيفضى ~' + m8.depletion + (m8.nextCheckout && m8.depletion <= m8.nextCheckout ? ' — قبل الـcheck-out الجاي (' + m8.nextCheckout + '). محتاج شحن وتسجيل في WeShare.' : '. محتاج شحن قريب.') }); } catch (e) {} // pending confirmations — announce when the count RISES (a per-row key would need row ids the sync report doesn't carry) var pend = (weshareStep && weshareStep.pendingSkipped) || 0; var last = parseInt(sp.getProperty('TG_LAST_PENDING') || '0', 10); if (pend > last) cands.push({ key: 'pend|' + pend + '|' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), text: '💳 ' + pend + ' مصروف مستني تأكيد مين دفع في WeShare (كان ' + last + '). بعد التأكيد هيدخلوا الـP&L.' }); sp.setProperty('TG_LAST_PENDING', String(pend)); // same-day Direct check-in with no payment route confirmed (spec A5) try { var todayK = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); buildStaysM_(SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet()).forEach(function (s) { var st = (s.status || '').toLowerCase(); if (s.portal !== 'Direct' || s.payRoute || st.indexOf('cancel') > -1) return; if ((s.checkIn || '').slice(0, 10) !== todayK) return; cands.push({ key: 'pay|' + s.id + '|' + todayK, text: '💵 ضيف Direct بيدخل النهارده — ' + s.flat + ' (' + (s.guest || '') + ') ومفيش وسيلة دفع متأكدة. التأكيد من Today → Needs you.' }); }); } catch (e) {} return cands; } /* the instant-freshness stamp: any Hub-side change Emad must see writes a new value here */ function dcTouch_() { try { var dc = SpreadsheetApp.openById(DRYCLEAN_ID), sh = dc.getSheetByName('Meta'); if (!sh) { sh = dc.insertSheet('Meta'); sh.getRange(1, 2).setValue('hub change stamp — Emad\'s app rebuilds its panel when this moves'); } sh.getRange(1, 1).setValue(new Date().toISOString()); } catch (e) {} } /* Costs bought for the whole portfolio (WeShare Apartment = "To allocate") are filed against this pseudo-flat and allocated across the real flats by the sheet. They are NOT a flat, so anything listing per-flat detail has to handle them explicitly or the detail stops matching the total. */ var POOL_FLAT = '(portfolio)'; /* Cost lines a PARTNER does not carry — they come out of our side, not the owner's payout. `rent` because we hold the lease on the flats we run ourselves; `subscription` because Smoobu is our booking software for the whole portfolio, not a cost of their apartment. */ var PARTNER_NOT_BORNE = ['rent', 'subscription']; var TAB = { pnl: 'Monthly P&L', config: 'Config', targets: 'Targets', status: 'Status', fx: 'FX rates' }; // ---- entry point -------------------------------------------------- function doGet(e) { try { var p = {}; if (e && e.parameter) for (var _k in e.parameter) p[_k] = e.parameter[_k]; if (e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) { try { var _b = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents); for (var _k2 in _b) p[_k2] = _b[_k2]; } catch (_ee) {} } // POST text/plain JSON body → params (keeps secrets out of the URL) p.sid = p.sid || p.session; // clients MUST send the token as 'session=': 'sid' is a RESERVED param on script.google.com — Google's edge intercepts it and returns a Drive 400 before the script ever runs var fn = p.fn || 'props_get_all'; // ---- diag: dump the pre-existing 'Users' tab (to check schema + whether set_password disturbed it) if (fn === 'diag_users') { // ADMIN SESSION ONLY — dumps usernames + password hashes/salts. Never reachable with DIAG_TOKEN. if (!writeOk_(p)) return json({ ok: false, error: 'unauthorized', code: 'WRITE_AUTH', version: VERSION }); var uss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), out = {}; ['Users', 'Auth users'].forEach(function (nm) { var s = uss.getSheetByName(nm); out[nm] = s ? s.getRange(1, 1, s.getLastRow(), s.getLastColumn()).getValues().map(function (r) { return r.map(String); }) : null; }); return json({ ok: true, version: VERSION, tabs: out }); } // ---- diag: READ ONE TAB, read-only. &tab= [&book=props|weshare|dryclean] [&limit=&offset=] // Exists so a diagnosis doesn't require pasting sheet contents by hand. // CREDENTIAL TABS ARE NEVER DUMPABLE — 'Auth *', 'Users', 'Sessions' are refused in every // workbook, so this can't be turned into a password-hash reader. Each line renews on its own staggered cycle; the dates live in Script Property PHONE_BILL_DUE (JSON flat→yyyy-MM-dd, editable without code, self-seeded below from Sarah's list). Sarah + Khaled — never Emad — get a reminder 2 days before and a nudge on the due day (announce-once); after the due day the date auto-advances 3 months. Payment itself is a WeShare cash entry (TV/Phone/Internet) — the P&L keeps its monthly accrual. */ function phoneBills_() { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var due = {}; try { due = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('PHONE_BILL_DUE') || '{}'); } catch (e) {} if (due['17A+17B']) { // Sarah 2026-08-04: separate lines, one row each due['17A'] = due['17A'] || due['17A+17B']; due['17B'] = due['17B'] || due['17A+17B']; delete due['17A+17B']; sp.setProperty('PHONE_BILL_DUE', JSON.stringify(due)); } if (!Object.keys(due).length) { due = { '17A': '2026-08-19', '17B': '2026-08-19', 'Zayed 2000': '2026-09-28', '28A': '2026-10-03', '8A': '2026-10-20' }; sp.setProperty('PHONE_BILL_DUE', JSON.stringify(due)); } var pr = {}; try { pr = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('PHONE_BILL_PRICE') || '{}'); } catch (e2) {} var ORDER = ['8A', '17A', '17B', '28A', 'Zayed 2000']; return Object.keys(due).sort(function (a, b) { return ORDER.indexOf(a) - ORDER.indexOf(b); }) .map(function (f) { return { line: f, due: due[f], price: pr[f] != null ? pr[f] : 136.8 }; }); } function phoneBillWatch_() { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var bills = phoneBills_(), due = {}, price = {}; bills.forEach(function (b) { due[b.line] = b.due; price[b.line] = b.price; }); var todayK = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var sent = {}; try { sent = JSON.parse(sp.getProperty('PHONE_BILL_SENT') || '{}'); } catch (e2) {} var changed = false, msgs = []; Object.keys(due).forEach(function (f) { var d = String(due[f]); if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d)) return; var dueD = new Date(d + 'T12:00:00'); var warnK = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(dueD.getTime() - 2 * 86400000), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); if (todayK >= warnK && todayK < d && !sent['w|' + f + '|' + d]) { msgs.push('📞 فاتورة تليفون ' + f + ' (~' + price[f] + ' ج) تستحق يوم ' + d.slice(5).split('-').reverse().join('/') + ' — بعد الدفع سجّلوها في «وي شير»'); sent['w|' + f + '|' + d] = todayK; changed = true; } if (todayK === d && !sent['d|' + f + '|' + d]) { msgs.push('📞 النهارده آخر يوم لفاتورة تليفون ' + f + ' — ماتتأخرش'); sent['d|' + f + '|' + d] = todayK; changed = true; } if (todayK > d) { // passed → next quarter, forever var nx = new Date(dueD); nx.setMonth(nx.getMonth() + 3); due[f] = Utilities.formatDate(nx, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); changed = true; } }); if (msgs.length) tgHubNotify_(msgs.join('\n'), { noEmad: true }); if (changed) { sp.setProperty('PHONE_BILL_DUE', JSON.stringify(due)); var floor = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 86400000), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var pruned = {}; Object.keys(sent).forEach(function (k) { if (String(sent[k]) >= floor) pruned[k] = sent[k]; }); sp.setProperty('PHONE_BILL_SENT', JSON.stringify(pruned)); } return { due: due, sent: msgs.length }; } /* ============================================================ * HUB PAYLOAD CACHE (perf, 2026-08-02). Measured: propsGetAll takes ~10s server-side and its * 460KB answer is identical for every admin request until something changes. So the finished * JSON is stored (gzipped, chunked under the 100KB/slot cache limit) and served until the * change stamp moves. The stamp is a CELL (Meta!A1), not a Script Property, so الكنترول — a * different script that writes our Repairs tab — can move it too. * Who moves the stamp: every write branch in doGet · smoobuSync (30-min trigger) · the sheet * menu tools · manual edits via the simple onEdit trigger below · الكنترول's repair open/close. * Partner/demo payloads are per-flat and rare — they bypass this entirely. * ============================================================ */ var HUB_PAY_TTL = 21600; // 6h backstop for any write path we missed /* SECTION STAMPS (2026-08-04, built for the 4-6-more-apartments future): the payload is cached in six sections, each with its own stamp in Meta!A1:A7 — A1 stays the GLOBAL stamp (any change; what clients and الكنترول watch), A2..A7 are per-section. A write bumps only its section, so a cleaning confirm rebuilds ~0.2s of payload instead of the whole ~4.5s (and the whole ~10s once the portfolio doubles). Row layout is FIXED — الكنترول writes A1+A5 directly. */ var HUB_SECT_ROW = { base: 2, stays: 3, cleans: 4, repairs: 5, internet: 6, defaults: 7 }; function hubMetaSheet_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Meta'); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet('Meta'); sh.getRange(1, 2).setValue('change stamps — A1 global · A2 base · A3 stays · A4 cleans · A5 repairs · A6 internet · A7 defaults'); try { sh.hideSheet(); } catch (eh) {} } return sh; } function hubStamp_() { try { return str(hubMetaSheet_().getRange(1, 1).getValue()); } catch (e) { return 'nostamp-' + new Date().toISOString(); } // unreadable → never matches → always rebuild } function hubSectStamps_() { var out = { global: '' }; try { var v = hubMetaSheet_().getRange(1, 1, 7, 1).getValues(); out.global = str(v[0][0]); for (var tag in HUB_SECT_ROW) { var s = str(v[HUB_SECT_ROW[tag] - 1][0]); out[tag] = s || out.global; // unseeded cell → global (heals on first bump) } } catch (e) { for (var t2 in HUB_SECT_ROW) out[t2] = 'nostamp-' + new Date().toISOString(); } return out; } /* tags: array of section names to bump, or omitted → ALL sections (the safe default). The global stamp A1 always moves — it drives the assembled-payload cache, client caches and الكنترول. */ function hubBump_(tags) { try { var sh = hubMetaSheet_(); var mark = new Date().toISOString() + '·' + Utilities.getUuid().slice(0, 8); var v = sh.getRange(1, 1, 7, 1).getValues(); v[0][0] = mark; var all = !tags || !tags.length; for (var tag in HUB_SECT_ROW) if (all || tags.indexOf(tag) > -1) v[HUB_SECT_ROW[tag] - 1][0] = mark; sh.getRange(1, 1, 7, 1).setValues(v); } catch (e) {} } /* Simple trigger — fires on MANUAL sheet edits only (script writes call hubBump_ themselves). The edited TAB names its section; an unrecognized tab bumps everything (fail-safe). */ var HUB_TAB_SECT = { 'Stays': ['base', 'stays'], 'Revenue apportionment': ['base'], 'Revenue by month': ['base'], 'Monthly P&L': ['base'], 'Cost line items': ['base'], 'Electricity detail': ['base'], 'Direct USD costs': ['base'], 'FX rates': ['base'], 'Month status': ['base'], 'Config': ['base'], 'Targets': ['base'], 'Cleaning schedule': ['cleans'], 'Repairs': ['repairs'], 'Repair trades': ['repairs', 'defaults'], 'Internet bundles': ['internet'], 'Internet readings': ['internet'], 'Internet catalog': ['internet'], 'Payment defaults': ['defaults'], 'Channel aliases': ['defaults'] }; function onEdit(e) { try { var nm = e && e.range ? e.range.getSheet().getName() : ''; if (nm === 'Meta') return; hubBump_(HUB_TAB_SECT[nm] || null); // null → all sections } catch (err) {} } function hubPayloadStore_(dataStr, stamp, pfx) { pfx = pfx || 'hubPay_'; try { var b64 = Utilities.base64Encode(Utilities.gzip(Utilities.newBlob(dataStr)).getBytes()); var SIZE = 90000, parts = []; for (var i = 0; i < b64.length; i += SIZE) parts.push(b64.slice(i, i + SIZE)); if (parts.length > 9) return; // absurdly large — serve uncached rather than thrash var put = {}; put[pfx + 'meta'] = JSON.stringify({ stamp: stamp, n: parts.length, day: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd') }); // meta.asOf must not cross midnight for (var p = 0; p < parts.length; p++) put[pfx + p] = parts[p]; CacheService.getScriptCache().putAll(put, HUB_PAY_TTL); } catch (e) {} } /* THE SECTION ASSEMBLER (2026-08-04). Serves the admin payload by stitching per-section caches, rebuilding ONLY sections whose stamp moved: cheap sections (stays/cleans/repairs/internet/ defaults) live as small JSON cache entries; the expensive BASE (meta+flats+targets+pnl+ portfolio+tx, ~350KB) lives gzip-chunked under 'hubB_' keyed by ITS OWN stamp. A cleaning confirm therefore rebuilds ~0.2s of payload; only money writes/syncs pay the full base cost. Returns the complete data-JSON string; the caller stores it in the assembled caches (hubPay_). */ var HUB_BASE_KEYS = ['meta', 'flats', 'targets', 'pnl', 'portfolio', 'tx']; function hubAssembleAdmin_() { var st = hubSectStamps_(), c = CacheService.getScriptCache(); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var reuse = {}, sectJson = {}; function sect(tag, build) { try { var raw = c.get('hubSect_' + tag); if (raw) { var o = JSON.parse(raw); if (o.s === st[tag]) { sectJson[tag] = o.j; return JSON.parse(o.j); } } } catch (e) {} var v = build(), j = JSON.stringify(v); sectJson[tag] = j; try { c.put('hubSect_' + tag, JSON.stringify({ s: st[tag], j: j }), 21600); } catch (e2) {} return v; } reuse.stays = sect('stays', function () { return buildStays(ss); }); reuse.cleans = sect('cleans', function () { return buildCleans(ss); }); reuse.repairs = sect('repairs', function () { return buildRepairs(ss); }); reuse.internet = sect('internet', function () { return buildInternet(ss); }); reuse.defaults = sect('defaults', function () { return { paymentDefaults: buildPaymentDefaults(ss), channelAliases: buildChannelAliases(ss), repairTrades: repairTrades_(ss) }; }); // base: its own gzip-chunked cache, keyed by the BASE stamp (+ the day guard inside the store) var baseStr = hubPayloadGet_(st.base, 'hubB_'); if (!baseStr) { var full = propsGetAll({ role: 'admin', user: 'assemble', flat: '' }, 'full', reuse); var baseObj = {}; HUB_BASE_KEYS.forEach(function (k) { baseObj[k] = full[k]; }); baseStr = JSON.stringify(baseObj); hubPayloadStore_(baseStr, st.base, 'hubB_'); } // stitch: base's outer braces open the object; the cheap sections append as top-level keys return baseStr.slice(0, -1) + ',"stays":' + sectJson.stays + ',"cleans":' + sectJson.cleans + ',"repairs":' + sectJson.repairs + ',"internet":'+ sectJson.internet + ',"paymentDefaults":' + JSON.stringify(reuse.defaults.paymentDefaults) + ',"channelAliases":' + JSON.stringify(reuse.defaults.channelAliases) + ',"repairTrades":' + JSON.stringify(reuse.defaults.repairTrades) + '}'; } // meta-only freshness probe (~50ms) — keepWarm's every-minute check must not unzip the payload function hubPayFresh_(stamp, pfx) { try { var m = CacheService.getScriptCache().get((pfx || 'hubPay_') + 'meta'); if (!m) return false; m = JSON.parse(m); return m.stamp === stamp && m.day === Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } catch (e) { return false; } } function hubPayloadGet_(stamp, pfx) { pfx = pfx || 'hubPay_'; try { var c = CacheService.getScriptCache(), m = c.get(pfx + 'meta'); if (!m) return null; m = JSON.parse(m); if (m.stamp !== stamp) return null; if (m.day !== Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd')) return null; var keys = []; for (var i = 0; i < m.n; i++) keys.push(pfx + i); var got = c.getAll(keys), b64 = ''; for (var j = 0; j < m.n; j++) { if (got[pfx + j] == null) return null; b64 += got[pfx + j]; } return Utilities.ungzip(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(b64), 'application/x-gzip')).getDataAsString(); } catch (e) { return null; } } /* scope=live (perf fix #3, agreed 2026-08-02): the everyday screens (Today/Week/Internet/Repairs) don't read `tx` at all, and only need recent stays and 2 months of P&L — so they get the full payload minus: tx (dropped), stays older than 90 days (checkout-based; future always kept), pnl/portfolio months before the last 3. Stats/Close/partners keep scope=full untouched. RULE: a screen that starts reading tx or older months must switch itself back to scope=full. */ function hubTrimLive_(data) { var out = {}; for (var k in data) out[k] = data[k]; delete out.tx; var now = new Date(); var stayFloor = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(now.getTime() - 90 * 86400000), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); out.stays = (data.stays || []).filter(function (s) { return !s.checkOut || s.checkOut >= stayFloor; }); var mFloor = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 2, 1), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); var pnl = {}; for (var f in (data.pnl || {})) { var src = data.pnl[f], dst = {}; for (var m in src) if (m >= mFloor) dst[m] = src[m]; pnl[f] = dst; } out.pnl = pnl; if (data.portfolio) { var pf = {}; for (var m2 in data.portfolio) if (m2 >= mFloor) pf[m2] = data.portfolio[m2]; out.portfolio = pf; } return out; } // ---- role --------------------------------------------------------- function resolveRole(user) { user = (user || '').toString().trim(); if (user === 'sarah' || user === 'khaled') return { role: 'admin', user: user }; if (user.indexOf('partner_') === 0) return { role: 'partner', user: user, flat: user.slice(8) }; return { role: 'admin', user: user || 'sarah' }; // default admin; tighten once partner logins exist } // ============================================================ // SESSION AUTH (Step 1) — login issues a token; validateSession_ gates endpoints. Passwords = salted SHA-256. // Users tab: Username · PasswordHash · Salt · Role(admin|partner) · Flat(partner's flat). Sessions tab: Token · Username · Expires · CreatedAt. // ============================================================ var SESSION_TTL_DAYS = 30; // dedicated tabs ('Auth users' / 'Auth sessions') so we never collide with any pre-existing 'Users' tab function usersSheet_(ss) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Auth users'); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet('Auth users'); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([['Username', 'PasswordHash', 'Salt', 'Role', 'Flat']]); sh.getRange(2, 1, 2, 5).setValues([['sarah', '', '', 'admin', ''], ['khaled', '', '', 'admin', '']]); // seeded; passwords set via set_password } return sh; } function sessionsSheet_(ss) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Auth sessions'); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet('Auth sessions'); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([['Token', 'Username', 'Expires', 'CreatedAt']]); } return sh; } function hashPw_(pw, salt) { return Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, salt + '|' + pw + '|' + salt) .map(function (b) { return ('0' + (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join(''); } function newToken_() { return (Utilities.getUuid() + Utilities.getUuid()).replace(/-/g, ''); } function login_(username, password) { if (!username || !password) return { ok: false, error: 'username and password required' }; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), v = usersSheet_(ss).getDataRange().getValues(), un = str(username).toLowerCase(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (str(v[i][0]).toLowerCase() !== un) continue; var hash = str(v[i][1]), salt = str(v[i][2]); if (!hash) return { ok: false, error: 'no password set for this user — ask an admin to set one' }; if (hashPw_(password, salt) !== hash) return { ok: false, error: 'wrong username or password' }; var token = newToken_(), exp = new Date(Date.now() + SESSION_TTL_DAYS * 86400000); sessionsSheet_(ss).appendRow([token, str(v[i][0]), exp.toISOString(), new Date().toISOString()]); // ISO strings, not Date objects (Date cells were crashing getValues()) return { ok: true, sid: token, user: str(v[i][0]), role: str(v[i][3]) || 'admin', flat: str(v[i][4]), expires: exp.toISOString() }; } return { ok: false, error: 'wrong username or password' }; } // returns {user, role, flat} for a valid, unexpired token; else null function validateSession_(token) { if (!token) return null; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), v = sessionsSheet_(ss).getDataRange().getValues(), now = Date.now(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (str(v[i][0]) !== token) continue; var exp = v[i][2] instanceof Date ? v[i][2].getTime() : new Date(v[i][2]).getTime(); if (isNaN(exp) || exp < now) return null; var un = str(v[i][1]).toLowerCase(), uv = usersSheet_(ss).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var j = 1; j < uv.length; j++) if (str(uv[j][0]).toLowerCase() === un) return { user: str(uv[j][0]), role: str(uv[j][3]) || 'admin', flat: str(uv[j][4]) }; // Session with no matching row in 'Auth users' (user deleted/renamed) → DENY. Fail closed. // This previously returned role 'admin', which meant deleting someone did not revoke their live // session AND silently promoted it to full access — the removal action granted access. Never // infer a role when the account is gone. Prefer the "Remove a person" menu item, which deletes // the user and their sessions together. return null; } return null; } function logout_(token) { if (!token) return false; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = sessionsSheet_(ss), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = v.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) if (str(v[i][0]) === token) { sh.deleteRow(i + 1); return true; } return false; } function setPassword_(username, pw, role, flat) { if (!username || !pw) return { ok: false, error: 'user and pw required' }; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = usersSheet_(ss), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), un = str(username).toLowerCase(); var salt = newToken_().slice(0, 16), hash = hashPw_(pw, salt); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (str(v[i][0]).toLowerCase() === un) { sh.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(hash); sh.getRange(i + 1, 3).setValue(salt); if (role) sh.getRange(i + 1, 4).setValue(role); if (flat) sh.getRange(i + 1, 5).setValue(flat); return { ok: true, user: str(v[i][0]), updated: true }; } } sh.appendRow([str(username), hash, salt, role || 'admin', flat || '']); return { ok: true, user: str(username), created: true }; } // Sign out every existing session for a user. Called after a password change so an old // password's sessions (or a stolen token) can't keep working. function revokeSessionsFor_(username) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = sessionsSheet_(ss), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var un = str(username).toLowerCase(), n = 0; for (var i = v.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) if (str(v[i][1]).toLowerCase() === un) { sh.deleteRow(i + 1); n++; } return n; } /* ---------- password admin, run from the SHEET (menu: "The Hub" → …) ------------------- * This is the ONLY way passwords get set. Why not a web page: typed here the password never * appears in a URL, browser history, or the source code — and it still works if you're locked * out of the app, because it runs as the sheet's owner. */ function onOpen() { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu('The Hub') .addItem('Set / reset a password', 'setPasswordPrompt') .addItem('Who can sign in', 'listUsersPrompt') .addSeparator() .addItem('Remove a person', 'removeUserPrompt') .addItem('Sign everyone out', 'signEveryoneOutPrompt') .addSeparator() .addItem('Backfill repair IDs', 'backfillRepairIdsPrompt') .addItem('Electricity — fix duplicate rows', 'electricityFixDuplicates') .addItem('Electricity — refresh accruals (all flats)', 'electricityRefreshAccruals') .addItem('Electricity — apply new bills (true-up)', 'electricityApplyBills') .addItem('Electricity — rebuild a flat from WeShare', 'electricityRestatePrompt') .addItem('Electricity — sync 8A recharges', 'electricitySync8A') .addItem('Electricity — restate one bill', 'electricityRestateOneBill') .addItem('Electricity — undo a bill (back to accruals)', 'electricityUndoBill') .addItem('Electricity — repair PerMonth (#DIV/0!)', 'electricityFixPerMonth') .addItem('Other revenue — build the ledger', 'otherRevenueBuild') .addItem('WeShare — pending rows: clear the split (heal)', 'wsPendingHealPrompt') .addItem('Stays — apply the agreed corrections', 'staysApplyCorrections') .addItem('Revenue — rebuild on the new rules', 'revenueRebuild') .addItem('Revenue — check the apportionment', 'apportionHealPrompt') .addSeparator() .addItem('Dry-clean — backfill missing laundry', 'dcBackfillLaundryPrompt') .addItem('Open the new month (P&L rows)', 'openNewMonthPrompt') .addItem('Recurring — seed this month', 'recurringPrompt') .addItem('Cleaning — refresh accrual', 'cleaningAccrualPrompt') .addSeparator() .addItem('Blocked days — backfill history', 'blocksBackfillPrompt') .addSeparator() .addItem('Demo mode — status', 'demoModeStatus') .addItem('Demo mode — turn ON', 'demoModeOn') .addItem('Demo mode — turn OFF', 'demoModeOff') .addSeparator() .addItem('Test Telegram (Hub bot)', 'tgHubTest') .addItem('Triggers — install Thursday digest (8pm)', 'weeklyDigestInstallPrompt') .addSeparator() .addItem('Debug access — status', 'diagAccessStatus') .addItem('Debug access — turn ON', 'diagAccessOn') .addItem('Debug access — turn OFF', 'diagAccessOff') .addToUi(); } /* ============================================================ * ELECTRICITY — restate Zayed 2000 (one-off, 2026-07-26) * * WHY. Zayed's electricity line mixed two unrelated costs and mis-spread one bill: * • the building / common-area charge (33–327 EGP) was added into EffectiveAmount alongside the * flat's own bill, so months with only a building charge read 28–51 EGP. The accrual logic then * took that as "the last actual" and collapsed. * • the Feb-2026 bill (4,632.40) was booked to February ALONE with SpanMonths=1, although by the * house rule — a bill covers the months since the prior bill — it covers Oct-2025 → Feb-2026. * February therefore carried five months of consumption and Oct–Jan read ~40 EGP. * • Mar–Jul 2026 have no bill yet and no accrual, so they were near-zero. * • One building charge (172, 2025-04-29) sat in 'Cost line items' as category "Electricity", * which the P&L never reads (electricity comes only from 'Electricity detail') — counted NOWHERE. * * Bill history, taken from the WeShare payments (the cash record), apartment meter only: * 2025-08-26 8,390.00 covers 2025-03 → 2025-08 (6) 1,398.33 / month * 2025-09-25 3,253.00 covers 2025-09 (1) 3,253.00 * 2026-02-09 4,632.40 covers 2025-10 → 2026-02 (5) 926.48 / month * then no bill → 2026-03 → 2026-07 accrue at the same month last year (1,398.33) * * SAFETY: dry-run preview first, and the existing Zayed rows are copied to a backup tab before * anything is deleted. Writes text months as text (Sheets otherwise coerces "2025-03" to a date) * and keeps PerMonth as the =D/E formula the tab uses. * ============================================================ */ /* NOTHING here is a hardcoded amount or month. Bills come from the WeShare cash record, spans come * from the house rule (a bill covers the months since the prior bill), and accruals are computed * from the flat's own actual history. Which flat a historical WeShare row belongs to is matched on * the Apartment column when set, else on a token in the description (Zayed's rows say "401"). */ /* METERS, not flats. A payment belongs to a METER; a meter may feed more than one flat and only a * share of it may be OWH's. Getting this wrong is not cosmetic: the 17 meter is one bill split 50/50 * between 17A and 17B, and WeShare records it under assorted descriptions ("17A Electricity", * "17 Electricity") with the Apartment column empty. Matching per-flat on "17a"/"17b" therefore * attributed the whole bill to 17A, missed "17 Electricity" entirely, and — because the sheet stores * the 50% figure while WeShare holds the full one — the already-applied check never matched, so the * true-up tool re-applied bills that were long since booked. * share = the portion of the whole bill THIS FLAT carries (not the meter's OWH total, and never * divided again by flats.length). The 17 meter is one bill split 50/50 between 17A and 17B, both of * which are OWH flats — each carries 0.5 and together they account for all of it. The studio meter * is shared with Sarah's home, so 8A carries 0.5 and the remainder is not an OWH cost at all. * Cross-check against the sheet: 3,465 × 0.5 = 1,732.50 over 6 months = 288.75/month, which is * exactly what 'Electricity detail' already holds for 17A. */ var ELEC_METERS = [ { name: '17 (shared 17A/17B)', tokens: ['17a', '17b', '17 '], flats: ['17A', '17B'], share: 0.5 }, { name: '28A', tokens: ['28a', '28 '], flats: ['28A'], share: 1 }, { name: 'Zayed 2000 (401)', tokens: ['401', 'zayed'], flats: ['Zayed 2000'], share: 1 }, { name: 'Studio (8A)', tokens: ['studio', '8a'], flats: ['8A'], share: 0.5 } ]; function elecMeterFor_(flat) { for (var i = 0; i < ELEC_METERS.length; i++) if (ELEC_METERS[i].flats.indexOf(flat) > -1) return ELEC_METERS[i]; return null; } /* Every electricity payment for a flat, split apartment-meter vs building/common-area. * Amounts are returned as this FLAT'S share: raw × meter.share ÷ number of flats on the meter — so * they line up with EffectiveAmount in 'Electricity detail' and with what the P&L already holds. */ /* The applied-bill stamp. Kept inside the Source text rather than a new column: the writer rebuilds the whole block by width, and adding a column there is how rows get shifted. `[ws:]` is exact, survives a rebuild, and stays readable in the sheet. Why an id at all: the previous check compared bill month + amount, and the sheet stores the METER SHARE (17A = 50%) while WeShare holds the full bill — so it never matched and bills booked months earlier were applied again. Run by hand that was one bad afternoon; on an automatic sync it would repeat every single run. */ function elecWsTag_(id) { return id ? ' [ws:' + id + ']' : ''; } /* ---- EXECUTION-SCOPED MEMOS (perf, 2026-08-02). One Sync-all had grown to read the ~5,800-row WeShare Expenses tab ~9 times (the bills plan alone once per flat) and rebuild Stays 3 times — that, not any one step, is why sync crawled. These memo the big reads for the LIFE OF ONE EXECUTION only (globals reset per invocation, so no cross-request staleness). SAFE BECAUSE OF PIPELINE ORDER: the electricity/alert steps that use these run AFTER every step that writes WeShare or Stays — first use captures the post-write state. Do not call wsEv_ from syncDryCleanWS / syncInventoryWS / syncWeShare: they read-then-append and must see their own fresh reads. */ var __wsEv = null, __staysM = null; function wsEv_() { if (!__wsEv) __wsEv = SpreadsheetApp.openById(WESHARE_ID).getSheetByName('Expenses').getDataRange().getValues(); return __wsEv; } function buildStaysM_(ss) { if (!__staysM) __staysM = buildStays(ss); return __staysM; } /* ============================================================ * 8A CARD DEPLETION (§7 ①, agreed 2026-08-01). Emad logs the RAW card balance (the full shared * card) in the dry-clean app's MeterReadings tab; recharges come from WeShare at their RAW value. * Consumption between two readings = earlier balance + recharges in between − later balance. * Burn is per CALENDAR day — Sarah's rule: the home consumes its half whether or not a guest is in, * so vacancy burns too. Averaging across whole intervals bakes that in, and projecting it over * every future day errs prudent: the alert can fire early, never late. * ============================================================ */ function elecMeter8A_() { try { var mr = SpreadsheetApp.openById(DRYCLEAN_ID).getSheetByName('MeterReadings'); if (!mr || mr.getLastRow() < 2) return null; var n = Math.min(6, mr.getLastRow() - 1); var rv = mr.getRange(mr.getLastRow() - n + 1, 1, n, 5).getValues() .map(function (r) { return { bal: Number(r[1]) || 0, kind: String(r[4] || ''), // col E: 'after' = post-insertion reading at: (r[2] instanceof Date) ? r[2] : new Date(String(r[2]).replace(' ', 'T')) }; }) // tolerate coerced Date cells .filter(function (x) { return x.at && !isNaN(x.at.getTime()); }); if (!rv.length) return null; var last = rv[rv.length - 1], now = new Date(); var rech = elecPrepaidRechargesFor_('8A'); // .raw = full card value, .date = day paid /* SAME-VISIT RULE (2026-08-07, mirrors الكنترول's computeMeterScreen_): a recharge is dated, never timed, and Emad recharges then reads in the same visit — so a recharge dated the same day as a reading is already inside that reading's balance. The old noon-guess put the 02-08 recharge (1,000) inside the next interval and showed ~528 EGP/day instead of ~44. */ var day8 = function (d) { return Utilities.formatDate(d, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); }; var rawBetween = function (a, b) { var t = 0, aD = day8(a), bD = day8(b); rech.forEach(function (r) { if (r.date > aD && r.date <= bD) t += r.raw; }); return t; }; var cons = 0, days = 0, perDay = []; /* CREDIT REACHES THE METER WHEN EMAD APPLIES IT, NOT WHEN KHALED BUYS IT (Sarah 2026-08-12). Emad tops the card up from a pre-charged card and records the amount with his reading; Khaled restocks that pre-charged card later, which is a WeShare EXPENSE on its own date. So from the cutover the burn counts top-ups only — WeShare dates say nothing about when power became available. Older intervals keep the WeShare basis (no top-ups were recorded then). This mirrors الكنترول's computeMeterScreen_ exactly; edit the two together. */ /* MEASURE ONLY WHAT CAN BE MEASURED (Sarah 2026-08-12). The card's balance does NOT move by the amount paid: the tariff is tiered and crossing a tier carries penalties, so a 1,000 recharge can show up as much less. Any interval containing a top-up is therefore uninterpretable — we cannot tell burn from fees. So those intervals are SKIPPED, and the daily burn is taken only from stretches where the balance simply fell with nothing added. That needs no assumption about price at all. Mirrored in الكنترول — edit together. */ var METER_CREDIT_CUTOVER = '2026-08-13', METER_FLOOR = 400; for (var i = 1; i < rv.length; i++) { var bDay8 = Utilities.formatDate(rv[i].at, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var isJump = (bDay8 >= METER_CREDIT_CUTOVER) ? (rv[i].kind === 'after') : (rawBetween(rv[i - 1].at, rv[i].at) > 0); if (isJump) continue; // the recharge instant — not measurable var c = rv[i - 1].bal - rv[i].bal; // pure decline var dd = (rv[i].at - rv[i - 1].at) / 86400000; if (c >= 0 && dd > 0.25) { cons += c; days += dd; perDay.push(c / dd); } // a negative interval = entry error; skip it } if (perDay.length > 3) perDay = perDay.slice(-3); // the estimate rides the last 3 clean stretches cons = 0; days = 0; // recompute the rate from those three only perDay.forEach(function (r) { cons += r; }); days = perDay.length; var rate = days > 0 ? cons / days : 0; /* IS THE EVIDENCE ANY GOOD? (Sarah 2026-08-11: "the 8A alert says it's about to deplete, which is not the case".) The projection is only as honest as the last reading: one misread digit, or a recharge paid but never logged in WeShare, makes the burn look enormous and the card look empty. So when the newest interval runs far hotter than the ones before it, the alert stops asserting a depletion date and asks for the reading to be checked instead. */ var suspect = false, prevRate = 0; if (perDay.length >= 2) { var last1 = perDay[perDay.length - 1], earlier = perDay.slice(0, -1); prevRate = earlier.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + x; }, 0) / earlier.length; if (prevRate > 0 && last1 > prevRate * 2.5) suspect = true; } var ageDays = Math.floor((now - last.at) / 86400000); /* Since the last reading, nothing has been ADDED to the meter that we know of — a WeShare purchase since then is credit sitting on the pre-charged card, not power on the meter. Adding it here is what let a projection climb while the meter was actually draining. */ var projBal = last.bal - (rate * (now - last.at) / 86400000); var depDate = (rate > 0 && projBal > 0) ? new Date(now.getTime() + (projBal / rate) * 86400000) : null; var nco = null; // next 8A check-out buildStaysM_(SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet()).forEach(function (s) { var st = (s.status || '').toLowerCase(); if (s.flat !== '8A' || s.portal === 'Blocked' || st.indexOf('cancel') > -1) return; if (s.checkOut && s.checkOut.slice(0, 10) >= Utilities.formatDate(now, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd')) if (!nco || s.checkOut < nco) nco = s.checkOut.slice(0, 10); }); var depK = depDate ? Utilities.formatDate(depDate, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd') : null; return { balance: last.bal, readAgoDays: ageDays, ratePerDay: round2(rate), lastReadAt: Utilities.formatDate(last.at, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), // show the evidence, not just the verdict readings: rv.length, intervals: perDay.length, lastIntervalRate: perDay.length ? round2(perDay[perDay.length - 1]) : 0, priorRate: round2(prevRate), suspect: suspect, projectedBalance: round2(Math.max(projBal, 0)), depletion: depK, nextCheckout: nco, /* THE FLOOR IS THE PRIMARY ALARM (Sarah 2026-08-12): below 400 EGP the card must go in, and that needs no rate at all — it cannot break when the tariff misbehaves. The projection is a secondary, clearly-estimated warning beside it. */ floor: METER_FLOOR, belowFloor: (last.bal < METER_FLOOR), alert: !!(last.bal < METER_FLOOR || (depK && rate > 0 && ((nco && depK <= nco) || (depDate - now) / 86400000 <= 3))) }; } catch (e) { return null; } } /* Typed flat name → the EXACT id the sheet uses, case-insensitive. Every prompt tool must resolve through this: "zayed 2000" met a case-sensitive === and matched nothing — in the one-bill tool that was a confusing "no bill found", but in the full rebuild an unmatched name would leave the old rows standing and APPEND the recomputed set beside them, doubling the flat. */ function elecCanonFlat_(input) { var t = str(input).trim().toLowerCase(); if (!t) return null; for (var i = 0; i < ELEC_METERS.length; i++) for (var j = 0; j < ELEC_METERS[i].flats.length; j++) if (ELEC_METERS[i].flats[j].toLowerCase() === t) return ELEC_METERS[i].flats[j]; return null; } function elecPaymentsFor_(flat) { var meter = elecMeterFor_(flat); if (!meter) return { apartment: [], building: [], factor: 1 }; var factor = meter.share; // already this flat's portion — do NOT divide again var ev = wsEv_(); // memoized — the bills plan calls this once PER FLAT var hdr = ev[0].map(function (h) { return str(h).toLowerCase(); }), iApt = hdr.indexOf('apartment'); var apt = [], bldg = []; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { var w = ev[i]; if (!w[0] || String(w[0]) === 'DELETED') continue; if (str(w[1]) !== 'Airbnb Monthly' || str(w[7]) !== 'expense') continue; var desc = str(w[3]), cat = str(w[4]), dl = desc.toLowerCase(); if (!/electric/i.test(cat) && !/electric/i.test(desc)) continue; // electricity of some kind if (/supplies|supply/i.test(dl)) continue; // "electric supplies" is maintenance // Apartment column wins when set, but it names ONE flat while a shared meter feeds several — // so accept it if it names any flat on this meter, else fall back to the meter's tokens. var rowFlat = iApt >= 0 ? str(w[iApt]) : ''; var match = rowFlat ? (meter.flats.indexOf(rowFlat) > -1) : meter.tokens.some(function (t) { return dl.indexOf(t) > -1; }); if (!match) continue; var d = parseDCymd_(w[2]); if (!d) continue; var raw = Number(w[5]) || 0; // w[0] is the WeShare row id — the only stable handle on a payment (see elecWsTag_) var rec = { id: str(w[0]), date: d, amount: round2(raw * factor), raw: raw, desc: desc, cat: cat }; if (/building|common/i.test(dl)) bldg.push(rec); else apt.push(rec); // ← the building split } apt.sort(function (a, b) { return a.date < b.date ? -1 : 1; }); bldg.sort(function (a, b) { return a.date < b.date ? -1 : 1; }); return { apartment: apt, building: bldg, factor: factor }; } /* Turn apartment payments into covered spans using the house rule. * firstFrom = the earliest month to cover (taken from the tab, not invented). */ function elecBillSpans_(payments, firstFrom) { var out = [], prevBillMonth = null; payments.forEach(function (p) { var bm = p.date.slice(0, 7); var from = prevBillMonth ? monthAdd_(prevBillMonth, 1) : (firstFrom || bm); if (from > bm) from = bm; // two bills in one month → single month out.push({ id: p.id, from: from, to: bm, billMonth: bm, amount: p.amount, span: monthsBetween_(from, bm), paid: p.date }); prevBillMonth = bm; }); return out; } /* PREPAID meters are NOT accrued. 8A is a prepaid card shared with the (non-Airbnb) home and its * recharges are logged in WeShare under the **Monthly Expense** group as "studio electricity", at a * 50% share — so there is no late bill to accrue against, and its recharge timing is not * consumption. Real consumption for 8A needs the meter-balance screen in the dry-clean app. */ var ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS = { '8A': true }; /* THE ACCRUAL RULE: the HIGHER of * (a) the same calendar month last year — carries whatever seasonal shape the history has * (b) the most recent actual month — carries today's price and usage level * falling back to the mean of the last 3 actuals, then the last actual, when (a) doesn't exist. * * WHY THE HIGHER OF THE TWO, rather than a drift ratio. Drift (recent actuals ÷ the same months a * year earlier) is the textbook way to strip last year's tariff out of the estimate, and it fixed * 17A/17B exactly (288.75 → 512.53, matching their actual level). But it CANNOT work on this history: * bills spanning 4–6 months were spread evenly, so those months are a flat line with no seasonal * shape. Dividing by a flattened base gave 28A a drift of 0.72 and an estimate of 987 for July, when * June's real bill was 1,460 — worse than doing nothing. And unclamped it produced 3,000 for 8A. * * Taking the higher of the two is deliberately PRUDENT: an accrual exists so a month can close, and * under-accruing hides cost until the bill lands and then dumps it on one month. It is a bias, and it * is the bias worth having here. * 17A/17B max(288.75, 512.50) = 512.50 * 28A max(1375.50, 1460) = 1460 * Zayed max(1398.33, 926.48) = 1398.33 (926.48 is a stale winter figure) * * Revisit once there are real month-by-month bills for two summers — then drift becomes meaningful * and this can become seasonal properly. A judged override ("manual" in column H) always wins. */ function elecAccrualFor_(actuals, month) { if (!actuals.length) return null; var byMonth = {}; actuals.forEach(function (a) { byMonth[a.covered] = (byMonth[a.covered] || 0) + a.per; }); var months = Object.keys(byMonth).filter(function (m) { return m < month; }).sort(); if (!months.length) return null; var lastYear = monthAdd_(month, -12), lastActualMonth = months[months.length - 1]; var lyVal = byMonth[lastYear], recentVal = byMonth[lastActualMonth]; if (lyVal != null) { if (recentVal >= lyVal) return { amount: round2(recentVal), basis: 'most recent actual (' + lastActualMonth + ' = ' + round2(recentVal) + '), higher than the same month last year (' + lastYear + ' = ' + round2(lyVal) + ')' }; return { amount: round2(lyVal), basis: 'same month last year (' + lastYear + ' = ' + round2(lyVal) + '), higher than the most recent actual (' + lastActualMonth + ' = ' + round2(recentVal) + ')' }; } var take = months.slice(-3), sum = 0; take.forEach(function (m) { sum += byMonth[m]; }); if (take.length === 3) return { amount: round2(sum / 3), basis: 'mean of the last 3 actual months (' + take.join(', ') + ') — no same month last year' }; return { amount: round2(recentVal), basis: 'last actual month (' + lastActualMonth + ') — no same month last year' }; } function monthsBetween_(a, b) { // inclusive count of 'yyyy-MM' months var ay = +a.slice(0, 4), am = +a.slice(5, 7), by = +b.slice(0, 4), bm = +b.slice(5, 7); return (by - ay) * 12 + (bm - am) + 1; } function monthAdd_(m, n) { var y = +m.slice(0, 4), mo = +m.slice(5, 7) - 1 + n; y += Math.floor(mo / 12); mo = ((mo % 12) + 12) % 12; return y + '-' + ('0' + (mo + 1)).slice(-2); } /* Builds the Electricity detail rows for a flat: actual rows from the bills, then accrual rows for * every month from the last covered month up to `throughMonth`. Pure — computes, never writes. */ function elecDesiredRows_(flat, spans, throughMonth, share) { var rows = [], actuals = []; share = share || 1; spans.forEach(function (b) { var eff = round2(b.amount * share); for (var i = 0; i < b.span; i++) { var cm = monthAdd_(b.from, i), per = round2(eff / b.span); rows.push({ covered: cm, billMonth: b.billMonth, amount: eff, span: b.span, type: 'actual', source: eff.toFixed(1) + ' EGP x' + share.toFixed(1) + ' (' + flat + ' Electricity — apartment meter; ' + 'bill paid ' + b.paid + ', covers ' + b.from + '..' + b.to + ' (' + b.span + (b.span === 1 ? ' month' : ' months') + '); building/common-area charge excluded)' + elecWsTag_(b.id) }); actuals.push({ covered: cm, per: per }); } }); var lastCovered = spans.length ? spans[spans.length - 1].to : null; if (lastCovered && throughMonth > lastCovered) { for (var m = monthAdd_(lastCovered, 1); m <= throughMonth; m = monthAdd_(m, 1)) { var acc = elecAccrualFor_(actuals, m); if (!acc) continue; rows.push({ covered: m, billMonth: '', amount: acc.amount, span: 1, type: 'accrual', source: 'Accrual - ' + acc.amount.toFixed(2) + ' EGP (' + flat + ' Electricity — no bill yet; ' + 'estimate = ' + acc.basis + '; the difference goes to the month the bill arrives)' }); } } return rows; } /* Is this Electricity detail row an accrual? Col H says so for rows this tool wrote; older rows * only say it in the Source text, so honour both. */ function elecIsAccrual_(rowVals) { return str(rowVals[7]).toLowerCase() === 'accrual' || /^\s*accrual\b/i.test(str(rowVals[6])); } /* ---------- THE ONE WRITER FOR 'Electricity detail' ---------- * Every electricity tool computes the desired FINAL set of rows and hands it here. Nothing calls * deleteRow: on this tab deleteRow reports success and changes nothing (three separate * implementations, zero rows removed), while setValues has always worked. So the whole data block * is rewritten in a single setValues and padded with blanks — the end state is computed rather than * patched, which also makes every tool safe to re-run. * * Months are written as TEXT. The workbook convention is text months and the Monthly P&L stores * them as text; if these become real dates the P&L's SUMIFS only still matches by Sheets coercing * "2026-07" into a date, which is luck rather than design. Formatting the columns and flushing * BEFORE the write is what makes the text stick. */ function elecWriteAll_(ed, keep, cols, oldLastRow) { var block = []; keep.forEach(function (vals, k) { var rw = k + 2, copy = vals.slice(0, cols); while (copy.length < cols) copy.push(''); copy[1] = monthKey(vals[1]) || vals[1]; // normalise to yyyy-MM copy[2] = vals[2] ? (monthKey(vals[2]) || vals[2]) : ''; // PerMonth, for its NEW row. Guarded: a blank or zero span used to make this #DIV/0!, and a // single error cell here poisons the whole P&L row through SUMIFS — Costs, Net and Sarah's // share all became #DIV/0! (8A 2025-03 and 2025-05 sat like that). A row covers one month, so // a missing span means one, not divide-by-nothing. copy[5] = '=IF(N(E' + rw + ')>0,D' + rw + '/E' + rw + ',D' + rw + ')'; block.push(copy); }); var blank = []; for (var i = 0; i < cols; i++) blank.push(''); while (block.length < oldLastRow - 1) block.push(blank.slice()); // wipe whatever was below ed.getRange(2, 2, block.length, 2).setNumberFormat('@'); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); // commit the text format first ed.getRange(2, 1, block.length, cols).setValues(block); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return keep.length; } /* Build one Electricity detail row. Column F is left blank — elecWriteAll_ puts the formula in. */ function elecRowArr_(cols, flat, covered, billMonth, amount, span, source, type) { var a = []; for (var i = 0; i < cols; i++) a.push(''); a[0] = flat; a[1] = covered; a[2] = billMonth || ''; a[3] = amount; a[4] = span; a[6] = source; a[7] = type; return a; } /* ============================================================ * REPAIR: PerMonth = #DIV/0! (Sarah 2026-08-06) * * Found while reconciling revenue: 8A 2025-03 and 2025-05 carried SpanMonths = 0, so PerMonth * (=D/E) was #DIV/0!. That error does not stay in this tab — the P&L's electricity SUMIFS pulls it * straight through, so Costs total, Costs (USD), Net (USD) and Sarah's share all read #DIV/0! for * those months. Two bad cells made two closed months unreadable everywhere costs are shown. * * WHAT IT DOES, and deliberately no more: * • a row's span of 0 / blank / non-numeric becomes 1 — the row covers exactly one month * (column B), and every sibling row on this tab spreads its full amount over one month * • rewrites the PerMonth formula on every data row with the guarded form, so a future zero * degrades to "the whole amount in this month" instead of poisoning the P&L * * It does NOT re-derive the amounts. If a span of 0 was hiding a bill that really covers several * months, healing it to 1 recognises the whole amount in one month — the dry run shows exactly * which flat and month move, so that judgement stays with Sarah rather than with this function. * ============================================================ */ function elecPerMonthPlan_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); if (!ed) throw new Error('no Electricity detail tab'); var lastR = ed.getLastRow(), cols = ed.getLastColumn(); if (lastR < 2) return { rows: 0, fixes: [], ed: ed, cols: cols, lastR: lastR }; var v = ed.getRange(2, 1, lastR - 1, Math.min(cols, 8)).getValues(); var fixes = []; for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { var flat = str(v[i][0]); if (!flat) continue; var span = v[i][4], n = (span === '' || span === null) ? 0 : Number(span); if (n > 0 && isFinite(n)) continue; // healthy fixes.push({ row: i + 2, flat: flat, covered: str(v[i][1]) || monthKey(v[i][1]), billMonth: str(v[i][2]) || monthKey(v[i][2]), amount: Number(v[i][3]) || 0, was: (span === '' ? '(blank)' : String(span)), type: str(v[i][7]) }); } return { rows: v.length, fixes: fixes, ed: ed, cols: cols, lastR: lastR }; } function electricityFixPerMonth() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var P = elecPerMonthPlan_(); if (!P.fixes.length) { ui.alert('Electricity — PerMonth\n\nNothing to repair. Every one of the ' + P.rows + ' rows has a span of 1 or more.\n\nRe-applying the guarded formula anyway so a ' + 'future zero cannot reach the P&L.'); elecPerMonthApply_(P, false); return; } var lines = P.fixes.map(function (f) { return ' ' + f.flat + ' ' + f.covered + ' span ' + f.was + ' → 1 ' + 'PerMonth #DIV/0! → ' + Math.round(f.amount).toLocaleString() + ' EGP' + (f.billMonth ? ' (bill ' + f.billMonth + ')' : ''); }); var ok = ui.alert('Electricity — repair PerMonth', P.fixes.length + ' row(s) divide by a zero span, so PerMonth is #DIV/0! — and the P&L reads ' + 'that error straight through into Costs and Net for those months.\n\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n\nEach of these becomes a cost in a CLOSED month. Today that month shows an error rather ' + 'than a number, so nothing is being silently restated — a broken cell becomes a real one.\n\n' + 'The PerMonth formula on all ' + P.rows + ' rows is also rewritten so a zero span can never ' + 'reach the P&L again.\n\nApply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var r = elecPerMonthApply_(P, true); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\nspans healed: ' + r.healed + '\nPerMonth formulas rewritten: ' + r.formulas + '\n\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n\nCheck the Monthly P&L — those rows should now show numbers where they showed #DIV/0!.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('PerMonth repair failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* Write half. Kept separate so the plan can be shown before anything moves. */ function elecPerMonthApply_(P, healSpans) { var healed = 0; if (healSpans && P.fixes.length) { P.fixes.forEach(function (f) { P.ed.getRange(f.row, 5).setValue(1); healed++; }); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } var n = P.lastR - 1, fml = []; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var rw = i + 2; fml.push(['=IF(N(E' + rw + ')>0,D' + rw + '/E' + rw + ',D' + rw + ')']); } if (n > 0) P.ed.getRange(2, 6, n, 1).setFormulas(fml); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); hubBump_(['base']); return { healed: healed, formulas: n }; } /* ============================================================ * ONGOING: keep accruals current, for every flat. Safe to re-run — and it must be, because it is * meant to run every month. * * WHAT IT WILL AND WON'T TOUCH: * • It never modifies a month marked `final` in the Status tab. Closed months stay closed — that * is the whole reason a late bill is settled as a difference in the month it arrives rather * than by restating history. * • It rewrites only OPEN accrual months, so a changed estimate is picked up. * • It removes an accrual once a real bill covers that month, which is what prevents the actual * and the accrual both counting. * * NOTE: with months closing as promptly as they do here (everything through 2026-06 is final), an * accrual that is wrong is not corrected in place — the error lands in the bill month instead. That * is why the estimate basis matters. * ============================================================ */ /* PLAN half — no UI, no writes; shared by the menu tool and the sync pipeline. */ function elecRefreshAccrualsPlan_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); if (!ed) return { ok: false, error: 'No "Electricity detail" tab.' }; // month status var stat = {}, sv = (ss.getSheetByName(TAB.status) || { getDataRange: function () { return { getValues: function () { return []; } }; } }).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var s = 1; s < sv.length; s++) { var m = monthKey(sv[s][0]); if (m) stat[m] = str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase(); } var isFinal = function (m) { return stat[m] === 'final'; }; var now = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); var oldLastRow = ed.getLastRow(), cols = Math.max(9, ed.getLastColumn()); var ev = ed.getRange(1, 1, oldLastRow, cols).getValues(); var flats = {}, plan = [], removals = [], dropRows = {}; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { var f = str(ev[i][0]), cm = monthKey(ev[i][1]); if (!f || !cm) continue; if (!flats[f]) flats[f] = { actual: [], accrual: [] }; var per = (Number(ev[i][4]) ? Number(ev[i][3]) / Number(ev[i][4]) : Number(ev[i][5])) || 0; if (elecIsAccrual_(ev[i])) flats[f].accrual.push({ row: i + 1, covered: cm, per: per, manual: /manual/i.test(str(ev[i][7])) }); else flats[f].actual.push({ row: i + 1, covered: cm, per: per }); } Object.keys(flats).forEach(function (f) { if (ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS[f]) return; // prepaid card — nothing to accrue (see ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS) var A = flats[f]; var lastActual = A.actual.reduce(function (mx, a) { return (!mx || a.covered > mx) ? a.covered : mx; }, null); var actualMonths = {}; A.actual.forEach(function (a) { actualMonths[a.covered] = true; }); // 1. an accrual for a month a bill now covers, or in an OPEN month (about to be rebuilt) → drop. // A MANUAL override is never overwritten: no formula fits this data well (bills are irregular // and spread, and the tariff has moved), so a judged figure must be able to stand. Put the // word "manual" in column H — e.g. "accrual (manual)" — and this leaves the row alone. A.accrual.forEach(function (a) { if (isFinal(a.covered)) return; // never touch a closed month if (a.manual) return; // never overwrite a judged figure dropRows[a.row] = true; // rebuilt below, not deleted if (actualMonths[a.covered] || a.covered <= (lastActual || '')) removals.push({ row: a.row, flat: f, covered: a.covered, why: 'settled — a bill now covers it' }); else removals.push({ row: a.row, flat: f, covered: a.covered, why: 'stale estimate — rebuilt below' }); }); var manualMonths = {}; A.accrual.forEach(function (a) { if (a.manual) manualMonths[a.covered] = true; }); // 2. every OPEN month after the last actual, up to this month, needs an accrual if (!lastActual) return; for (var m = monthAdd_(lastActual, 1); m <= now; m = monthAdd_(m, 1)) { if (isFinal(m) || actualMonths[m] || manualMonths[m]) continue; var acc = elecAccrualFor_(A.actual, m); if (!acc) continue; plan.push({ flat: f, covered: m, amount: acc.amount, basis: acc.basis }); } }); return { ok: true, ed: ed, cols: cols, oldLastRow: oldLastRow, ev: ev, plan: plan, removals: removals, dropRows: dropRows }; } /* The sheet-menu tool — dry-run preview, then the shared write. */ function electricityRefreshAccruals() { hubBump_(['base']); // menu tools write via script — onEdit never fires for them var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); var P = elecRefreshAccrualsPlan_(); if (!P.ok) { ui.alert(P.error); return; } if (!P.plan.length && !P.removals.length) { ui.alert('Nothing to do ✓\n\nEvery flat already has a current accrual, and no closed month is affected.'); return; } var addLines = P.plan.map(function (p) { return ' ' + p.flat + ' ' + p.covered + ' ' + p.amount + ' (' + p.basis + ')'; }).join('\n'); var remLines = P.removals.map(function (r) { return ' ' + r.flat + ' ' + r.covered + ' — ' + r.why; }).join('\n'); if (ui.alert('Refresh electricity accruals', 'DRY RUN — nothing changed yet.\n\nADD / REBUILD (open months only)\n' + (addLines || ' none') + '\n\nREMOVE\n' + (remLines || ' none') + '\n\nClosed (final) months are never touched.\n\nApply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var W = elecRefreshAccrualsWrite_(P); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\nWrote ' + P.plan.length + ' accrual row(s), removed ' + W.removed + '.\n' + 'Closed months untouched.\n\n' + (W.dups.length ? '⚠️ MORE THAN ONE ROW for: ' + W.dups.join(', ') + '\n\nRun "Electricity — fix duplicate rows" before trusting the P&L.' : 'Checked: exactly one row per flat/month ✓')); } /* WRITE half. */ function elecRefreshAccrualsWrite_(P) { var ed = P.ed, cols = P.cols, oldLastRow = P.oldLastRow, ev = P.ev, plan = P.plan, dropRows = P.dropRows, removals = P.removals; /* Rebuild rather than delete — see elecWriteAll_. Keep every row except the accruals being replaced, append the new ones, write the block once. */ var keep = []; for (var kr = 1; kr < ev.length; kr++) { if (dropRows[kr + 1]) continue; // superseded accrual if (!str(ev[kr][0]) && !monthKey(ev[kr][1])) continue; // blank filler row keep.push(ev[kr]); } plan.forEach(function (p) { keep.push(elecRowArr_(cols, p.flat, p.covered, '', p.amount, 1, 'Accrual - ' + p.amount.toFixed(2) + ' EGP (' + p.flat + ' Electricity — no bill yet; estimate = ' + p.basis + '; the difference goes to the month the bill arrives)', 'accrual')); }); var removedCount = removals.length; elecWriteAll_(ed, keep, cols, oldLastRow); if (!str(ed.getRange(1, 8).getValue())) ed.getRange(1, 8).setValue('Type'); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); /* VERIFY: exactly one row per flat+month for everything we just touched. The P&L SUMIFS adds every matching row, so a duplicate silently multiplies a month's cost — which is precisely what went unnoticed before. Better to be told now than to find it in a closed month. */ var check = ed.getDataRange().getValues(), seen = {}, dups = []; for (var c2 = 1; c2 < check.length; c2++) { var cf = str(check[c2][0]), cm2 = monthKey(check[c2][1]); if (!cf || !cm2) continue; var key2 = cf + '|' + cm2; seen[key2] = (seen[key2] || 0) + 1; if (seen[key2] === 2) dups.push(key2); } return { added: plan.length, removed: removedCount, dups: dups }; } /* ============================================================ * APPLY NEW ELECTRICITY BILLS (the true-up) * * Deliberately a MENU action with a dry run, not part of the hourly sync. A true-up moves real money * between months in a P&L Sarah closes herself; bills arrive a few times a year, so there is nothing * to gain from doing it unattended and a lot to lose from doing it silently. * * THE ARITHMETIC. A bill of B covers M1..Mn (n = months since the prior bill), so the true monthly * cost is B/n. For each covered month: * OPEN → write the actual (B/n). Any accrual there is removed — that is the settlement. * FINAL → left completely alone. Whatever it recorded (its accrual) stands. * The bill must still be fully recognised, so the shortfall on the closed months becomes one row in * the month the bill arrived: * * true-up = closed_count × (B/n) − Σ(accruals already recorded in those closed months) * * which is exactly the error in the closed months' estimates. Positive = they under-accrued (extra * cost now); negative = they over-accrued (a credit now). Derivation: total to recognise is B, of * which open months take open_count×B/n and closed months already took Σaccruals; the remainder is * n×B/n − open_count×B/n − Σaccruals = closed_count×B/n − Σaccruals. * * Skips any bill already applied (matched on ContributingBillMonth + EffectiveAmount), so re-running * is safe — the lesson from double-writing the building rows. * ============================================================ */ /* ============================================================ * ELECTRICITY ON SYNC (§5.2a/c/d) — the whole point: "Sync all" is the ONLY click. * Order matters: bills first (they settle accruals and write actuals), then the accrual refresher * re-plans from the fresh sheet so new actuals seed the estimates. * A bill the magnitude rule defers is NOT applied — it is stored for Today's "Needs you", where * Sarah decides: absorb (run the true-up menu tool) or restate (run the rebuild). Every sync * recomputes and restores the list, so acting on a bill clears its alert on the next sync. * ============================================================ */ /* ============================================================ * PREPAID RECHARGES → 'Electricity detail' (8A ①, §7 workflow). * The prepaid meter has no bills: money is recharges in WeShare's **Monthly Expense** group * ("Studio Electricity"), recognised in the month PAID at the meter share, type `actual` — no * accrual, no true-up (see ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS).
