Until now those rows were mirrored by hand, and * hand-mirroring drifted a month without changing the total: Feb-2026 held 500 with no recharge * behind it while July held 500 against two paid recharges (5 + 24 Jul = 1,000 at 50%). * * Derived state, not appends: each OPEN month's row is recomputed as (sum of that month's * recharges × share) with every [ws:id] in Source — so re-running is a no-op, a second recharge in * the same month updates the row, and a stale mirror row with no recharge behind it is removed. * CLOSED months are never written; a closed month whose booked figure disagrees with the recharges * is REPORTED (Needs-you) exactly like the bill decisions. `manual` rows are never touched. */ function elecPrepaidRechargesFor_(flat) { var meter = elecMeterFor_(flat); if (!meter) return []; var ev = wsEv_(); // memoized (see wsEv_) var recs = []; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { var w = ev[i]; if (!w[0] || String(w[0]) === 'DELETED') continue; if (str(w[1]) !== 'Monthly Expense' || str(w[7]) !== 'expense') continue; var desc = str(w[3]), dl = desc.toLowerCase(); if (!/electric/i.test(desc + ' ' + str(w[4]))) continue; if (/wifi|landline|internet|supplies|supply/i.test(dl)) continue; if (!meter.tokens.some(function (t) { return dl.indexOf(t) > -1; })) continue; // home's own bill has no token if (str(w[15]).toLowerCase() === 'pending') continue; var d = parseDCymd_(w[2]); if (!d) continue; recs.push({ id: str(w[0]), date: d, month: d.slice(0, 7), raw: Number(w[5]) || 0, amount: round2((Number(w[5]) || 0) * meter.share) }); } recs.sort(function (a, b) { return a.date < b.date ? -1 : 1; }); return recs; } function elecPrepaidSyncStep_() { var out = { written: 0, removed: 0, unchanged: 0, mismatches: [] }; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); if (!ed) { out.error = 'no Electricity detail tab'; return out; } var stat = {}, sv = (ss.getSheetByName(TAB.status) || { getDataRange: function () { return { getValues: function () { return []; } }; } }).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var s = 1; s < sv.length; s++) { var mk = monthKey(sv[s][0]); if (mk) stat[mk] = str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase(); } var isFinal = function (m) { return stat[m] === 'final'; }; /* THE FLOOR: only months the P&L actually tracks (the Month-status window). WeShare's recharge history reaches back to 2019, and the first run walked all of it — rewrote years of snapshot-era rows and raised a mismatch for every old month whose hand-mirror disagreed. Ancient months are nobody's decision; before the status window this step neither writes nor reports. Derived from the data, not hardcoded. */ var statFloor = null; Object.keys(stat).forEach(function (m) { if (!statFloor || m < statFloor) statFloor = m; }); var oldLastRow = ed.getLastRow(), cols = Math.max(9, ed.getLastColumn()); var ev = ed.getRange(1, 1, oldLastRow, cols).getValues(); var dropRows = {}, appendRows = []; Object.keys(ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS).forEach(function (flat) { var recs = elecPrepaidRechargesFor_(flat); if (!recs.length) return; var window0 = recs[0].month; // before WeShare history, booked rows are the snapshot — not comparable if (statFloor && statFloor > window0) window0 = statFloor; var byMonth = {}; recs.forEach(function (r) { (byMonth[r.month] = byMonth[r.month] || []).push(r); }); var booked = {}; // month → { rows:[rowNo], eff, manual, tags:{} } for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { if (str(ev[i][0]) !== flat) continue; var cm = monthKey(ev[i][1]); if (!cm) continue; var B = booked[cm] = booked[cm] || { rows: [], eff: 0, manual: false, tags: {} }; B.rows.push(i + 1); B.eff += (Number(ev[i][4]) ? Number(ev[i][3]) / Number(ev[i][4]) : Number(ev[i][5])) || 0; if (/manual/i.test(str(ev[i][7]))) B.manual = true; var tg = str(ev[i][6]).match(/\[ws:[^\]]+\]/g); if (tg) tg.forEach(function (t) { B.tags[t.slice(4, -1)] = true; }); } // every month in the window: those with recharges, plus booked months with none var months = {}; Object.keys(byMonth).forEach(function (m) { if (m >= window0) months[m] = true; }); Object.keys(booked).forEach(function (m) { if (m >= window0) months[m] = true; }); Object.keys(months).sort().forEach(function (m) { var rs = byMonth[m] || [], B = booked[m]; var eff = round2(rs.reduce(function (a, r) { return a + r.amount; }, 0)); if (B && B.manual) return; // a judged figure stands if (isFinal(m)) { // report, never write if (round2((B ? B.eff : 0)) !== eff) out.mismatches.push({ flat: flat, month: m, booked: round2(B ? B.eff : 0), derived: eff, payments: rs.map(function (r) { return r.date + ' ' + r.raw; }) }); return; } var ok = B && round2(B.eff) === eff && rs.every(function (r) { return B.tags[r.id]; }); if (ok) { out.unchanged++; return; } // idempotent: nothing to do if (B) B.rows.forEach(function (rn) { dropRows[rn] = true; }); if (eff > 0) { appendRows.push(elecRowArr_(cols, flat, m, m, eff, 1, eff.toFixed(1) + ' EGP (' + flat + ' prepaid — recharge' + (rs.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' ' + rs.map(function (r) { return r.date + ' ' + r.raw; }).join(', ') + ' at x' + (elecMeterFor_(flat).share) + ', recognised in the month paid)' + rs.map(function (r) { return elecWsTag_(r.id); }).join(''), 'actual')); out.written++; } else if (B) out.removed++; // mirror row with no recharge behind it }); }); if (Object.keys(dropRows).length || appendRows.length) { var keep = []; for (var kr = 1; kr < ev.length; kr++) { if (dropRows[kr + 1]) continue; if (!str(ev[kr][0]) && !monthKey(ev[kr][1])) continue; keep.push(ev[kr]); } appendRows.forEach(function (r) { keep.push(r); }); elecWriteAll_(ed, keep, cols, oldLastRow); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } return out; } /* ============================================================ * RESTATE ONE BILL — the narrow restate: spread ONE unapplied bill over exactly the months it * covers, and touch nothing else. Exists because the full rebuild's blast radius grows with the * history — Sarah: "i don't want to restate everything if i have just one deviation". * • only the covered span is written; older bills, other months, building rows: untouched * • no true-up row — spreading over the span IS the alternative to the lump * • closed months whose figure changes get Type `restated` + the old figure kept in Source * • `manual` rows inside the span are left standing and reported, never overwritten * • rows carry [ws:id], so the next sync sees the bill settled and the alert clears itself * ============================================================ */ function electricityRestateOneBill() { hubBump_(['base']); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var P = elecApplyBillsPlan_(); if (!P.ok) { ui.alert(P.error); return; } if (!P.actions.length) { ui.alert('No unapplied bill found ✓\n\nEvery electricity bill in WeShare is already reflected in Electricity detail.'); return; } var q = ui.prompt('Restate one bill', 'Unapplied bills:\n' + P.actions.map(function (a) { return ' ' + a.flat + ' — ' + a.bill.amount + ' EGP paid ' + a.bill.paid + ', covers ' + a.bill.from + '..' + a.bill.to; }).join('\n') + '\n\nWhich flat?', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL); if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var flat = elecCanonFlat_(q.getResponseText()); if (!flat) { ui.alert('"' + str(q.getResponseText()).trim() + '" is not a known flat. Use 8A, 17A, 17B, 28A or Zayed 2000.'); return; } var acts = P.actions.filter(function (a) { return a.flat === flat; }); if (!acts.length) { ui.alert('No unapplied bill for ' + flat + '. Nothing changed.'); return; } acts.sort(function (x, y) { return x.bill.billMonth < y.bill.billMonth ? 1 : -1; }); var a = acts[0]; // the latest — normally the only one var per = a.per, stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var stat = {}, sv = (ss.getSheetByName(TAB.status) || { getDataRange: function () { return { getValues: function () { return []; } }; } }).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var s = 1; s < sv.length; s++) { var mk = monthKey(sv[s][0]); if (mk) stat[mk] = str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase(); } // the span's months, what each holds today, and what it becomes var span = [], m; for (var k = 0; k < a.bill.span; k++) { m = monthAdd_(a.bill.from, k); span.push(m); } var rowsByMonth = {}, manualMonths = []; P.flats[flat].rows.forEach(function (r) { if (span.indexOf(r.covered) < 0) return; if (r.manual) { if (manualMonths.indexOf(r.covered) < 0) manualMonths.push(r.covered); return; } (rowsByMonth[r.covered] = rowsByMonth[r.covered] || []).push(r); }); var lines = span.map(function (mm) { if (manualMonths.indexOf(mm) > -1) return ' ' + mm + ' MANUAL — left alone'; var old = (rowsByMonth[mm] || []).reduce(function (t, r) { return t + r.per; }, 0); var closed = stat[mm] === 'final'; return ' ' + mm + ' ' + round2(old) + ' → ' + per + (closed && round2(old) !== per ? ' (closed — will be marked restated)' : ''); }).join('\n'); if (ui.alert('Restate ' + flat + ' — bill ' + a.bill.billMonth, 'DRY RUN — nothing changed yet.\n\n' + a.bill.amount + ' EGP over ' + a.bill.span + ' month(s) = ' + per + '/mo\n\n' + lines + '\n\nOnly these months are touched. No true-up row is written.\n\nApply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var dropSet = {}; Object.keys(rowsByMonth).forEach(function (mm) { rowsByMonth[mm].forEach(function (r) { dropSet[r.row] = true; }); }); var keep = [], restated = []; for (var kr = 1; kr < P.ev.length; kr++) { if (dropSet[kr + 1]) continue; if (!str(P.ev[kr][0]) && !monthKey(P.ev[kr][1])) continue; keep.push(P.ev[kr]); } span.forEach(function (mm) { if (manualMonths.indexOf(mm) > -1) return; var old = round2((rowsByMonth[mm] || []).reduce(function (t, r) { return t + r.per; }, 0)); var isRestate = (stat[mm] === 'final') && old !== per; var src = a.bill.amount.toFixed(2) + ' EGP (' + flat + ' Electricity — apartment meter; bill paid ' + a.bill.paid + ', covers ' + a.bill.from + '..' + a.bill.to + ' (' + a.bill.span + '); restated one bill)' + (isRestate ? ' [restated ' + stamp + ', was ' + old.toFixed(2) + ']' : '') + elecWsTag_(a.bill.id); keep.push(elecRowArr_(P.cols, flat, mm, a.bill.billMonth, a.bill.amount, a.bill.span, src, isRestate ? 'restated' : 'actual')); if (isRestate) restated.push(mm + ' (' + old.toFixed(2) + ' → ' + per + ')'); }); elecWriteAll_(P.ed, keep, P.cols, P.oldLastRow); if (!str(P.ed.getRange(1, 8).getValue())) P.ed.getRange(1, 8).setValue('Type'); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); // the standing guarantee: one row per flat/month across everything just touched var chk = P.ed.getDataRange().getValues(), seen = {}, dups = []; for (var z = 1; z < chk.length; z++) { var zf = str(chk[z][0]), zm = monthKey(chk[z][1]); if (!zf || !zm) continue; var zk = zf + '|' + zm; seen[zk] = (seen[zk] || 0) + 1; if (seen[zk] === 2) dups.push(zk); } ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + flat + ' bill ' + a.bill.billMonth + ' spread over ' + a.bill.span + ' month(s) at ' + per + '/mo.' + (restated.length ? '\n\n🔁 RESTATED closed months:\n ' + restated.join('\n ') : '') + (manualMonths.length ? '\n\n✋ left alone (manual): ' + manualMonths.join(', ') : '') + '\n\n' + (dups.length ? '⚠️ more than one row for: ' + dups.join(', ') : 'Verified: exactly one row per flat/month ✓') + '\n\nThe next Sync all sees this bill settled — its alert clears itself.'); } /* ============================================================ * UNDO A BILL — put a flat's LATEST applied bill back to the un-applied state. * Two uses: (1) reversing an absorb decision — the sync applied a bill (or you ran the true-up) * and you'd rather restate via the rebuild instead; (2) testing the decision alert end-to-end. * What it does: removes the rows the latest bill wrote (actual + true-up, matched on the * ContributingBillMonth), then refreshes accruals so the reopened months are estimated again. * Closed months are untouched as always — their accrual rows never left. The next Sync-all * re-plans the bill from WeShare and, if it is big, raises the decision in Needs-you. * Refuses if the bill was applied via a REBUILD (restated rows) — undo that by rebuilding again. */ function electricityUndoBill() { hubBump_(['base']); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var q = ui.prompt('Undo a bill', 'Which flat? (17A · 17B · 28A · Zayed 2000)\n\nIts LATEST applied bill goes back to un-applied: the rows it wrote are removed, accruals are re-estimated, and the next Sync-all raises it again — as a decision if it is big.', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL); if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var flat = elecCanonFlat_(q.getResponseText()); if (!flat) { ui.alert('"' + str(q.getResponseText()).trim() + '" is not a known flat. Use 8A, 17A, 17B, 28A or Zayed 2000.'); return; } if (ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS[flat]) { ui.alert(flat + ' is prepaid — it has no bills. Nothing changed.'); return; } var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); var oldLastRow = ed.getLastRow(), cols = Math.max(9, ed.getLastColumn()); var ev = ed.getRange(1, 1, oldLastRow, cols).getValues(); var latest = null; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { if (str(ev[i][0]) !== flat || elecIsAccrual_(ev[i])) continue; var bm = monthKey(ev[i][2]); if (bm && (!latest || bm > latest)) latest = bm; } if (!latest) { ui.alert('No applied bill found for ' + flat + '.'); return; } var drop = [], hasRestated = false; for (var j = 1; j < ev.length; j++) { if (str(ev[j][0]) !== flat || monthKey(ev[j][2]) !== latest || elecIsAccrual_(ev[j])) continue; if (/restated/i.test(str(ev[j][7]))) hasRestated = true; drop.push({ row: j + 1, covered: monthKey(ev[j][1]), type: str(ev[j][7]) }); } if (hasRestated) { ui.alert('Bill ' + latest + ' was applied via a REBUILD (restated rows).\n\nUndoing a restatement is done by rebuilding again — run "Electricity — rebuild a flat from WeShare". Nothing changed.'); return; } if (ui.alert('Undo ' + flat + ' bill ' + latest, 'Removes ' + drop.length + ' row(s):\n' + drop.map(function (d) { return ' ' + d.covered + ' (' + (d.type || 'actual') + ')'; }).join('\n') + '\n\nThen accruals are re-estimated for the reopened months. Proceed?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var dropSet = {}; drop.forEach(function (d) { dropSet[d.row] = true; }); var keep = []; for (var kr = 1; kr < ev.length; kr++) { if (dropSet[kr + 1]) continue; if (!str(ev[kr][0]) && !monthKey(ev[kr][1])) continue; keep.push(ev[kr]); } elecWriteAll_(ed, keep, cols, oldLastRow); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); var R = elecRefreshAccrualsPlan_(); // refill the reopened months with estimates var added = 0; if (R.ok && (R.plan.length || R.removals.length)) { added = elecRefreshAccrualsWrite_(R).added; } ui.alert('Done ✓\n\nBill ' + latest + ' is un-applied for ' + flat + ' (' + drop.length + ' row(s) removed, ' + added + ' accrual(s) re-estimated).\n\nNow press SYNC ALL in Today: the bill is re-planned from WeShare — ' + 'a big one appears in "Needs you" as a decision instead of being applied.'); } /* Sheet-menu wrapper — same step the sync runs, for checking it by hand. Safe without a preview: derived-state and idempotent, closed and manual rows are never written. */ function electricitySync8A() { hubBump_(['base']); var r = elecPrepaidSyncStep_(); SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(r.error ? ('Failed: ' + r.error) : 'Done ✓\n\n' + r.written + ' month row(s) written, ' + r.removed + ' stale mirror row(s) removed, ' + r.unchanged + ' already correct.\n' + (r.mismatches.length ? '\n⚠️ CLOSED months disagreeing with the recharges (reported, not changed):\n' + r.mismatches.map(function (x) { return ' ' + x.flat + ' ' + x.month + ' — booked ' + x.booked + ', recharges say ' + x.derived; }).join('\n') : 'Closed months all agree with the recharges ✓')); } function elecSyncStep_() { var out = { applied: 0, settled: 0, accrualsAdded: 0, accrualsRemoved: 0, deferred: [], dups: [] }; var P = elecApplyBillsPlan_(); if (!P.ok) { out.error = P.error; return out; } var safe = [], defer = []; P.actions.forEach(function (a) { (elecDecisionNeeded_(a) ? defer : safe).push(a); }); if (safe.length) { var W = elecApplyBillsWrite_(P, safe); out.applied = W.writes; out.settled = W.settled; out.dups = W.dups; } out.deferred = defer.map(function (a) { return { flat: a.flat, billMonth: a.bill.billMonth, amount: a.bill.amount, covers: a.bill.from + '..' + a.bill.to, span: a.bill.span, perMonth: a.per, closedMonths: a.closedMonths, accrued: a.closedAccrued, trueUp: a.trueUp, postTo: a.postTo }; }); var R = elecRefreshAccrualsPlan_(); // fresh read AFTER the bill writes if (R.ok && (R.plan.length || R.removals.length)) { var W2 = elecRefreshAccrualsWrite_(R); out.accrualsAdded = W2.added; out.accrualsRemoved = W2.removed; W2.dups.forEach(function (d) { if (out.dups.indexOf(d) < 0) out.dups.push(d); }); } try { out.prepaid = elecPrepaidSyncStep_(); } catch (ep) { out.prepaid = { error: String(ep && ep.message || ep) }; } // what props_alerts serves — refreshed on every sync, so it can never go stale silently. // METER8A is cached here too: computing it live on every Today load opened the DC workbook and // scanned all of WeShare + Stays per request, which slowed the whole Hub. Sync-time is the // right freshness anyway — a reading only changes when Emad logs one and someone syncs. var sp2 = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); sp2.setProperty('ELEC_DECISIONS', JSON.stringify(out.deferred)); sp2.deleteProperty('ELEC_PREPAID_NOTES'); // retired alert (2026-08-01) — the menu tool keeps the report try { sp2.setProperty('ELEC_METER8A', JSON.stringify(elecMeter8A_())); } catch (em) {} return out; } /* PLAN: read the sheet + WeShare and compute what each unapplied bill would do. No UI, no writes — shared by the sheet-menu tool and the sync pipeline, so both act on identical arithmetic. */ function elecApplyBillsPlan_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); if (!ed) return { ok: false, error: 'No "Electricity detail" tab.' }; var stat = {}, sv = (ss.getSheetByName(TAB.status) || { getDataRange: function () { return { getValues: function () { return []; } }; } }).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var s = 1; s < sv.length; s++) { var mk = monthKey(sv[s][0]); if (mk) stat[mk] = str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase(); } var isFinal = function (m) { return stat[m] === 'final'; }; var nowM = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); var oldLastRow = ed.getLastRow(), cols = Math.max(9, ed.getLastColumn()); var ev = ed.getRange(1, 1, oldLastRow, cols).getValues(); var flats = {}; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { var f = str(ev[i][0]), cm = monthKey(ev[i][1]); if (!f || !cm) continue; if (!flats[f]) flats[f] = { rows: [], applied: {}, appliedIds: {} }; var per = (Number(ev[i][4]) ? Number(ev[i][3]) / Number(ev[i][4]) : Number(ev[i][5])) || 0; flats[f].rows.push({ row: i + 1, covered: cm, billMonth: monthKey(ev[i][2]) || '', eff: Number(ev[i][3]) || 0, per: per, accrual: elecIsAccrual_(ev[i]), manual: /manual/i.test(str(ev[i][7])) }); var bm = monthKey(ev[i][2]); if (bm && !elecIsAccrual_(ev[i])) flats[f].applied[bm + '|' + round2(Number(ev[i][3]) || 0)] = true; // every [ws:] stamped into Source by a previous run — the exact record of what is applied var tags = str(ev[i][6]).match(/\[ws:[^\]]+\]/g); if (tags) tags.forEach(function (t) { flats[f].appliedIds[t.slice(4, -1)] = true; }); } var actions = [], notes = []; Object.keys(flats).forEach(function (f) { if (ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS[f]) return; // prepaid — no bills to apply var pay = elecPaymentsFor_(f); if (!pay.apartment.length) return; var A = flats[f]; // the earliest month this flat is covered from, taken from the tab var earliest = A.rows.reduce(function (mn, r) { return (!mn || r.covered < mn) ? r.covered : mn; }, null); var spans = elecBillSpans_(pay.apartment, earliest); spans.forEach(function (b) { /* Applied already? Two checks, and BOTH must be kept. The id check is exact and is how it works from now on. The amount check is the only thing that recognises rows written before ids existed — drop it and every historic bill would be applied a second time on the next run. Once a row has been rewritten with its [ws:] tag the id check carries it, and the legacy check simply stops mattering. */ if (b.id && A.appliedIds[b.id]) return; if (A.applied[b.billMonth + '|' + round2(b.amount)]) return; var per = round2(b.amount / b.span), openMonths = [], closedMonths = [], closedAccrued = 0; for (var k = 0; k < b.span; k++) { var m = monthAdd_(b.from, k); if (isFinal(m)) { closedMonths.push(m); A.rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.covered === m) closedAccrued += r.per; }); } else openMonths.push(m); } var trueUp = round2(closedMonths.length * per - closedAccrued); var postTo = !isFinal(b.billMonth) ? b.billMonth : (!isFinal(nowM) ? nowM : ''); actions.push({ flat: f, bill: b, per: per, openMonths: openMonths, closedMonths: closedMonths, closedAccrued: round2(closedAccrued), trueUp: trueUp, postTo: postTo }); if (trueUp !== 0 && !postTo) notes.push(f + ': bill ' + b.billMonth + ' needs a true-up of ' + trueUp + ' but every candidate month is closed — reopen one.'); }); }); return { ok: true, ed: ed, cols: cols, oldLastRow: oldLastRow, ev: ev, flats: flats, actions: actions, notes: notes }; } /* THE MAGNITUDE RULE (§5.2c). A bill covering only open months corrects itself exactly — apply it silently. One touching closed months is fine while the difference is small; when the correction is bigger than a fifth of the bill or bigger than one month's true cost, dumping it into the bill month produces a figure that means nothing (Zayed's five-month bill would leave July at 54.68 against a true ~1,130). That call — absorb or restate — belongs to Sarah, so the sync defers it. */ function elecDecisionNeeded_(a) { if (!a.closedMonths.length) return false; /* HALF a month's true cost, not a full month. The first live case proved the looser test wrong: Zayed's five-month 5,648 bill had a true-up of −1,074.92 against a per-month of 1,129.60 — under both old thresholds by ~5%, so the sync absorbed it and July read 54.68 (95% of the month wiped out) with no decision raised. A correction that moves the receiving month by more than half its true cost is a distortion, not a rounding. */ return Math.abs(a.trueUp) > 0.20 * Math.abs(a.bill.amount) || Math.abs(a.trueUp) > 0.5 * Math.abs(a.per); } /* The sheet-menu tool. Running it BY HAND is the "absorb" decision, so it applies everything — after showing exactly what will happen, with the big ones flagged and the rebuild named. */ function electricityApplyBills() { hubBump_(['base']); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); var P = elecApplyBillsPlan_(); if (!P.ok) { ui.alert(P.error); return; } if (!P.actions.length) { ui.alert('Nothing to apply ✓\n\nEvery electricity bill in WeShare is already reflected in Electricity detail.'); return; } var lines = P.actions.map(function (a) { return '• ' + a.flat + ' bill ' + a.bill.billMonth + ' ' + a.bill.amount + ' EGP over ' + a.bill.span + ' month(s) = ' + a.per + '/mo\n' + ' open, actual written: ' + (a.openMonths.join(', ') || 'none') + '\n' + ' closed, left alone: ' + (a.closedMonths.join(', ') || 'none') + (a.closedMonths.length ? ' (accrued ' + a.closedAccrued + ' vs ' + round2(a.closedMonths.length * a.per) + ')' : '') + '\n' + ' TRUE-UP ' + (a.trueUp >= 0 ? '+' : '') + a.trueUp + ' → ' + (a.postTo || 'NOWHERE — all months closed') + (elecDecisionNeeded_(a) ? '\n ⚠️ LARGE — applying puts the whole correction in ' + (a.postTo || '?') + '. To spread it over the covered months instead, cancel and run "Electricity — rebuild a flat from WeShare".' : ''); }).join('\n'); if (ui.alert('Apply electricity bills', 'DRY RUN — nothing changed yet.\n\n' + lines + (P.notes.length ? '\n\n⚠️ ' + P.notes.join('\n⚠️ ') : '') + '\n\nClosed months are never modified.\n\nApply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var W = elecApplyBillsWrite_(P, P.actions); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + W.writes + ' row(s) written, ' + W.settled + ' accrual(s) settled.\nClosed months untouched.\n\n' + (W.dups.length ? '⚠️ more than one row for: ' + W.dups.join(', ') : 'Verified: exactly one row per flat/month ✓')); } /* WRITE: settle + rebuild for the given subset of planned actions. */ function elecApplyBillsWrite_(P, actions) { var ed = P.ed, cols = P.cols, oldLastRow = P.oldLastRow, ev = P.ev, flats = P.flats; /* SETTLE + WRITE as one rebuild (see elecWriteAll_). The settlement — removing the accrual for an open month the bill now covers — is the step that must not fail: leave the accrual in place beside the new actual and that month is counted twice. deleteRow cannot be trusted here, so the accrual is simply excluded from the rebuilt block instead. */ var dropRows = {}, writes = 0; actions.forEach(function (a) { a.openMonths.forEach(function (m) { flats[a.flat].rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.covered === m && r.accrual && !r.manual) dropRows[r.row] = true; }); }); }); var settled = Object.keys(dropRows).length; var keep = []; for (var kr = 1; kr < ev.length; kr++) { if (dropRows[kr + 1]) continue; if (!str(ev[kr][0]) && !monthKey(ev[kr][1])) continue; // blank filler row keep.push(ev[kr]); } actions.forEach(function (a) { a.openMonths.forEach(function (m) { keep.push(elecRowArr_(cols, a.flat, m, a.bill.billMonth, a.bill.amount, a.bill.span, a.bill.amount.toFixed(2) + ' EGP (' + a.flat + ' Electricity — apartment meter; bill paid ' + a.bill.paid + ', covers ' + a.bill.from + '..' + a.bill.to + ' (' + a.bill.span + '))' + elecWsTag_(a.bill.id), 'actual')); writes++; }); if (a.trueUp !== 0 && a.postTo) { keep.push(elecRowArr_(cols, a.flat, a.postTo, a.bill.billMonth, a.trueUp, 1, 'True-up ' + (a.trueUp >= 0 ? '+' : '') + a.trueUp.toFixed(2) + ' EGP (' + a.flat + ' Electricity — bill ' + a.bill.billMonth + ' covered ' + a.bill.from + '..' + a.bill.to + '; ' + a.closedMonths.length + ' month(s) already closed had accrued ' + a.closedAccrued + ' against ' + round2(a.closedMonths.length * a.per) + ', so the difference lands here)' + elecWsTag_(a.bill.id), 'true-up')); writes++; } }); elecWriteAll_(ed, keep, cols, oldLastRow); if (!str(ed.getRange(1, 8).getValue())) ed.getRange(1, 8).setValue('Type'); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); // a true-up beside an unsettled accrual would double-count, so prove one row per flat/month var chk = ed.getDataRange().getValues(), seen2 = {}, dup2 = []; for (var z = 1; z < chk.length; z++) { var zf = str(chk[z][0]), zm = monthKey(chk[z][1]); if (!zf || !zm) continue; var zk = zf + '|' + zm; seen2[zk] = (seen2[zk] || 0) + 1; if (seen2[zk] === 2) dup2.push(zk); } return { writes: writes, settled: settled, dups: dup2 }; } /* ============================================================ * ELECTRICITY — COLLAPSE DUPLICATE ROWS * * The P&L sums EVERY 'Electricity detail' row matching flat+month, so two rows for the same month * double that month's cost. Duplicates appeared because the refresher's delete step did not always * match what it intended to remove; rather than keep guessing at that, this is a small tool that * does one thing and states its rules plainly: * * • only ever looks at ONE flat+month group at a time * • never touches a month marked `final` → those are reported, not changed * • never touches a row marked `manual` * • group of accruals only → keep the LAST, delete the rest * • group mixing actual + accrual → delete the accruals, keep the actual(s) (that is the settlement) * • two or more ACTUALS → reported, never guessed at — that needs a human * * Re-reads and re-matches immediately before each delete, and verifies afterwards. * ============================================================ */ function electricityFixDuplicates() { hubBump_(['base']); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); if (!ed) { ui.alert('No "Electricity detail" tab.'); return; } var stat = {}, sv = (ss.getSheetByName(TAB.status) || { getDataRange: function () { return { getValues: function () { return []; } }; } }).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var s = 1; s < sv.length; s++) { var mk = monthKey(sv[s][0]); if (mk) stat[mk] = str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase(); } var lastRow = ed.getLastRow(), cols = Math.max(9, ed.getLastColumn()); if (lastRow < 2) { ui.alert('"Electricity detail" has no data rows.'); return; } var all = ed.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, cols).getValues(); var v = all, groups = {}, order = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var f = str(v[i][0]), m = monthKey(v[i][1]); if (!f || !m) continue; var key = f + '|' + m; if (!groups[key]) { groups[key] = []; order.push(key); } groups[key].push({ row: i + 1, flat: f, month: m, vals: v[i], accrual: elecIsAccrual_(v[i]), manual: /manual/i.test(str(v[i][7])), per: (Number(v[i][4]) ? Number(v[i][3]) / Number(v[i][4]) : Number(v[i][5])) || 0 }); } var toDelete = [], reportOnly = [], keep = []; order.forEach(function (key) { var g = groups[key]; if (g.length < 2) { keep.push(g[0].vals); return; } var parts = key.split('|'), month = parts[1]; var sum = 0; g.forEach(function (r) { sum += r.per; }); if (stat[month] === 'final') { g.forEach(function (r) { keep.push(r.vals); }); // closed → keep every row, untouched reportOnly.push(key + ' — ' + g.length + ' rows, month is CLOSED (left alone), sums to ' + round2(sum)); return; } var actuals = g.filter(function (r) { return !r.accrual; }); var manuals = g.filter(function (r) { return r.manual; }); if (actuals.length > 1) { g.forEach(function (r) { keep.push(r.vals); }); // two real bills → never guess reportOnly.push(key + ' — ' + actuals.length + ' ACTUAL rows, needs a human (left as-is)'); return; } // one survivor per group: a manual override wins, then a real actual, else the newest accrual var winner = manuals.length ? manuals[manuals.length - 1] : (actuals.length === 1 ? actuals[0] : g[g.length - 1]); g.forEach(function (r) { if (r === winner) keep.push(r.vals); else toDelete.push({ row: r.row, key: key, why: r.accrual ? 'duplicate accrual' : 'superseded' }); }); }); if (!toDelete.length && !reportOnly.length) { ui.alert('Nothing to fix ✓\n\nExactly one row per flat/month everywhere.'); return; } var byKey = {}; toDelete.forEach(function (d) { byKey[d.key] = (byKey[d.key] || 0) + 1; }); var lines = Object.keys(byKey).map(function (k) { return ' ' + k + ' → delete ' + byKey[k] + ', keep 1'; }).join('\n'); if (ui.alert('Collapse duplicate electricity rows', 'DRY RUN — nothing changed yet.\n\nDELETE\n' + (lines || ' none') + (reportOnly.length ? '\n\nREPORTED, NOT CHANGED\n ' + reportOnly.join('\n ') : '') + '\n\nApply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } /* ---- REWRITE THE BLOCK INSTEAD OF DELETING ROWS ---- deleteRow does not work on this tab. Three separate implementations each reported success and changed nothing, and the proof is in the sheet: every PerMonth formula still references its own row (=D82/E82 at row 82). Sheets rewrites those references whenever a row above is removed, so no row has ever actually been deleted here — while setValue writes have always landed. So this uses only the API path that demonstrably works: build the desired final block in memory and write it in ONE setValues call, padding the remainder with blanks. Nothing is deleted, the end state is computed rather than patched, and re-running is therefore harmless. */ var stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd HHmm'); var bk = ss.insertSheet('Elec detail backup ' + stamp); bk.getRange(1, 1, all.length, cols).setValues(all); // full snapshot first var rowsBefore = ed.getLastRow(); elecWriteAll_(ed, keep, cols, lastRow); // the one writer — keeps months as TEXT var rowsAfter = ed.getLastRow(); // verify var v2 = ed.getDataRange().getValues(), seen = {}, still = []; for (var j = 1; j < v2.length; j++) { var f2 = str(v2[j][0]), m2 = monthKey(v2[j][1]); if (!f2 || !m2) continue; var k2 = f2 + '|' + m2; seen[k2] = (seen[k2] || 0) + 1; if (seen[k2] === 2) still.push(k2); } var actuallyRemoved = rowsBefore - rowsAfter; ui.alert( (still.length ? '⚠️ NOT CLEAN\n\n' : 'Done ✓\n\n') + 'File: ' + ss.getName() + '\n id ' + ss.getId() + '\n\nData rows: ' + (rowsBefore - 1) + ' → ' + (rowsAfter - 1) + '\nRemoved ' + actuallyRemoved + ' of ' + toDelete.length + ' planned' + (actuallyRemoved === toDelete.length ? ' ✓' : ' ⚠️') + '\n\n' + (still.length ? 'Still duplicated: ' + still.join(', ') + '\nSend Claude this whole message.' : 'Verified: exactly one row per flat/month ✓') + (reportOnly.length ? '\n\nLeft alone on purpose:\n ' + reportOnly.join('\n ') : '') + '\n\nBackup tab: "Elec detail backup ' + stamp + '"'); } function electricityRestateZayed() { return electricityRestateFlat_('Zayed 2000'); } /* Rebuild any one flat from the WeShare cash record. Use after fixing how payments are read — it * discards that flat's rows and recomputes them, so it also clears bad rows a tool wrote earlier. */ function electricityRestatePrompt() { hubBump_(['base']); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); var q = ui.prompt('Rebuild a flat from WeShare', 'Which flat? (8A · 17A · 17B · 28A · Zayed 2000)\n\n' + 'Its rows in Electricity detail are recomputed from the WeShare payments: bills spread since the ' + 'prior bill, the meter share applied, then accruals for months with no bill yet.', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL); if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var flat = elecCanonFlat_(q.getResponseText()); // exact-cased id — a mismatch here would APPEND beside the old rows instead of replacing if (!flat) { ui.alert('"' + str(q.getResponseText()).trim() + '" is not a known flat. Use 8A, 17A, 17B, 28A or Zayed 2000.'); return; } if (ELEC_PREPAID_FLATS[flat]) { ui.alert(flat + ' is a PREPAID card.\n\nIts recharges are logged in WeShare under the "Monthly Expense" ' + 'group, not "Airbnb Monthly", so there are no bills to rebuild from. Nothing changed.'); return; } electricityRestateFlat_(flat); } /* Rebuilds one flat's Electricity detail rows from the WeShare cash record + the accrual rule, and * moves its building/common-area charges to 'Cost line items' as compound maintenance. */ function electricityRestateFlat_(flat) { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'), cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (!ed || !cli) { ui.alert('Need both "Electricity detail" and "Cost line items" tabs.'); return; } var ev = ed.getDataRange().getValues(), existing = [], curTotal = 0, earliest = null; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { if (str(ev[i][0]) !== flat) continue; existing.push(i + 1); curTotal += Number(ev[i][5]) || 0; var cm = monthKey(ev[i][1]); if (cm && (!earliest || cm < earliest)) earliest = cm; // from the tab, not invented } var pay = elecPaymentsFor_(flat); if (!pay.apartment.length) { ui.alert('No apartment electricity payments found in WeShare for ' + flat + ' — nothing to rebuild.'); return; } var spans = elecBillSpans_(pay.apartment, earliest); var through = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); // accrue up to the current month var desired = elecDesiredRows_(flat, spans, through, 1); var newTotal = 0, nAcc = 0; desired.forEach(function (r) { newTotal += r.amount / r.span; if (r.type === 'accrual') nAcc++; }); // the orphan: a Cost line items row categorised 'Electricity' — the P&L never reads that category var cv = cli.getDataRange().getValues(), orphanRow = 0, orphanAmt = 0; for (var c = 1; c < cv.length; c++) { if (str(cv[c][0]) === flat && /^\s*electricity\s*$/i.test(str(cv[c][2]))) { orphanRow = c + 1; orphanAmt = Number(cv[c][4]) || 0; break; } } var bldgTotal = 0; pay.building.forEach(function (b) { bldgTotal += b.amount; }); // rows a previous run of this tool left behind — they get removed, so re-running is safe var priorRuns = 0; for (var pr = 1; pr < cv.length; pr++) if (str(cv[pr][0]) === flat && /^restated\b/i.test(str(cv[pr][5]))) priorRuns++; var billLines = spans.map(function (b) { return ' ' + b.paid + ' ' + b.amount + ' EGP → ' + b.from + '..' + b.to + ' (' + b.span + ') = ' + round2(b.amount / b.span) + '/mo'; }).join('\n'); var accLines = desired.filter(function (r) { return r.type === 'accrual'; }) .map(function (r) { return ' ' + r.covered + ' ' + r.amount; }).join('\n'); var msg = 'DRY RUN — nothing changed yet.\n\nBILLS FOUND IN WESHARE (apartment meter)\n' + billLines + '\n\nACCRUALS COMPUTED (no bill yet)\n' + (accLines || ' none') + '\n\nELECTRICITY DETAIL\n replace ' + existing.length + ' rows with ' + desired.length + '\n monthly total ' + Math.round(curTotal) + ' → ' + Math.round(newTotal) + ' EGP (' + (newTotal >= curTotal ? '+' : '') + Math.round(newTotal - curTotal) + ')' + '\n\nCOST LINE ITEMS\n add ' + pay.building.length + ' building rows as "compound maintenance" = ' + round2(bldgTotal) + ' EGP\n' + (priorRuns ? ' first REMOVE ' + priorRuns + ' row(s) from an earlier run of this tool (so re-running never double-counts)\n' : '') + (orphanRow ? ' re-categorise row ' + orphanRow + ' (' + orphanAmt + ' EGP, "Electricity" → counted nowhere)\n' : '') + '\nCurrent rows are backed up first.\n\nApply?'; if (ui.alert('Restate ' + flat + ' electricity', msg, ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } // ---- backup, then apply var stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd HHmm'); var bk = ss.insertSheet('Elec detail backup ' + stamp); bk.getRange(1, 1, 1, ev[0].length).setValues([ev[0]]); if (existing.length) { var bkRows = existing.map(function (rn) { return ev[rn - 1]; }); bk.getRange(2, 1, bkRows.length, ev[0].length).setValues(bkRows); } /* Rebuild rather than delete (see elecWriteAll_): keep every other flat's rows untouched, drop this flat's old ones, append the recomputed set, write the block once. */ var cols2 = Math.max(9, ed.getLastColumn()), oldLast = ed.getLastRow(); var dropSet = {}; existing.forEach(function (rn) { dropSet[rn] = true; }); var keep2 = []; for (var kk = 1; kk < ev.length; kk++) { if (dropSet[kk + 1]) continue; if (!str(ev[kk][0]) && !monthKey(ev[kk][1])) continue; keep2.push(ev[kk]); } /* THE RESTATED MARK (§5.2e). Rebuilding is the one tool allowed to change a closed month, so a closed month whose figure moves must say so on the row itself: Type becomes `restated` and the Source keeps the superseded figure. Sarah's requirement, verbatim: "so that i understand and follow-up on what is happening". Open months keep their ordinary actual/accrual types. */ var statR = {}, svR = (ss.getSheetByName(TAB.status) || { getDataRange: function () { return { getValues: function () { return []; } }; } }).getDataRange().getValues(); for (var sr = 1; sr < svR.length; sr++) { var mR = monthKey(svR[sr][0]); if (mR) statR[mR] = str(svR[sr][1]).toLowerCase(); } var oldPer = {}; // covered month → what the P&L used to charge existing.forEach(function (rn) { var row = ev[rn - 1], cmO = monthKey(row[1]); if (!cmO) return; var perO = (Number(row[4]) ? Number(row[3]) / Number(row[4]) : Number(row[5])) || 0; oldPer[cmO] = (oldPer[cmO] || 0) + perO; }); var restatedMonths = []; desired.forEach(function (r) { var newPer = round2(r.amount / (r.span || 1)); if (statR[r.covered] === 'final' && oldPer[r.covered] != null && round2(oldPer[r.covered]) !== newPer) { r.type = 'restated'; r.source += ' [restated ' + stamp + ', was ' + round2(oldPer[r.covered]).toFixed(2) + ']'; restatedMonths.push(r.covered + ' (' + round2(oldPer[r.covered]).toFixed(2) + ' → ' + newPer.toFixed(2) + ')'); } keep2.push(elecRowArr_(cols2, flat, r.covered, r.billMonth, r.amount, r.span, r.source, r.type)); }); elecWriteAll_(ed, keep2, cols2, oldLast); if (!str(ed.getRange(1, 8).getValue())) ed.getRange(1, 8).setValue('Type'); if (orphanRow) cli.getRange(orphanRow, 3).setValue('compound maintenance'); /* RE-RUNNABLE: drop any rows a previous run of this tool created for this flat before writing new ones. Without this, running the restatement twice appended the building charges twice and double-counted compound maintenance — Electricity detail looked fine because it is deleted and rebuilt, which hid the problem. Only rows this tool wrote are touched (Source starts "restated"). */ var cvNow = cli.getDataRange().getValues(), dupRows = []; for (var dz = cvNow.length - 1; dz >= 1; dz--) { if (str(cvNow[dz][0]) === flat && /^restated\b/i.test(str(cvNow[dz][5]))) dupRows.push(dz + 1); } dupRows.forEach(function (rn) { cli.deleteRow(rn); }); // already descending /* Dates must be REAL date values in the same display format as the rest of column B: the P&L filters this tab with $B:$B >= DATEVALUE(month&"-01") and < EDATE(...,1) — a text date satisfies neither, and the row would silently count nowhere. Copy the format from the NEAREST populated date cell (scanning upward from the bottom) so new rows match the format actually in current use, not whatever row 2 happens to have. Dates are written at midday so no timezone shift can move the day. */ var cStart = cli.getLastRow() + 1; var dateFmt = ''; for (var fr = cli.getLastRow(); fr >= 2; fr--) { if (cli.getRange(fr, 2).getValue() instanceof Date) { dateFmt = cli.getRange(fr, 2).getNumberFormat(); break; } } pay.building.forEach(function (b, k) { var rw = cStart + k; if (dateFmt) cli.getRange(rw, 2).setNumberFormat(dateFmt); cli.getRange(rw, 1, 1, 6).setValues([[ flat, new Date(b.date + 'T12:00:00'), 'compound maintenance', b.desc + ' — building/common-area electricity, moved off the flat meter (was on the electricity line)', b.amount, 'restated ' + stamp ]]); }); // prove the dates landed as dates, not text — otherwise the P&L would silently drop them var badDates = 0; for (var vk = 0; vk < pay.building.length; vk++) if (!(cli.getRange(cStart + vk, 2).getValue() instanceof Date)) badDates++; SpreadsheetApp.flush(); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\nElectricity detail: ' + desired.length + ' ' + flat + ' rows (' + nAcc + ' accruals).' + (restatedMonths.length ? '\n\n🔁 RESTATED closed months:\n ' + restatedMonths.join('\n ') + '\nEach carries a "restated" mark and its old figure — visible in the Close drill-down.' : '') + '\nCost line items: ' + pay.building.length + ' building rows as compound maintenance' + (orphanRow ? ' + 1 re-categorised' : '') + '.' + (badDates ? '\n\n⚠️ ' + badDates + ' date cell(s) did NOT store as a real date — tell Claude before closing the month.' : '\n\nDate check: all ' + pay.building.length + ' stored as real dates ✓') + '\n\nBackup tab: "Elec detail backup ' + stamp + '".'); } /* Gives every Repairs row a real ID in column A. * Why: rows pasted as history have a blank column A, and buildRepairs() then FABRICATES an id * (`'RH' + rowNumber`) so the app has something to key on. But delete/edit look that id up in * column A, find nothing, and fail with "repair not found" — so those tickets could be neither * deleted nor edited. Measured 2026-07-25: 37 of 39 rows had no real ID. * IDs are written as RB (distinct from R for app-created tickets) so it's obvious * which were backfilled. Never overwrites an existing ID. */ function backfillRepairIdsPrompt() { hubBump_(['repairs']); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Repairs'); if (!sh) { ui.alert('No "Repairs" tab found.'); return; } var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), missing = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (!str(v[i][0]) && (str(v[i][1]) || str(v[i][2]) || str(v[i][5]))) missing.push(i + 1); // has content, no ID } if (!missing.length) { ui.alert('Nothing to do ✓\n\nEvery repair row already has an ID.'); return; } if (ui.alert('Backfill repair IDs', missing.length + ' repair row' + (missing.length > 1 ? 's have' : ' has') + ' no ID, which is why deleting or ' + 'editing them fails with "repair not found".\n\nWrite an ID into column A for those rows?\n\n' + 'Existing IDs are never touched.', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO) !== ui.Button.YES) return; for (var m = 0; m < missing.length; m++) sh.getRange(missing[m], 1).setValue('RB' + missing[m]); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + missing.length + ' row' + (missing.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' given an ID. Those repairs can now be edited and deleted.'); } /* ---------- read-only debug access, switched from here (no code edit, no redeploy) ---------- * Grants a temporary READ-ONLY token for diagnosis. It cannot write, cannot change a password, * and cannot read password hashes. Every grant is a NEW token with an expiry, so access lapses * by itself; "turn OFF" kills it immediately. */ function diagAccessOn() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); var q = ui.prompt('Debug access — turn ON', 'Grant READ-ONLY access for how many hours?\n\n' + 'Default 24. It expires by itself — you do not have to remember to switch it off.\n' + 'It can read (including the P&L) but cannot change anything.', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL); if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var hours = parseFloat(str(q.getResponseText()).trim() || '24'); if (!isFinite(hours) || hours <= 0) { ui.alert('Enter a number of hours greater than 0 — nothing changed.'); return; } if (hours > 720) { ui.alert('That is over a month. Pick 720 hours or fewer.'); return; } var token = 'OWH-DIAG-' + newToken_().slice(0, 24); // fresh every grant var exp = new Date(Date.now() + hours * 3600000); PropertiesService.getScriptProperties() .setProperty('DIAG_TOKEN', token) .setProperty('DIAG_TOKEN_EXPIRES', exp.toISOString()); ui.alert('Debug access is ON ✓\n\n' + 'Expires: ' + Utilities.formatDate(exp, TZ, 'EEE d MMM, HH:mm') + ' (' + hours + 'h)\n\n' + 'Token to share:\n\n' + token + '\n\n' + 'Read-only: it cannot change data, passwords, or sessions.\n' + 'Turn it off any time with "Debug access — turn OFF".'); } function diagAccessOff() { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var had = !!sp.getProperty('DIAG_TOKEN'); sp.deleteProperty('DIAG_TOKEN'); sp.deleteProperty('DIAG_TOKEN_EXPIRES'); SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(had ? 'Debug access is OFF ✓\n\nThe token stops working immediately. Any future grant mints a new one.' : 'Debug access was already off — nothing to revoke.'); } function diagAccessStatus() { var s = diagStatus_(); var msg = s.on ? 'Debug access: ON\n\nExpires ' + (s.expires ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(s.expires), TZ, 'EEE d MMM, HH:mm') : 'never (no expiry set)') + (s.hoursLeft != null ? '\n' + s.hoursLeft + ' hours left' : '') + '\n\nRead-only: no writes, no password changes, no session changes.' : (s.lapsed ? 'Debug access: OFF (it expired on its own ✓)' : 'Debug access: OFF'); SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg); } /* Removes the account AND its sessions in one step. Use this instead of deleting a row by hand: * a leftover session row is a token that still works. */ function removeUserPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), B = ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL; var q = ui.prompt('Remove a person', 'Username to remove completely:', B); if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var user = str(q.getResponseText()).trim(); if (!user) { ui.alert('No username entered — nothing changed.'); return; } var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = usersSheet_(ss), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var un = user.toLowerCase(), removed = 0; for (var i = v.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) if (str(v[i][0]).toLowerCase() === un) { sh.deleteRow(i + 1); removed++; } if (!removed) { ui.alert('No user called "' + user + '" — nothing changed.'); return; } var revoked = revokeSessionsFor_(user); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); ui.alert('Removed ✓\n\n"' + user + '" can no longer sign in' + (revoked ? ', and ' + revoked + ' signed-in device' + (revoked > 1 ? 's were' : ' was') + ' signed out.' : '.')); } /* Signs out every device for everyone (tokens cleared). Passwords are untouched — each person * simply signs in again. Use after removing people by hand, or if a device is lost. */ function signEveryoneOutPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); var r = ui.alert('Sign everyone out', 'This signs out every device for every person, including you.\n\n' + 'Passwords are NOT changed — everyone just signs in again.\n\nContinue?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (r !== ui.Button.YES) return; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = sessionsSheet_(ss); var n = Math.max(0, sh.getLastRow() - 1); if (n) sh.deleteRows(2, n); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + n + ' session' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' cleared. Everyone signs in again.'); } function setPasswordPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), B = ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL; var q = ui.prompt('Set / reset a password', 'Username (e.g. sarah or khaled):', B); if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var user = str(q.getResponseText()).trim(); if (!user) { ui.alert('No username entered — nothing changed.'); return; } var q2 = ui.prompt('Set / reset a password', 'New password for "' + user + '":\n\nAt least 8 characters. It is hashed with a random salt — ' + 'the sheet never stores the password itself.', B); if (q2.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var pw = q2.getResponseText(); if (!pw || pw.length < 8) { ui.alert('Password must be at least 8 characters — nothing changed.'); return; } var q3 = ui.prompt('Set / reset a password', 'Role for "' + user + '" — type admin or partner\n\n' + 'admin = full P&L + can change things.\npartner = their own flat only (rent and attribution hidden).', B); if (q3.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; var role = (str(q3.getResponseText()).trim() || 'admin').toLowerCase(); if (role !== 'admin' && role !== 'partner') { ui.alert('Role must be "admin" or "partner" — nothing changed.'); return; } var flat = ''; if (role === 'partner') { var q4 = ui.prompt('Set / reset a password', 'Which flat may this partner see? (e.g. 8A)', B); if (q4.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return; flat = str(q4.getResponseText()).trim(); if (!flat) { ui.alert('A partner needs a flat — nothing changed.'); return; } } var res = setPassword_(user, pw, role, flat); if (!res || !res.ok) { ui.alert('Failed: ' + ((res && res.error) || 'unknown')); return; } var revoked = revokeSessionsFor_(user); // a password change signs out old sessions ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n"' + user + '" (' + role + (flat ? ' · ' + flat : '') + ') can now sign in' + (res.created ? ' — new user created.' : ' with the new password.') + (revoked ? '\n\n' + revoked + ' existing session' + (revoked > 1 ? 's were' : ' was') + ' signed out.' : '')); } function listUsersPrompt() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), v = usersSheet_(ss).getDataRange().getValues(); var sv = sessionsSheet_(ss).getDataRange().getValues(), now = Date.now(), live = {}; for (var s = 1; s < sv.length; s++) { var exp = sv[s][2] instanceof Date ? sv[s][2].getTime() : new Date(sv[s][2]).getTime(); if (!isNaN(exp) && exp > now) live[str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase()] = (live[str(sv[s][1]).toLowerCase()] || 0) + 1; } var lines = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (!str(v[i][0])) continue; var n = live[str(v[i][0]).toLowerCase()] || 0; lines.push('• ' + str(v[i][0]) + ' — ' + (str(v[i][3]) || 'admin') + (str(v[i][4]) ? ' (' + str(v[i][4]) + ')' : '') + (str(v[i][1]) ? '' : ' ⚠ NO PASSWORD SET') + (n ? ' · signed in on ' + n + ' device' + (n > 1 ? 's' : '') : '')); } SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Who can sign in to The Hub\n\n' + (lines.join('\n') || 'nobody yet') + '\n\nTo remove someone: delete their row in the "Auth users" tab.'); } // Writes, diagnostics and password changes: a valid ADMIN SESSION and nothing else. No token can // substitute — a token that ships to a browser is public, and would hand over the whole P&L. // Locked out? Set a password from the sheet: menu "The Hub" → "Set / reset a password" (owner-only). function writeOk_(p) { var s = validateSession_(p.sid); return !!(s && s.role === 'admin'); } // Read-only diagnostics: DIAG_TOKEN, restricted to the DIAG_FNS allow-list. Deliberately cannot // write, cannot change a password, and cannot dump password hashes — so debugging never requires // handing out admin rights. Blank DIAG_TOKEN to revoke instantly. function diagOk_(p) { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var tok = sp.getProperty('DIAG_TOKEN'); if (!tok) return false; // switched off (default state) var exp = sp.getProperty('DIAG_TOKEN_EXPIRES'); if (exp && new Date(exp).getTime() < Date.now()) return false; // lapsed on its own if (str(p.token) !== tok) return false; return DIAG_FNS.indexOf(str(p.fn)) > -1; // read-only allow-list } // Is debug access on? Returns {on, expires, hoursLeft} — used by the menu and diag_valsess. function diagStatus_() { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var tok = sp.getProperty('DIAG_TOKEN'), exp = sp.getProperty('DIAG_TOKEN_EXPIRES'); if (!tok) return { on: false }; var ms = exp ? new Date(exp).getTime() - Date.now() : null; if (ms !== null && ms < 0) return { on: false, lapsed: true, expires: exp }; return { on: true, expires: exp || null, hoursLeft: ms === null ? null : Math.round(ms / 3600000 * 10) / 10 }; } // Sync only: an admin session, OR SYNC_TOKEN held server-side by WeShare's backend. // Worst case if SYNC_TOKEN leaks: someone re-runs an idempotent sync. It returns no data and // cannot write P&L inputs, change passwords, or reach any diagnostic. function syncOk_(p) { if (writeOk_(p)) return true; return !!(SYNC_TOKEN && SYNC_TOKEN !== 'CHANGE-ME-SYNC' && p.token === SYNC_TOKEN); } // ---- main builder ------------------------------------------------- var __PGA_T = {}; // cumulative ms after each build stage — filled per run, read by diag_getall /* reuse (2026-08-04, section cache): pre-built {stays, cleans, repairs, internet, defaults} from the section caches — when given, those builders are skipped and only the BASE (tabs+pnl+tx) is computed. Partner/demo calls never pass reuse and behave exactly as before. */ function propsGetAll(who, scope, reuse) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var __t0 = Date.now(); __PGA_T = {}; function __tick(k){ __PGA_T[k] = Date.now() - __t0; } var configRows = readTab(ss, TAB.config); // Property, attributionType, khaledRevPct, onAirbnbSplits, isLongLet, hasPartner, ownerSharePct, isDemo, demoSource, displayName var targetRows = readTab(ss, TAB.targets); // Property, Target B (30-day), Target A (30-day), [note] var statusRows = readTab(ss, TAB.status); // Month, Status, Closed at, FX used var fxRows = readTab(ss, TAB.fx); // Month, EGP per USD, Source var pnlTab = readTabWithHeader(ss, TAB.pnl); __tick('tabs'); // ---- flats master + config var flats = configRows.rows.map(function (r) { var g = colGetter(configRows.idx, r); return { id: g('Property'), name: str(g('displayName')) || g('Property'), // the demo flat can present under a friendlier name isLongLet: truthy(g('isLongLet')), onAirbnbSplits: truthy(g('onAirbnbSplits')), isDemo: truthy(g('isDemo')), demoSource: str(g('demoSource')), partner: { hasPartner: truthy(g('hasPartner')), sharePctOfTotalRev: numOrNull(g('ownerSharePct')) }, attribution: { type: g('attributionType'), khaledPctOfTotalRev: pctToNum(g('khaledRevPct')) } }; }); var flatsById = index(flats, 'id'); var flatsOrder = flats.map(function (f) { return f.id; }); /* ---- the demo flat ------------------------------------------------------------------------ A Config row flagged `isDemo` holds NO data of its own. It borrows another flat's real figures at read time (`demoSource`) and re-presents them under its OWN partner terms, so a demo partner can exist without inventing one on a real flat. It is removed from the roster below in EVERY case, so it is absent from `flats`, `flatsOrder`, the portfolio and every admin screen — those all iterate flatsOrder, so none of them can pick it up by accident. It materialises only for a session whose flat IS the demo flat, or for an admin that explicitly asks with scope='demo'. The sheet-menu master switch kills both at once. */ var demoFlat = null; for (var di = 0; di < flats.length; di++) if (flats[di].isDemo) { demoFlat = flats[di]; break; } var demoId = demoFlat ? demoFlat.id : null; var demoOn = demoIsOn_(); var wantDemo = !!(demoFlat && demoOn && (who.flat === demoId || (who.role === 'admin' && scope === 'demo'))); if (demoId) { // never part of the real roster, switch on or off flats = flats.filter(function (f) { return f.id !== demoId; }); flatsOrder = flatsOrder.filter(function (f) { return f !== demoId; }); delete flatsById[demoId]; } // the kill switch: a partner assigned to the demo flat gets nothing while it is off if (demoFlat && who.flat === demoId && !demoOn) throw new Error('The demo is switched off.'); if (wantDemo && !flatsById[str(demoFlat.demoSource)]) throw new Error('Demo source flat "' + str(demoFlat.demoSource) + '" not found in Config.'); /* What this response actually renders. An admin previewing the demo is given the PARTNER view — the same payload the partner receives, so a demo can never show something rosier than reality. */ var viewRole = wantDemo ? 'partner' : who.role; var readFlat = wantDemo ? str(demoFlat.demoSource) : who.flat; // whose real rows to borrow var asDemo = function (o) { // relabel a borrowed row as the demo flat var c = {}; for (var k in o) c[k] = o[k]; c.flat = demoId; return c; }; // ---- targets var targets = { daysBasis: 30, byFlat: {} }; targetRows.rows.forEach(function (r) { var g = colGetter(targetRows.idx, r); var name = g('Property'); if (!name || /total/i.test(name)) return; // skip the "Total payout" row targets.byFlat[name] = { B: num(g('Target B (30-day)')), A: num(g('Target A (30-day)')) }; }); // ---- status map (month -> final|open) var statusByMonth = {}; statusRows.rows.forEach(function (r) { var g = colGetter(statusRows.idx, r); var m = monthKey(g('Month')); if (m) statusByMonth[m] = (g('Status') || 'open').toString().toLowerCase(); }); // ---- fx (last closing carried forward) var fxCurrent = null, fxSourceMonth = null, fxByMonth = {}, fxCarried = {}; fxRows.rows.forEach(function (r) { var g = colGetter(fxRows.idx, r); var m = monthKey(g('Month')), rate = num(g('EGP per USD')); if (m && rate) { fxByMonth[m] = rate; fxCurrent = rate; fxSourceMonth = m; // last non-empty wins /* a CARRIED rate (seeded when the month opened) keeps the P&L honest but is NOT a decision — it must not silence the "set the closing rate" prompt. Source col is positional (3rd), the same cell setFx writes, so no header-name assumption. */ if (/^carried/i.test(str(r[2]))) fxCarried[m] = true; } }); // ---- Monthly P&L → pnl + portfolio var H = pnlTab.idx; var iProp = H['Property'], iMonth = H['Month'], iRev = H['Revenue (USD)']; var iCostsTotal = H['Costs total (EGP)'], iNet = H['Net (USD)']; var iOther = H['Other revenue (USD)'], iEarn = H['Earnings (USD)']; var iKh = H['Khaled (USD)'], iSa = H['Sarah (USD)'], iSub = H['Subscription (USD)']; // cost lines = the category columns strictly between Revenue and Costs-total (EGP), plus Subscription (USD) var costCols = []; for (var c = iRev + 1; c < iCostsTotal; c++) costCols.push({ i: c, label: pnlTab.header[c], key: slug(pnlTab.header[c]), currency: 'EGP' }); if (iSub != null) costCols.push({ i: iSub, label: 'Subscription (USD)', key: 'subscription', currency: 'USD' }); var pnl = {}, portfolio = {}, currentMonth = null; pnlTab.rows.forEach(function (row) { var flat = str(row[iProp]); var month = monthKey(row[iMonth]); if (!flat || !month || !flatsById[flat]) return; // skip blanks / non-flat rows var status = statusByMonth[month] || 'open'; if (status === 'open') currentMonth = maxMonth(currentMonth, month); var costs = costCols.map(function (cc) { return { key: cc.key, label: cc.label, amount: round2(row[cc.i]), currency: cc.currency }; }); var rec = { status: status, revenue: { total: round2(row[iRev]), other: round2(row[iOther]), earnings: round2(row[iEarn]) }, costs: costs, net: round2(row[iNet]), attribution: { khaled: round2(row[iKh]), sarah: round2(row[iSa]) } }; // ---- partner scoping: only their flat, hide internal split + rent, add fee/payout, force in_progress until final if (viewRole === 'partner') { if (flat !== readFlat) return; rec.attribution = null; /* Drop the lines we carry ourselves. Hiding them is NOT enough: the sheet's Net already has them deducted, so each one must be added back or the partner's own arithmetic (Revenue − Costs − Fee = Net) stops working on their statement. The month's EGP/USD rate is recovered from the sheet's own figures — Net is the sheet's conversion of the EGP costs, so the ratio gives the rate back. Nothing hardcoded. */ var egpTot = 0, usdTot = 0; rec.costs.forEach(function (c) { if (!c.amount) return; if (c.currency === 'EGP') egpTot += c.amount; else usdTot += c.amount; }); var allCostsUsd = rec.revenue.total - rec.net, egpAsUsd = allCostsUsd - usdTot; var rate = (egpTot > 0 && egpAsUsd > 0.01) ? egpTot / egpAsUsd : 0; var kept = [], addBack = 0; rec.costs.forEach(function (c) { if (PARTNER_NOT_BORNE.indexOf(c.key) < 0) { kept.push(c); return; } if (!c.amount) return; addBack += (c.currency === 'EGP') ? (rate ? c.amount / rate : 0) : c.amount; }); rec.costs = kept; rec.net = round2(rec.net + addBack); // the demo charges the DEMO row's terms — the source flat's own config is never touched or read var cfg = wantDemo ? demoFlat : flatsById[flat]; var sharePct = sharePct_(cfg && cfg.partner && cfg.partner.sharePctOfTotalRev); var fee = round2(rec.revenue.total * sharePct); rec.partnerView = { managementFeePct: sharePct, managementFee: fee, netPayout: round2(rec.net - fee) }; if (status !== 'final') rec.status = 'in_progress'; } var outFlat = (wantDemo && flat === readFlat) ? demoId : flat; (pnl[outFlat] = pnl[outFlat] || {})[month] = rec; // ---- portfolio aggregation (admin only; a demo response carries no portfolio) if (who.role === 'admin' && !wantDemo) { var pf = portfolio[month] = portfolio[month] || { revenueTotal: 0, netTotal: 0, costTotals: {}, attribution: { khaled: 0, sarah: 0 } }; pf.revenueTotal = round2(pf.revenueTotal + rec.revenue.total); pf.netTotal = round2(pf.netTotal + rec.net); pf.attribution.khaled = round2(pf.attribution.khaled + (row[iKh] || 0)); pf.attribution.sarah = round2(pf.attribution.sarah + (row[iSa] || 0)); costs.forEach(function (c) { pf.costTotals[c.key] = round2((pf.costTotals[c.key] || 0) + (c.amount || 0)); }); } }); // ---- a demo response contains the demo flat and nothing else if (wantDemo) { // strip demoSource/isDemo — the borrowed flat's identity is ours, not the viewer's business flats = [{ id: demoId, name: demoFlat.name, isLongLet: demoFlat.isLongLet, onAirbnbSplits: demoFlat.onAirbnbSplits, partner: demoFlat.partner, attribution: null }]; flatsOrder = [demoId]; var srcTarget = targets.byFlat[readFlat]; targets = { daysBasis: targets.daysBasis, byFlat: srcTarget ? (function () { var b = {}; b[demoId] = srcTarget; return b; })() : {} }; } // ---- meta var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var meta = { asOf: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'), role: viewRole, currentMonth: currentMonth, fx: { current: fxCurrent, sourceMonth: fxSourceMonth, isCurrentMonthSet: !!(currentMonth && fxByMonth[currentMonth] && !fxCarried[currentMonth]), isCarried: !!(currentMonth && fxCarried[currentMonth]) }, flatsOrder: flatsOrder, smoobu: { lastSync: sp.getProperty('SMOOBU_LAST_SYNC') || null, lastResult: sp.getProperty('SMOOBU_LAST_RESULT') || null } }; /* Demo signalling. `active` tells the partner screens they are showing borrowed figures, so they can say so on screen. The admin form (flat/source/on) is what lets an admin open the preview. */ if (wantDemo) meta.demo = { active: true, flat: demoId }; // no `source` — see the note on flats above else if (who.role === 'admin' && demoId) meta.demo = { active: false, on: demoOn, flat: demoId, source: str(demoFlat.demoSource) }; __tick('pnl'); // ---- drill-down transactions (date · description · amount) for every cost line var tx = buildTx(ss); __tick('tx'); var stays = (reuse && reuse.stays) || buildStays(ss); __tick('stays'); // bookings feed (Today / This-week / occupancy) var cleans = (reuse && reuse.cleans) || buildCleans(ss); __tick('cleans'); // confirmed cleaning schedule (This-week / RULE A6) var repairs = (reuse && reuse.repairs) || buildRepairs(ss); __tick('repairs'); // open + recent repair tickets (§13.4) var internet = (reuse && reuse.internet) || buildInternet(ss); __tick('internet'); // bundle renewals + quota readings (Screen 8 / §13.3) if (viewRole === 'partner') { var mine = function (o) { return o.flat === readFlat; }; var relabel = wantDemo ? asDemo : function (o) { return o; }; /* Drill-down must not carry what the statement no longer shows (see PARTNER_NOT_BORNE) … */ var drop = function (t) { return t.cat === 'rent' || t.cat === '__subscription'; }; var ownTx = tx.filter(function (t) { return mine(t) && !drop(t); }); /* … and it MUST carry this flat's share of the pooled rows, or the detail stops adding up to the line above it. Supplies bought for every apartment land on POOL_FLAT with no flat of their own: in July 2026 that was 1,817.04 EGP of them, so 17A's supplies line read 687.93 while the drill-down showed only the 291.00 that happened to be booked directly. Looked like the entries had gone missing; they were simply un-attributed. The flat's share is derived, not assumed: the P&L already charged it a total for the category, so (that total − what was booked directly) is what came out of the pool, and that over the pool gives this flat's proportion. Each pooled row is then shown at that proportion — the partner sees their slice and its description, never the pool's full value, which is portfolio data. */ var pnlKey = wantDemo ? demoId : readFlat; var pooled = tx.filter(function (t) { return t.flat === POOL_FLAT && !drop(t); }); var shareRows = []; if (pooled.length) { var agg = {}; // 'ym|cat' → { pool, own } pooled.forEach(function (t) { var k = t.ym + '|' + t.cat; (agg[k] = agg[k] || { pool: 0, own: 0 }).pool += (t.amount || 0); }); ownTx.forEach(function (t) { var k = t.ym + '|' + t.cat; if (agg[k]) agg[k].own += (t.amount || 0); }); pooled.forEach(function (t) { var a = agg[t.ym + '|' + t.cat]; if (!a || !a.pool) return; var rec2 = pnl[pnlKey] && pnl[pnlKey][t.ym]; if (!rec2) return; var key = slug(t.cat); // 'TV/Phone/Internet' → 'tv-phone-internet' var lineCol = null; (rec2.costs || []).forEach(function (c) { if (c.key === key) lineCol = c.amount; }); if (lineCol == null) return; // category the partner doesn't carry var fromPool = lineCol - a.own; if (fromPool <= 0) return; // nothing of this pool reached them var c2 = {}; for (var kk in t) c2[kk] = t[kk]; c2.flat = readFlat; c2.amount = round2((t.amount || 0) * (fromPool / a.pool)); c2.desc = str(t.desc) + ' · shared, your share'; shareRows.push(c2); }); } tx = ownTx.concat(shareRows).map(relabel); stays = stays.filter(mine).map(relabel); cleans = cleans.filter(mine).map(relabel); repairs = repairs.filter(mine).map(relabel); internet = { bundles: internet.bundles.filter(mine).map(relabel), readings: internet.readings.filter(mine).map(relabel) }; } var paymentDefaults = (reuse && reuse.defaults) ? reuse.defaults.paymentDefaults : buildPaymentDefaults(ss); // editable fallback account per flat×channel var channelAliases = (reuse && reuse.defaults) ? reuse.defaults.channelAliases : buildChannelAliases(ss); // editable channel grouping + revenue-inclusion (test bookings excluded) __tick('defaults'); var out = { meta: meta, flats: flats, targets: targets, pnl: pnl, tx: tx, stays: stays, cleans: cleans, repairs: repairs, internet: internet, paymentDefaults: paymentDefaults, channelAliases: channelAliases }; if (who.role === 'admin') out.repairTrades = (reuse && reuse.defaults) ? reuse.defaults.repairTrades : repairTrades_(ss); if (who.role === 'admin' && !wantDemo) out.portfolio = portfolio; if (viewRole === 'partner') { var only = wantDemo ? demoId : who.flat; out.flats = flats.filter(function (f) { return f.id === only; }); } return out; } /* ---- demo mode ---------------------------------------------------- One switch, held in Script Properties so it flips from the sheet menu with no code edit and no redeploy. OFF is the default and the safe state: the demo flat then returns nothing at all, even to a partner who has a valid login for it. Nothing here touches a real flat's data or config — the demo borrows figures at read time and writes nothing, ever. */ function demoIsOn_() { return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('DEMO_MODE') === 'on'; } function demoFlatName_() { try { var t = readTab(SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), TAB.config); for (var i = 0; i < t.rows.length; i++) { var g = colGetter(t.idx, t.rows[i]); if (truthy(g('isDemo'))) return { id: str(g('Property')), source: str(g('demoSource')) }; } } catch (e) {} return null; } function demoModeOn() { hubBump_(['base']); // meta.demo lives in the admin payload var d = demoFlatName_(); var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); if (!d) { ui.alert('No demo flat is set up.\n\nAdd a row to Config with a name (e.g. "Demo"), tick isDemo, and put the flat to borrow from in demoSource (e.g. 17A).'); return; } PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('DEMO_MODE', 'on'); ui.alert('Demo mode is ON ✓\n\n"' + d.id + '" now mirrors ' + (d.source || '(no source set!)') + '\'s figures under its own partner terms.\n\n' + 'It stays out of every one of your screens — Today, This week, Close, Stats, Repairs and the ' + 'portfolio totals are all unchanged.\n\nTurn it off with "Demo mode — turn OFF".'); } function demoModeOff() { hubBump_(['base']); // meta.demo lives in the admin payload var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var had = sp.getProperty('DEMO_MODE') === 'on'; sp.deleteProperty('DEMO_MODE'); SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(had ? 'Demo mode is OFF ✓\n\nThe demo flat returns nothing from now on, including to anyone holding a demo login.' : 'Demo mode was already off.'); } function demoModeStatus() { var d = demoFlatName_(); SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert( 'Demo mode: ' + (demoIsOn_() ? 'ON' : 'OFF') + '\n\n' + (d ? 'Demo flat: ' + d.id + '\nBorrows from: ' + (d.source || '(not set)') : 'No demo flat configured — add a Config row with isDemo ticked.')); } // ---- sheet helpers ------------------------------------------------ function readTab(ss, name) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sh) throw new Error('missing tab: ' + name); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var header = v.length ? v[0].map(function (x) { return str(x); }) : []; var idx = {}; header.forEach(function (h, i) { if (h) idx[h] = i; }); return { header: header, idx: idx, rows: v.slice(1) }; } function readTabWithHeader(ss, name) { return readTab(ss, name); } function colGetter(idx, row) { return function (header) { var i = idx[header]; return i == null ? '' : row[i]; }; } function index(arr, key) { var m = {}; arr.forEach(function (o) { m[o[key]] = o; }); return m; } // ---- value helpers ------------------------------------------------ function str(x) { return (x == null ? '' : x).toString().trim(); } function num(x) { var n = parseFloat(x); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; } function numOrNull(x) { if (x === '' || x == null) return null; var n = parseFloat(x); return isNaN(n) ? null : n; } function round2(x){ var n = parseFloat(x); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : Math.round(n * 100) / 100; } function pctToNum(x){ var n = parseFloat(x); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; } // 0.2 stays 0.2 /* ownerSharePct read tolerantly: a percent-formatted cell gives 0.25, but someone typing a bare 25 means 25%, not 2500%. A share above 1 is never meaningful, so treat it as per-hundred. */ function sharePct_(x){ var n = parseFloat(x); if (isNaN(n) || n <= 0) return 0; return n > 1 ? n / 100 : n; } function truthy(x){ var s = str(x).toLowerCase(); return s === 'true' || s === 'yes' || s === '1'; } function slug(s) { return str(s).toLowerCase().replace(/\(egp\)|\(usd\)/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ''); } /* PERF 2026-08-04: Utilities.formatDate is a ~1-3ms Java-bridge call, and monthKey/dateStr run once or twice PER ROW over the tx/stays tabs — thousands of calls made the payload rebuild slow (profiled: tx 3.6-9s, stays 1.9s). Script-TZ date components give the same calendar day for our noon/midnight-anchored date cells (the LA↔Cairo offset is under the anchor's margin), so the pure-JS path below is equivalent and ~100× faster. */ function monthKey(x) { if (x == null || x === '') return null; if (Object.prototype.toString.call(x) === '[object Date]') return x.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (x.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); var s = x.toString().trim(); return /^\d{4}-\d{2}/.test(s) ? s.slice(0, 7) : null; } function maxMonth(a, b) { return !a ? b : (b > a ? b : a); } function dateStr(x, tz) { if (x == null || x === '') return ''; if (Object.prototype.toString.call(x) === '[object Date]') // pure-JS: see the PERF note on monthKey return x.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (x.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + x.getDate()).slice(-2); var s = x.toString(); return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s) ? s.slice(0, 10) : s; } // Times ("12:00") must be stored as text; if a legacy cell coerced to a time serial, recover HH:mm. var __SSTZ = null; // the spreadsheet-tz lookup is a service call — fetch once per execution function timeStr(x) { if (x == null || x === '') return ''; if (Object.prototype.toString.call(x) === '[object Date]') { if (!__SSTZ) __SSTZ = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSpreadsheetTimeZone(); return Utilities.formatDate(x, __SSTZ, 'HH:mm'); } return x.toString().trim(); } /** * Every cost entry behind the P&L, one flat/month/category at a time. * cat matches the Cost-line-items category string; electricity + subscription * come from their own tabs and use synthetic cats '__electricity' / '__subscription'. * Source tabs are read by fixed column position (stable structure). */ function buildTx(ss) { var tz = TZ, tx = []; // Cost line items: 0 Property · 1 Date · 2 Category · 3 Description · 4 Amount (EGP) · 5 Source var cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (cli) { var v = cli.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var r = v[i], flat = str(r[0]), ym = monthKey(r[1]); if (!flat || !ym) continue; tx.push({ flat: flat, ym: ym, cat: str(r[2]), date: dateStr(r[1], tz), desc: str(r[3]), amount: round2(r[4]), currency: 'EGP' }); } } // Electricity detail: 0 Property · 1 CoveredMonth · 5 PerMonth (EGP) · 6 Source var ed = ss.getSheetByName('Electricity detail'); if (ed) { var e = ed.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var j = 1; j < e.length; j++) { var er = e[j], f2 = str(er[0]), y2 = monthKey(er[1]); if (!f2 || !y2) continue; tx.push({ flat: f2, ym: y2, cat: '__electricity', date: y2, desc: str(er[6]), amount: round2(er[5]), currency: 'EGP' }); } } // Direct USD costs: 0 Property · 1 Month · 3 Note · 4 Amount (USD) var du = ss.getSheetByName('Direct USD costs'); if (du) { var u = du.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var k = 1; k < u.length; k++) { var ur = u[k], f3 = str(ur[0]), y3 = monthKey(ur[1]); if (!f3 || !y3) continue; tx.push({ flat: f3, ym: y3, cat: '__subscription', date: y3, desc: str(ur[3]), amount: round2(ur[4]), currency: 'USD' }); } } return tx; } // Bookings from the Stays tab (Smoobu-fed). Columns by position: // 0 Stay ID · 1 Property · 2 Portal · 4 Status · 6 Check-in · 7 Check-out · 8 Nights · 14 Guest · 29 All-in (USD) // Confirmed cleaning schedule (This-week screen writes it; RULE A6 cleaning cost reads it). // Tab 'Cleaning schedule': Week | Date | Flat | Type | Time | Door code | Guest in | Stay ID | Confirmed at | By function buildCleans(ss) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Cleaning schedule'); if (!sh) return []; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), tz = TZ, out = []; var cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 86400000); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var r = v[i]; if (!str(r[1])) continue; var d = r[1] instanceof Date ? r[1] : new Date(str(r[1]) + 'T12:00:00'); if (d < cutoff) continue; out.push({ week: dateStr(r[0], tz), date: dateStr(r[1], tz), flat: str(r[2]), type: str(r[3]), time: timeStr(r[4]), code: str(r[5]), inTime: timeStr(r[6]), stayId: str(r[7]), confirmedAt: r[8] instanceof Date ? r[8].toISOString() : str(r[8]), by: str(r[9]), codeEdited: str(r[10]) === 'yes', cleaner: str(r[11]) || 'amal' }); } return out; } // Replace one operational week's confirmed cleans (delete that week's rows, append the new set). function confirmCleans(week, cleansJson, who) { if (!week) throw new Error('week required'); var cleans = JSON.parse(cleansJson || '[]'); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Cleaning schedule'); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet('Cleaning schedule'); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 11).setValues([['Week', 'Date', 'Flat', 'Type', 'Time', 'Door code', 'Guest in', 'Stay ID', 'Confirmed at', 'By', 'Code edited']]); } /* 'Code edited' (K, §5.5): door codes are normally re-derived from bookings on every load, healing stale values — but a HAND-SET code must survive that healing, and without this flag a manual edit is indistinguishable from a stale derivation. */ if (!str(sh.getRange(1, 11).getValue())) sh.getRange(1, 11).setValue('Code edited'); if (!str(sh.getRange(1, 12).getValue())) sh.getRange(1, 12).setValue('Cleaner'); // amal | jinny | both (2026-08-03) /* Mid-week re-confirm (Sarah 2026-08-03): elapsed days are HISTORY — they already happened, they feed the per-visit cost and the سجل, so they stay untouched. Only today-forward is replaced. */ /* Replace by DATE, not by the Week label (Sarah 2026-08-13). The week start moved Saturday → Friday, so the same day now belongs to a different label: matching on the label left the old row in place and appended a second one for the same clean, and the screen — which reads the label too — showed neither. The day is the fact; the label is just how it was filed. */ var todayK = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var wkEndK = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(new Date(week + 'T12:00:00').getTime() + 6 * 86400000), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = v.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) { var dk = dateStr(v[i][1], TZ); if (dk >= week && dk <= wkEndK && dk >= todayK) sh.deleteRow(i + 1); } /* Elapsed days are history and stay untouched — but say so out loud rather than dropping the lines in silence, or the confirm reports a count the screen cannot explain. */ var skipped = cleans.filter(function (c) { return str(c.date) < todayK; }) .map(function (c) { return str(c.date) + ' ' + str(c.flat); }); cleans = cleans.filter(function (c) { return str(c.date) >= todayK; }); var now = new Date(); if (cleans.length) { var start = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(start, 1, cleans.length, 8).setNumberFormat('@'); // Week..Stay ID as TEXT so time/date never coerce var rows = cleans.map(function (c) { return [week, str(c.date), str(c.flat), str(c.type), str(c.time), str(c.code), str(c.inTime), str(c.stayId), now, str(who || 'app'), c.codeEdited ? 'yes' : '', str(c.cleaner || 'amal')]; }); sh.getRange(start, 1, rows.length, 12).setValues(rows); } SpreadsheetApp.flush(); try { PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty('WEEK_DRAFT_' + week); } catch (ed) {} // the confirm supersedes the draft var jny = jinnySyncWeek_(week, cleans, who); // جيني sessions → WeShare pending rows (payer Sarah) // Emad, instantly: stamp his app fresh + a Telegram with the size of his week dcTouch_(); var jinnyN = cleans.filter(function (c) { return c.cleaner === 'jinny' || c.cleaner === 'both'; }).length; var amalN = cleans.filter(function (c) { return (c.cleaner || 'amal') !== 'jinny'; }).length; /* No جيني telegram (Sarah 2026-08-04): the pending rows surface in WeShare's confirmation list exactly like dry-clean submissions — that's where they get confirmed.
