The confirm RESULT still carries the details, so the Week screen shows what was booked/removed/locked on the spot. */ tgHubNotify_('📅 اتأكد جدول النظافة لأسبوع ' + week + ' — ' + cleans.length + ' مهمة (أمل ' + amalN + ' · جيني ' + jinnyN + ').\nالتفاصيل في الكنترول.'); // plural audience — no gendered imperative return { week: week, count: cleans.length, skipped: skipped, jinny: jny }; } /* جيني في WeShare (Sarah 2026-08-03): one pending cleaning-labor row per confirmed جيني session, keyed JNY|, Apartment = the flat (WeShare then mirrors it to that flat's P&L cleaning line), payer default SARAH, amount = the most recent CONFIRMED jinny price (her price drifts — 650/775 — so the default self-updates; seeded 650). Reconciliation on every re-confirm: new session → append · dropped session with a PENDING row → row deleted · dropped session whose row is already CONFIRMED → never touched, reported in the Telegram instead (never delete settled money). Scope: this week's dates, today-forward only. Amal needs no row here — her salary is the existing WeShare cleaning-labor entry, allocated by dry-clean share, so جيني sessions never dilute her cost (decision A, 2026-08-03). */ /* HEAL (Sarah 2026-08-08): pending rows used to be born with their split already in K/L/M, and the GroupBalances formulas sum those columns blind — so unconfirmed money sat in the group from the moment a sync ran, and a price edited before confirm left the old OWH share behind (her Jinny 650/775 duplicate). Splits are now written ONLY at confirm; this zeroes the K/L/M of every row still pending so the balances go clean immediately. Safe to re-run; touches nothing confirmed. */ function wsPendingHealPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var wsh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(WESHARE_ID).getSheetByName('Expenses'); if (!wsh) { ui.alert('No Expenses tab.'); return; } var v = wsh.getDataRange().getValues(), hits = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (str(v[i][15]).toLowerCase() !== 'pending') continue; if (!num(v[i][10]) && !num(v[i][11]) && !num(v[i][12])) continue; // already clean hits.push({ row: i + 1, id: str(v[i][0]), desc: str(v[i][3]), amt: num(v[i][5]), s: num(v[i][10]), k: num(v[i][11]), o: num(v[i][12]) }); } if (!hits.length) { ui.alert('WeShare pending heal\n\nEvery pending row already has a zero split — nothing to do.'); return; } var lines = hits.map(function (h) { return ' ' + h.desc + ' (' + h.amt + ') S:' + h.s + ' K:' + h.k + ' OWH:' + h.o; }); var ok = ui.alert('WeShare — clear splits on pending rows', hits.length + ' pending row(s) carry a live split, so they are counted in the group before being confirmed:\n\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n\nZero their S/K/OWH columns? The split is written when you confirm, as it should be.', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } hits.forEach(function (h) { wsh.getRange(h.row, 11, 1, 3).setValues([[0, 0, 0]]); }); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + hits.length + ' pending row(s) cleaned — the group balances no longer include them.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Heal failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } function jinnySyncWeek_(week, cleans, who) { var res = { created: [], removed: [], locked: [], price: 650 }; var want = {}; cleans.forEach(function (c) { if (c.cleaner === 'jinny' || c.cleaner === 'both') want['JNY' + str(c.date) + '|' + str(c.flat)] = { date: str(c.date), flat: str(c.flat) }; }); var wsh; try { wsh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(WESHARE_ID).getSheetByName('Expenses'); } catch (ej) { return res; } if (!wsh) return res; var ev = wsh.getDataRange().getValues(); var lastD = '', manualDone = {}; for (var ji = 1; ji < ev.length; ji++) { var jid = str(ev[ji][0]), jConf = str(ev[ji][15]).toLowerCase() === 'confirmed'; /* A Jinny session confirmed OUTSIDE the pending card gets a random id the JNY dedup cannot see — Sarah confirmed 06-08 at 775 as a fresh row, and the sync faithfully re-created the 650 pending twin (2026-08-08). Any confirmed cleaning-labor row whose description names جيني now counts as that day+flat being done, whatever its id. */ if (jConf && str(ev[ji][4]) === 'cleaning-labor' && str(ev[ji][3]).indexOf('جيني') > -1) { var mdd = dateStr(ev[ji][2], TZ) || str(ev[ji][2]).slice(0, 10); var mfl = str(ev[ji][14]); if (mdd && mfl) manualDone['JNY' + mdd + '|' + mfl] = true; } if (jid.slice(0, 3) !== 'JNY') continue; if (jConf) { var jdd = jid.slice(3).split('|')[0]; if (jdd > lastD) { lastD = jdd; res.price = Number(ev[ji][5]) || res.price; } } } Object.keys(manualDone).forEach(function (k) { if (want[k]) { delete want[k]; res.locked.push(k.slice(3).replace('|', ' ') + ' (confirmed manually)'); } }); var todayJ = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var wkEnd = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(new Date(week + 'T12:00:00').getTime() + 6 * 86400000), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); for (var r = ev.length - 1; r >= 1; r--) { var rid = str(ev[r][0]); if (rid.slice(0, 3) !== 'JNY') continue; var rdate = rid.slice(3).split('|')[0]; // the date lives in the KEY — immune to cell coercion if (rdate < week || rdate > wkEnd || rdate < todayJ) continue; // outside this confirm's scope if (want[rid]) { delete want[rid]; continue; } // still scheduled — keep the row if (str(ev[r][15]).toLowerCase() === 'pending') { wsh.deleteRow(r + 1); res.removed.push(rid.slice(3).replace('|', ' ')); } else res.locked.push(rid.slice(3).replace('|', ' ')); } Object.keys(want).forEach(function (wid) { var c = want[wid], rr = wsh.getLastRow() + 1; wsh.getRange(rr, 1, 1, 17).setValues([[wid, 'Airbnb Monthly', c.date, 'نظافة جيني — ' + c.flat, 'cleaning-labor', res.price, 'EGP', 'expense', 'auto: jinny session (week ' + week + ') confirmed by ' + str(who || ''), '', 0, 0, 0, 'system', c.flat, 'pending', 'jinny']]); // split written at CONFIRM, never at birth (Sarah 2026-08-08: pending must not touch the group) res.created.push('• ' + c.date + ' — ' + c.flat); }); return res; } // ============================================================ // DRY CLEAN → Cost line items (laundry) sync. // Reads the Dry Clean Log sheet directly (openById — script runs as owner; no HTTP endpoint). // Appends ONE "laundry" row per NEW submission into 'Cost line items': // [Property, Date, "laundry", "Dry-clean submission (app log) #", cost EGP, "Dry clean log"] // The workbook's existing formulas then recompute laundry cost + supplies allocation automatically. // Dedup: (a) skip any submission whose ID is already embedded in a laundry row description; (b) a FLOOR // date = the latest date among the pre-existing (ID-less snapshot) laundry rows — only submissions AFTER // the floor are eligible, so the 154 already-imported rows can never double-count. // Inclusion: submissions with status = 'returned' (the laundry is back = the cost is real/incurred). // dryRun=true → compute + report only, write nothing. var DRYCLEAN_ID = '18053VBWoCoEqkBGJfUP2nB1nX3IGRBQ3qEDiR2sf35U'; function normApt_(a) { a = str(a); if (/zayed/i.test(a)) return 'Zayed 2000'; return a; } // DC timestamps are mixed: some cells are real Dates, some are spreadsheet SERIAL numbers. Return yyyy-MM-dd. function parseDCymd_(ts) { if (ts == null || ts === '') return ''; if (Object.prototype.toString.call(ts) === '[object Date]') return Utilities.formatDate(ts, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); if (typeof ts === 'number' && isFinite(ts)) // serial (days since 1899-12-30) return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Math.round((ts - 25569) * 86400000)), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var s = String(ts).trim(); if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(s)) // numeric string serial return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Math.round((parseFloat(s) - 25569) * 86400000)), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var d = new Date(s); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? '' : Utilities.formatDate(d, TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } function syncDryClean(dryRun) { var props = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var cli = props.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (!cli) return { ok: false, version: VERSION, error: 'no Cost line items tab' }; // 1) scan existing laundry rows → known submission IDs + floor date (max date of ID-less rows) var have = {}, floor = ''; var cv = cli.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < cv.length; i++) { if (str(cv[i][2]).toLowerCase() !== 'laundry') continue; var desc = str(cv[i][3]), mm = desc.match(/#([A-Za-z0-9]+)\s*$/); if (mm) { have[mm[1]] = 1; } else { var ds = dateStr(cv[i][1], TZ); if (ds && ds > floor) floor = ds; } // snapshot rows set the boundary } // 2) read the Dry Clean Log var dc = SpreadsheetApp.openById(DRYCLEAN_ID); var subs = dc.getSheetByName('Submissions').getDataRange().getValues(); var items = dc.getSheetByName('Items').getDataRange().getValues(); // cost per submission = SUM(Items.Total) grouped by Submission ID (col B=1, Total col H=7) var costBySub = {}; for (var j = 1; j < items.length; j++) { var sid = str(items[j][1]); if (!sid) continue; costBySub[sid] = (costBySub[sid] || 0) + (parseFloat(items[j][7]) || 0); } var flatSet = { '8A': 1, '17A': 1, '28A': 1, 'Zayed 2000': 1 }; var toAdd = [], rep = { floor: floor, subsTotal: subs.length - 1, returned: 0, paid: 0, skipHave: 0, skipFloor: 0, skipNotReturned: 0, unmappedApt: {}, zeroCost: 0, byFlat: {}, dateMin: '', dateMax: '', costTotal: 0 }; for (var k = 1; k < subs.length; k++) { var r = subs[k], sid = str(r[0]); if (!sid) continue; var apt = normApt_(r[1]); var ymd = parseDCymd_(r[2]); if (!ymd) continue; var paid = str(r[3]).toLowerCase() === 'paid'; var returned = str(r[5]).toLowerCase() === 'returned'; if (returned) rep.returned++; if (paid) rep.paid++; if (!returned) { rep.skipNotReturned++; continue; } if (floor && ymd <= floor) { rep.skipFloor++; continue; } if (have[sid]) { rep.skipHave++; continue; } if (!flatSet[apt]) { rep.unmappedApt[apt] = (rep.unmappedApt[apt] || 0) + 1; continue; } var cost = Math.round((costBySub[sid] || 0) * 100) / 100; if (cost <= 0) rep.zeroCost++; rep.byFlat[apt] = (rep.byFlat[apt] || 0) + 1; rep.costTotal = Math.round((rep.costTotal + cost) * 100) / 100; if (!rep.dateMin || ymd < rep.dateMin) rep.dateMin = ymd; if (!rep.dateMax || ymd > rep.dateMax) rep.dateMax = ymd; toAdd.push([apt, ymd, 'laundry', 'Dry-clean submission (app log) #' + sid, cost, 'Dry clean log']); } if (!dryRun && toAdd.length) { var start = cli.getLastRow() + 1; cli.getRange(start, 1, toAdd.length, 6).setValues(toAdd); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } return { ok: true, version: VERSION, dryRun: !!dryRun, wouldAdd: toAdd.length, added: dryRun ? 0 : toAdd.length, sample: toAdd.slice(0, 8), report: rep }; } // ============================================================ // PHASE 3 auto-source: Dry Clean PAID submissions → WeShare pending "laundry" rows. // Redirects the dry-clean flow: DC paid → WeShare (pending, confirm on home) → syncWeShare → P&L. // Dedup: WeShare row ID = "DC"; skip if it already exists. Floor 2026-07-18 (snapshot boundary — // earlier laundry is already in Cost line items). Default payer Khaled, OWH bears 100%. // WeShare Expenses cols (17): A id·B group·C date·D desc·E cat·F amt·G cur·H type·I notes·J invoice·K Sarah·L Khaled·M OWH·N loggedBy·O Apartment·P Confirmed·Q Source function syncDryCleanWS(dryRun) { var floor = '2026-07-18'; // split at confirm; note carries the default payer } // cutover: the old direct dry-clean→Cost-line-items rows (Source="Dry clean log") now flow via WeShare instead. var cli = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName('Cost line items'); } return { ok: true, version: VERSION, dryRun: !!dryRun, floor: floor, wouldAdd: toAdd.length, added: dryRun ? 0 : toAdd.length, removedOldCliRows: dryRun ? 0 : oldRows.length, report: rep, sample: toAdd.slice(0, 10) }; } // ---- Inventory RECEIVED orders → WeShare pending "supplies". InvOrders cols (DC sheet): // 0 id·1 itemId·2 itemName·3 qtyRequested·4 qtyReceived·5 orderedBy·6 orderedAt·7 receivedBy·8 receivedAt·9 status·10 unitPrice·11 total // Dedup key "INV"; floor = cutover (earlier supplies already in the snapshot as WeShare Groceries). // Default payer = orderedBy if Sarah/Khaled else Sarah; OWH bears; Apartment "To allocate" (dc-share split). function syncInventoryWS(dryRun) { var floor = '2026-07-24'; var sh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(DRYCLEAN_ID).getSheetByName('InvOrders'); if (!sh) return { ok: false, version: VERSION, error: 'no InvOrders tab' }; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var wsSh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(WESHARE_ID).getSheetByName('Expenses'); var ev = wsSh.getDataRange().getValues(), have = {}; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { var eid = str(ev[i][0]); if (eid) have[eid] = 1; } var toAdd = [], rep = { rows: v.length - 1, received: 0, notReceived: 0, skipFloor: 0, skipHave: 0, zeroCost: 0, statusCounts: {} }; for (var r = 1; r < v.length; r++) { var row = v[r], id = str(row[0]); if (!id) continue; var status = str(row[9]).toLowerCase(); rep.statusCounts[status || '(blank)'] = (rep.statusCounts[status || '(blank)'] || 0) + 1; var received = status.indexOf('receiv') > -1 || str(row[8]) !== ''; // status "received" OR receivedAt set if (!received || status.indexOf('cancel') > -1) { rep.notReceived++; continue; } rep.received++; var ymd = parseDCymd_(str(row[6]) || row[8]); if (!ymd) continue; // ORDER date (orderedAt) so Sarah/Khaled can verify; receivedAt fallback if (ymd <= floor) { rep.skipFloor++; continue; } var wid = 'INV' + id; if (have[wid]) { rep.skipHave++; continue; } var amt = Math.round(((Number(row[11]) || (Number(row[10]) * Number(row[4]))) || 0) * 100) / 100; if (amt <= 0) { rep.zeroCost++; continue; } // unpriced order — wait until it has a cost var orderedBy = str(row[5]); // the order PLACER (Sarah or Khaled) = the payer; Emad only marks received var sarahShare = orderedBy === 'Sarah' ? amt : 0, khaledShare = orderedBy === 'Khaled' ? amt : 0; toAdd.push([wid, 'Airbnb Monthly', ymd, 'Supplies: ' + str(row[2]), 'supplies', amt, 'EGP', 'expense', 'auto: inventory order ' + id + ' (ordered by ' + orderedBy + ')' + (khaledShare > 0 ? ' · payer:Khaled' : ''), '', 0, 0, 0, 'system', 'To allocate', 'pending', 'supplies']); } if (!dryRun && toAdd.length) { var start = wsSh.getLastRow() + 1; wsSh.getRange(start, 1, toAdd.length, 17).setValues(toAdd); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } return { ok: true, version: VERSION, dryRun: !!dryRun, floor: floor, wouldAdd: toAdd.length, added: dryRun ? 0 : toAdd.length, report: rep, sample: toAdd.slice(0, 10) }; } // DC-app → WeShare job: dry-clean laundry + inventory supplies (both read the DC sheet). One trigger (2h). function dryCleanWSJob() { var out = {}; try { out.laundry = syncDryCleanWS(false); } catch (e) { out.laundry = { error: String(e && e.message || e) }; } try { out.supplies = syncInventoryWS(false); } catch (e) { out.supplies = { error: String(e && e.message || e) }; } return out; } function dryCleanWSInstallTrigger() { // arms the WeShare push; removes the OLD direct-to-CLI job ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { var h = t.getHandlerFunction(); if (h === 'dryCleanWSJob' || h === 'dryCleanSyncJob') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('dryCleanWSJob').timeBased().everyHours(2).create(); return 'installed (old dryCleanSyncJob removed)'; } // ============================================================ // WeShare (OWH Expenses) → Cost line items mirror. // Editable map tab 'WeShare category map' drives rename + treatment (import / cash-only / exclude / electricity) // + allocation (flat / dcshare / equal). Report-only for now (writes land in the next iteration) so we verify // the mapping + apartment inference against live data first — same dry-run-first discipline as the DC sync. var WESHARE_ID = '1RnoTVR0o1UMkjme3iPeC0XziA28vqTVw2Ij-arsQlbc'; function inferApt_(desc) { var d = str(desc); if (/\b8A\b/i.test(d)) return '8A'; if (/\b17B\b/i.test(d)) return '17B'; if (/\b17A\b/i.test(d)) return '17A'; if (/\b28A\b/i.test(d)) return '28A'; if (/zayed|(^|\D)401(\D|$)/i.test(d)) return 'Zayed 2000'; return 'To allocate'; } function weshareCatMap_(ss) { var name = 'WeShare category map', sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(name); var seed = [ ['WeShare category', 'Workbook category', 'Treatment', 'Allocation', 'Note'], ['rent', 'rent', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['compound maintenance', 'compound maintenance', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['Mortgage', 'compound maintenance', 'import', 'flat', 'legacy — use rent / compound maintenance going forward'], ['cleaning-labor', 'cleaning-labor', 'import', 'dcshare', ''], ['Cleaning', '', 'exclude', '', 'legacy dry-clean mislabel — laundry now comes from the Dry Clean log; a real cleaning fee → log as cleaning-labor'], ['supplies', 'supplies', 'import', 'dcshare', ''], ['Groceries', 'supplies', 'import', 'dcshare', ''], ['Household supplies', 'supplies', 'import', 'dcshare', ''], ['laundry', 'laundry', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['Maintenance', 'Maintenance', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['Electricity', 'electricity', 'electricity', 'flat', 'routes to the Electricity detail tab'], ['Water', 'Water', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['Heat/gas', 'Heat/gas', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['TV/Phone/Internet', '', 'cash', '', 'internet: bundle accrual in the Hub; the recharge is cash-only'], ['Services', 'Services', 'import', 'equal', ''], ['Gifts', 'Gifts', 'import', 'equal', ''], ['Furniture', 'contingency', 'import', 'flat', ''], ['General', 'General', 'import', 'equal', ''], ['Insurance', 'contingency', 'import', 'equal', ''], ['Payment', '', 'exclude', '', 'settlements'], ['Smoobu', '', 'cash', '', 'modeled in USD'], ['subscription', '', 'cash', '', 'modeled in USD'], ['Dining out', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Games', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Music', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Sports', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Medical expenses', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Hotel', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Gas/fuel', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Electronics', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'], ['Charity', '', 'exclude', '', 'personal'] ]; sh.getRange(1, 1, seed.length, 5).setValues(seed); } var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), map = {}; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var k = str(v[i][0]).toLowerCase(); if (!k) continue; map[k] = { wb: str(v[i][1]), treat: str(v[i][2]).toLowerCase() || 'import', alloc: str(v[i][3]).toLowerCase() }; } // §5.4: internet CASH must never import — the P&L takes the bundle price from the bundles mirror. // Self-seeding here because the map tab already exists in the live sheet (the seed above only runs on creation). if (!map['internet recharge']) { sh.appendRow(['Internet recharge', '', 'exclude', '', 'cash for internet bundles — P&L takes the BUNDLE price via the bundles mirror, never the cash (§5.4)']); map['internet recharge'] = { wb: '', treat: 'exclude', alloc: '' }; } return map; } var OWH_FLATS = ['8A', '17A', '28A', 'Zayed 2000']; // active OWH flats for equal-split (17B long-let excluded) // Cost line items rows for one importable WeShare expense. Tagged in Source col as "WeShare #" for the mirror. function wsRowsFor_(rec) { var tag = 'WeShare #' + rec.id, rows = []; /* A NAMED FLAT BEATS THE CATEGORY'S ALLOCATION (Sarah 2026-08-09, and decision A 2026-08-03 said so all along): a جيني session is cleaning-labor WITH Apartment set, and it belongs to that flat's cleaning line — but this branch tested the category's dcshare rule first, so Jinny's 28A visit was being split across every flat by dry-clean share. dcshare now applies only when the row names no flat (Amal's salary, pooled supplies), which is what dcshare is FOR. The keyed mirror self-heals on the next sync: the (portfolio) rows' signatures no longer match, so they are deleted and rewritten per-flat. */ var hasFlat = rec.apt && rec.apt !== 'To allocate' && rec.apt !== '(portfolio)'; if (rec.alloc === 'dcshare' && !hasFlat) { // pooled cleaning/supplies — workbook formula splits by DC share rows.push(['(portfolio)', rec.date, rec.wbCat, rec.desc, round2(rec.amount), tag]); } else if (rec.alloc === 'equal' || rec.apt === 'To allocate') { // equal split across active flats var per = round2(rec.amount / OWH_FLATS.length); OWH_FLATS.forEach(function (f) { rows.push([f, rec.date, rec.wbCat, rec.desc + ' (shared ÷' + OWH_FLATS.length + ')', per, tag]); }); } else { // per-flat rows.push([rec.apt, rec.date, rec.wbCat, rec.desc, round2(rec.amount), tag]); } return rows; } function syncWeShare(dryRun) { var pss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var floor = '2026-07-13'; // last captured actual date before the gap (Sarah) var map = weshareCatMap_(pss); var ev = SpreadsheetApp.openById(WESHARE_ID).getSheetByName('Expenses').getDataRange().getValues(); var hdr = ev[0].map(function (x) { return str(x).toLowerCase(); }); var iApt = hdr.indexOf('apartment'), iConf = hdr.indexOf('confirmed'); // 1) build the DESIRED set of Cost-line-items rows, keyed by WeShare id var desired = {}, plan = { import: [], cash: [], exclude: [], electricity: [] }, unmapped = {}, byWbCat = {}, importTot = 0, pending = 0; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) { var w = ev[i]; if (!w[0] || String(w[0]) === 'DELETED') continue; if (str(w[1]) !== 'Airbnb Monthly' || str(w[7]) !== 'expense') continue; var ymd = parseDCymd_(w[2]); if (!ymd || ymd <= floor) continue; if (iConf >= 0 && str(w[iConf]).toLowerCase() === 'pending') { pending++; continue; } var cat = str(w[4]), m = map[cat.toLowerCase()]; if (!m) { unmapped[cat] = (unmapped[cat] || 0) + 1; continue; } var apt = (iApt >= 0 && str(w[iApt])) ? str(w[iApt]) : inferApt_(w[3]); /* BUILDING GUARD. A building / common-area electricity charge is NOT the flat's electricity: it must never reach the electricity line, whatever it was categorised as in WeShare (historically it arrived as both "Electricity" and "Services"). Left alone it lands in 'Electricity detail' and becomes the "last actual" the accrual carries forward — a 33–170 EGP common-area fee standing in for ~4,000 EGP of consumption, which is what collapsed Zayed's accrual. Forced to compound maintenance here so the category in WeShare can't cause it again. */ if (/electric/i.test(cat + ' ' + str(w[3])) && /\bbuilding\b|common[- ]?area/i.test(str(w[3]))) { m = { wb: 'compound maintenance', treat: 'import', alloc: 'flat' }; } var rec = { id: str(w[0]), date: ymd, wsCat: cat, wbCat: m.wb, treat: m.treat, alloc: m.alloc, apt: apt, amount: Number(w[5]) || 0, desc: str(w[3]) }; if (m.treat === 'import') { plan.import.push(rec); importTot += rec.amount; byWbCat[m.wb] = (byWbCat[m.wb] || 0) + rec.amount; desired[rec.id] = wsRowsFor_(rec); } else if (m.treat === 'electricity') plan.electricity.push(rec); // routed to Electricity detail — not written here yet else if (m.treat === 'cash') plan.cash.push(rec); else plan.exclude.push(rec); } // 2) index EXISTING mirror rows (Source = "WeShare #") in Cost line items var cli = pss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'), cv = cli.getDataRange().getValues(), existing = {}; function sig(prop, dateVal, ccat, amt) { return str(prop) + '|' + dateStr(dateVal, TZ) + '|' + str(ccat) + '|' + round2(amt); } for (var r = 1; r < cv.length; r++) { var mm = str(cv[r][5]).match(/^WeShare #(.+)$/); if (mm) (existing[mm[1]] = existing[mm[1]] || []).push({ row: r + 1, sig: sig(cv[r][0], cv[r][1], cv[r][2], cv[r][4]) }); } // 3) reconcile: add new, re-write changed, delete orphaned/reverted var toAdd = [], toDelete = [], rc = { add: 0, update: 0, del: 0, unchanged: 0 }; Object.keys(existing).forEach(function (id) { // in sheet but no longer desired → delete if (!desired[id]) { existing[id].forEach(function (e) { toDelete.push(e.row); }); rc.del++; } }); Object.keys(desired).forEach(function (id) { var want = desired[id]; if (!existing[id]) { want.forEach(function (row) { toAdd.push(row); }); rc.add++; return; } var exSig = existing[id].map(function (e) { return e.sig; }).sort(); var deSig = want.map(function (row) { return sig(row[0], row[1], row[2], row[4]); }).sort(); if (JSON.stringify(exSig) === JSON.stringify(deSig)) { rc.unchanged++; } else { existing[id].forEach(function (e) { toDelete.push(e.row); }); want.forEach(function (row) { toAdd.push(row); }); rc.update++; } }); if (!dryRun) { toDelete.sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; }).forEach(function (rw) { cli.deleteRow(rw); }); if (toAdd.length) { var start = cli.getLastRow() + 1; cli.getRange(start, 1, toAdd.length, 6).setValues(toAdd); } SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } return { ok: true, version: VERSION, dryRun: !!dryRun, floor: floor, hasAptCol: iApt >= 0, hasConfirmedCol: iConf >= 0, pendingSkipped: pending, counts: { importExpenses: plan.import.length, cash: plan.cash.length, electricity: plan.electricity.length, exclude: plan.exclude.length }, reconcile: rc, rowsToAdd: toAdd.length, rowsToDelete: toDelete.length, importTotalEGP: Math.round(importTot), importByWorkbookCat: byWbCat, unmappedCategories: unmapped, electricityPending: plan.electricity.map(function (e) { return { date: e.date, apt: e.apt, amount: e.amount, desc: e.desc }; }), addSample: toAdd.slice(0, 25) }; } /* ============================================================ * CLEANING ACCRUAL — PER SUBMISSION (Sarah 2026-08-05). Amal is paid at month end, so until that * payment is logged the month's cleaning pool is 0 and the P&L overstates net. Sarah's model — the * automated form of the per-visit rows she used to write by hand: * • rate = LAST SETTLED MONTH's cost per submission (its actual total ÷ its submissions), * carried forward. A salary change needs no configuration: the first month paid at * the new figure sets the new rate for every month after it. * • accrual = ONE row per submission as it happens — same flat, same date as its laundry row, * amount = the rate. So cost grows with the work, and each flat carries exactly its * own submissions × rate (no mid-month wobble in the split). * • settlement = when the month's real payment lands (WeShare mirror), the generated rows are * removed and the actual stands. The P&L's dcshare formula then spreads the ACTUAL * over the FINAL submission count — arithmetically the same as rewriting every * submission at the true rate, which is the month-end correction Sarah asked for. * • never touches a `final` month; the whole tagged block is recomputed and rewritten each run, * never appended (the electricity double-count lesson). * "Settled" = the month has cleaning-labor rows that are NOT tagged as generated (the WeShare * mirror, or the legacy hand-written rows) — so history is read as-is and never regenerated. * ============================================================ */ var CLEAN_ACCRUAL_TAG = 'cleaning accrual'; function cleanAccrualStep_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (!cli) return { skipped: 'no Cost line items tab' }; var st = readTab(ss, TAB.status), statusByMonth = {}; st.rows.forEach(function (r) { var g = colGetter(st.idx, r), m = monthKey(g('Month')); if (m) statusByMonth[m] = str(g('Status')).toLowerCase(); }); var v = cli.getDataRange().getValues(); var actualByMonth = {}, subsByMonth = {}, subRows = {}, existing = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var cat = str(v[i][2]), m = monthKey(v[i][1]); if (!m) continue; if (cat === 'laundry') { // one laundry row = one dry-clean submission subsByMonth[m] = (subsByMonth[m] || 0) + 1; (subRows[m] = subRows[m] || []).push({ flat: str(v[i][0]), date: v[i][1] }); } else if (cat === 'cleaning-labor') { if (str(v[i][5]) === CLEAN_ACCRUAL_TAG) existing.push({ row: i + 1, m: m, flat: str(v[i][0]), amt: num(v[i][4]) }); else actualByMonth[m] = (actualByMonth[m] || 0) + num(v[i][4]); } } var settled = Object.keys(actualByMonth).filter(function (m) { return subsByMonth[m] > 0; }).sort(); if (!settled.length) return { skipped: 'no settled month yet (need one paid month with submissions) — nothing to carry forward' }; // cost per submission of the most recent SETTLED month on or before m — carry forward, never backward function rateFor(m) { var r = 0, bm = ''; settled.forEach(function (sm) { if (sm <= m && sm > bm) { bm = sm; r = actualByMonth[sm] / subsByMonth[sm]; } }); if (!r) r = actualByMonth[settled[0]] / subsByMonth[settled[0]]; // before any settled month return { rate: r, from: bm || settled[0] }; } var nowM = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'), toAdd = [], accrued = {}; var d = new Date(+settled[0].slice(0, 4), +settled[0].slice(5, 7) - 1, 1); while (true) { var mk = d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); if (mk > nowM) break; d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1); if (actualByMonth[mk] || statusByMonth[mk] === 'final') continue; // settled or closed → nothing to estimate var subs = subRows[mk] || []; if (!subs.length) continue; // no cleans logged yet → no cost incurred yet var rf = rateFor(mk), amt = round2(rf.rate); accrued[mk] = { n: subs.length, rate: amt, from: rf.from }; subs.forEach(function (s) { toAdd.push([s.flat, s.date, 'cleaning-labor', 'Cleaning (Amal) — estimate at ' + amt + ' EGP/submission, the ' + rf.from + ' rate; replaced when the month is paid', amt, CLEAN_ACCRUAL_TAG]); }); } // no-op when the sheet already says what we want — a pointless rewrite would bump the change stamp var exSig = existing.map(function (a) { return a.m + '|' + a.flat + '|' + round2(a.amt); }).sort().join(','); var deSig = toAdd.map(function (r) { return monthKey(r[1]) + '|' + r[0] + '|' + round2(r[4]); }).sort().join(','); if (exSig === deSig) return { unchanged: true, accrued: accrued }; var toDelete = existing.map(function (a) { return a.row; }); toDelete.sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; }).forEach(function (rw) { cli.deleteRow(rw); }); if (toAdd.length) cli.getRange(cli.getLastRow() + 1, 1, toAdd.length, 6).setValues(toAdd); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return { accrued: accrued, removed: toDelete.length, wrote: toAdd.length }; } /* ============================================================ * RECURRING MONTHLY ENTRIES (Sarah 2026-08-05). Rent, Emad's fee, the landline, 17B's rent income * and the Smoobu subscription are facts that recur every month — but each was typed by hand, so a * month nobody touched read as ZERO. That is why 2026-08 opened with no rent, no ops fee and no * landline, and why 17B showed a loss: its rent income simply wasn't there. * Each entry carries forward from the most recent month that has it, keeping the original formulas * (=1000, =136.8/3) so the sheet stays self-documenting. Existing rows are never overwritten, so an * edit always wins over the carry-forward. * * WHICH ONES REACH WeShare (Sarah's rule) — all through the normal pending→confirm flow: * rent → monthly, payer SARAH, OWH 100%, category 'rent' (an IMPORT category) * landline → on each line's own due date, payer KHALED, category 'TV/Phone/Internet' (cash-only) * subscription → monthly, payer SARAH, OWH 100%, category 'Smoobu' (cash-only) * opsManager → NEVER: Emad is paid as one bundled entry in the Monthly Expense group * 17B rent → NEVER: it is income, not a cost * * THE DOUBLE-COUNT GUARD. 'rent' is an import category, so a confirmed WeShare rent row becomes a * Cost line items row. Rent therefore carries forward as an ACCRUAL (Source "rent accrual") and the * accrual is DELETED as soon as the real row lands — exactly the cleaning-labor pattern. Landline * and Smoobu are cash-only categories, so their WeShare rows can never reach the P&L and the * monthly accrual stays the only source: "WeShare captures the cash, the P&L captures the accrual". * ============================================================ */ var RECUR_RENT_TAG = 'rent accrual'; var RECUR_SYNC_SRC = /^(WeShare #|NetBundle #|DC backfill #|cleaning accrual|rent accrual)/; function monthFirst_(m) { return new Date(+m.slice(0, 4), +m.slice(5, 7) - 1, 1, 12, 0, 0); } /* the newest month before `upto` that carries this kind of row, and those rows */ function recurTemplate_(vals, fmls, cat, descRe, upto) { function keep(i) { return str(vals[i][2]) === cat && !RECUR_SYNC_SRC.test(str(vals[i][5])) && (!descRe || descRe.test(str(vals[i][3]))); } var best = ''; for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { if (!keep(i)) continue; var m = monthKey(vals[i][1]); if (m && m < upto && m > best) best = m; } if (!best) return null; var rows = []; for (var j = 1; j < vals.length; j++) { if (!keep(j) || monthKey(vals[j][1]) !== best) continue; rows.push({ flat: str(vals[j][0]), desc: str(vals[j][3]), amt: fmls[j][4] || vals[j][4], val: num(vals[j][4]), src: str(vals[j][5]) }); } return { from: best, rows: rows }; } function recurringStep_(month) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); month = month || Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); var out = { month: month, added: {}, droppedAccruals: 0, weshare: [] }; out.healed = allMonthHeal_(ss); // a Date in any Month column breaks that tab silently var cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (!cli) return { skipped: 'no Cost line items' }; var rng = cli.getDataRange(), vals = rng.getValues(), fmls = rng.getFormulas(); var have = {}, rentReal = {}, rentAccrual = []; for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { var m = monthKey(vals[i][1]); if (!m) continue; if (m === month) have[str(vals[i][0]) + '|' + str(vals[i][2]) + '|' + str(vals[i][3])] = true; if (str(vals[i][2]) === 'rent') { if (/^WeShare #/.test(str(vals[i][5]))) rentReal[str(vals[i][0]) + '|' + m] = true; else if (str(vals[i][5]) === RECUR_RENT_TAG) rentAccrual.push({ row: i + 1, key: str(vals[i][0]) + '|' + m }); } } // the accrual yields to the real payment the moment it is confirmed in WeShare var drop = rentAccrual.filter(function (a) { return rentReal[a.key]; }).map(function (a) { return a.row; }); drop.sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; }).forEach(function (r) { cli.deleteRow(r); }); out.droppedAccruals = drop.length; if (drop.length) { rng = cli.getDataRange(); vals = rng.getValues(); fmls = rng.getFormulas(); } var SPECS = [{ cat: 'rent', re: null, tag: RECUR_RENT_TAG }, { cat: 'opsManager', re: null, tag: null }, { cat: 'TV/Phone/Internet', re: /landline/i, tag: null }]; // landline ONLY — syncNetBundles_ owns "bundle accrual" var toAdd = [], rentRows = []; SPECS.forEach(function (sp) { var t = recurTemplate_(vals, fmls, sp.cat, sp.re, month); if (!t) return; t.rows.forEach(function (r) { if (sp.cat === 'rent' && rentReal[r.flat + '|' + month]) return; // already paid & confirmed if (have[r.flat + '|' + sp.cat + '|' + r.desc]) return; toAdd.push([r.flat, monthFirst_(month), sp.cat, r.desc, r.amt, sp.tag || r.src]); out.added[sp.cat] = (out.added[sp.cat] || 0) + 1; if (sp.cat === 'rent') rentRows.push({ flat: r.flat, amount: r.val }); }); }); if (toAdd.length) cli.getRange(cli.getLastRow() + 1, 1, toAdd.length, 6).setValues(toAdd); out.rent17b = recurRent17b_(ss, month); out.subscription = recurSubscription_(ss, month); out.weshare = recurWeShareRows_(ss, month, rentRows, out.subscription); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return out; } /* 17B's rent income — one row per month on 'Long-term rental income', formulas rebuilt for its row */ function recurRent17b_(ss, month) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Long-term rental income'); if (!sh) return null; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), last = null, lastM = ''; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var m = monthKey(v[i][1]); if (!m) continue; if (m === month) return { exists: true, amount: num(v[i][2]) }; if (m > lastM) { lastM = m; last = v[i]; } } if (!last) return null; var r = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(r, 2).setNumberFormat('@'); // TEXT month — its own FX VLOOKUP matches the FX tab's text months sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 6).setValues([[str(last[0]), month, num(last[2]), '=IFERROR(VLOOKUP($B' + r + ",'FX rates'!$A$2:$B$201,2,FALSE()),\"\")", '=C' + r + '/D' + r, 'carried from ' + lastM + ' — change it here when the rent changes']]); return { created: true, flat: str(last[0]), amount: num(last[2]), from: lastM }; } /* Smoobu — total ÷ number of Airbnb flats (Sarah 2026-08-05), so a 6th apartment redistributes */ function recurSubscription_(ss, month) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Direct USD costs'); if (!sh) return null; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), f = sh.getDataRange().getFormulas(); var lastM = '', eur = 71, rate = 1.15, existing = 0; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (str(v[i][2]).toLowerCase() !== 'subscription') continue; var m = monthKey(v[i][1]); if (!m) continue; if (m === month) existing++; if (m > lastM) { // recover the EUR total and rate from "=(71/4)*1.15" var mm = String(f[i][4] || '').match(/=\(?\s*([\d.]+)\s*\/\s*[\d.]+\s*\)?\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)/); if (mm) { eur = parseFloat(mm[1]); rate = parseFloat(mm[2]); } lastM = m; } } var flats = recurAirbnbFlats_(ss), n = flats.length || 1; if (existing) return { exists: true, eur: eur, rate: rate, n: n, perFlatUSD: round2(eur / n * rate) }; var rows = flats.map(function (fl) { return [fl, month, 'subscription', 'Smoobu subscription, ' + eur + ' EUR/month split ' + n + ' ways, EUR/USD~' + rate, '=(' + eur + '/' + n + ')*' + rate]; }); if (rows.length) { var start = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(start, 2, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); // TEXT month (see allMonthHeal_) sh.getRange(start, 1, rows.length, 5).setValues(rows); } return { created: rows.length, eur: eur, rate: rate, n: n, perFlatUSD: round2(eur / n * rate) }; } function recurAirbnbFlats_(ss) { var cfg = readTab(ss, TAB.config), out = []; cfg.rows.forEach(function (r) { var g = colGetter(cfg.idx, r); if (truthy(g('isDemo'))) return; if (truthy(g('onAirbnbSplits'))) out.push(str(g('Property'))); }); return out; } /* The cash side, all as PENDING rows in WeShare — keyed so a re-run can never duplicate. */ function recurWeShareRows_(ss, month, rentRows, sub) { var made = []; var wsh; try { wsh = SpreadsheetApp.openById(WESHARE_ID).getSheetByName('Expenses'); } catch (e) { return made; } if (!wsh) return made; var ev = wsh.getDataRange().getValues(), have = {}; for (var i = 1; i < ev.length; i++) have[str(ev[i][0])] = true; var add = []; function row(id, date, desc, cat, amt, payer, apt, src) { var sarah = payer === 'Sarah' ? amt : 0, khaled = payer === 'Khaled' ? amt : 0; add.push([id, 'Airbnb Monthly', date, desc, cat, amt, 'EGP', 'expense', 'auto: recurring ' + src + ' — confirm the payer' + (khaled > 0 ? ' · payer:Khaled' : ''), '', 0, 0, 0, 'system', apt, 'pending', src]); made.push(desc + ' · ' + Math.round(amt) + ' EGP · ' + payer); } // rent — monthly, Sarah, OWH 100% rentRows.forEach(function (r) { var id = 'RENT' + month + '|' + r.flat; if (have[id] || !(r.amount > 0)) return; row(id, month + '-01', 'إيجار ' + r.flat + ' — ' + month, 'rent', r.amount, 'Sarah', r.flat, 'rent'); }); // Smoobu — monthly, Sarah, OWH 100%, cash-only category so it can never reach the P&L if (sub && sub.eur) { var idS = 'SUB' + month; if (!have[idS]) { var fxm = fxForMonth(ss, month) || 49; var egp = round2(sub.eur * sub.rate * fxm); row(idS, month + '-01', 'اشتراك Smoobu — ' + month, 'Smoobu', egp, 'Sarah', 'To allocate', 'smoobu'); } } // landline — only when a line's quarterly bill is actually due; Khaled by default var todayK = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); phoneBills_().forEach(function (b) { if (b.due > todayK) return; // not due yet — nothing has been paid var idP = 'PHN' + b.line + '|' + b.due; if (have[idP]) return; var apt = b.line.indexOf('+') > -1 ? 'To allocate' : b.line; row(idP, b.due, 'فاتورة تليفون ' + b.line + ' — ربع سنوي', 'TV/Phone/Internet', b.price, 'Khaled', apt, 'landline'); }); if (add.length) wsh.getRange(wsh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, add.length, 17).setValues(add); return made; } /* What the Close screen shows and edits. Amounts are read from the month's own rows, so the box always reflects the sheet rather than a duplicate store. */ function recurringRead_(month) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); month = month || Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); var out = { month: month, rent: {}, ops: null, landline: null, rent17b: null, sub: null }; var cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (cli) { var v = cli.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (monthKey(v[i][1]) !== month) continue; var cat = str(v[i][2]), src = str(v[i][5]); if (RECUR_SYNC_SRC.test(src) && src !== RECUR_RENT_TAG) continue; // synced rows are not editable here if (cat === 'rent') out.rent[str(v[i][0])] = num(v[i][4]); else if (cat === 'opsManager' && out.ops == null) out.ops = num(v[i][4]); else if (cat === 'TV/Phone/Internet' && /landline/i.test(str(v[i][3])) && out.landline == null) out.landline = num(v[i][4]); } } var lt = ss.getSheetByName('Long-term rental income'); if (lt) { var lv = lt.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var j = 1; j < lv.length; j++) if (monthKey(lv[j][1]) === month) { out.rent17b = { flat: str(lv[j][0]), amount: num(lv[j][2]) }; break; } } var du = ss.getSheetByName('Direct USD costs'); if (du) { var dv = du.getDataRange().getValues(), df = du.getDataRange().getFormulas(); for (var k = 1; k < dv.length; k++) { if (str(dv[k][2]).toLowerCase() !== 'subscription' || monthKey(dv[k][1]) !== month) continue; var mm = String(df[k][4] || '').match(/=\(?\s*([\d.]+)\s*\/\s*([\d.]+)\s*\)?\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)/); out.sub = { eur: mm ? parseFloat(mm[1]) : null, n: mm ? parseFloat(mm[2]) : null, rate: mm ? parseFloat(mm[3]) : null, perFlatUSD: round2(num(dv[k][4])) }; break; } } return out; } /* Write the month's recurring amounts. Rows are created first (by the same seed step), then the amounts are set — so an edit and a first-time seed take the identical path. */ function recurringWrite_(p) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var month = str(p.month) || Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'); recurringStep_(month); var cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'), v = cli.getDataRange().getValues(); var rent = {}; try { rent = p.rent ? JSON.parse(p.rent) : {}; } catch (e) {} for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (monthKey(v[i][1]) !== month) continue; var cat = str(v[i][2]), src = str(v[i][5]), flat = str(v[i][0]); if (RECUR_SYNC_SRC.test(src) && src !== RECUR_RENT_TAG) continue; if (cat === 'rent' && rent[flat] != null && rent[flat] !== '') cli.getRange(i + 1, 5).setValue(Number(rent[flat])); else if (cat === 'opsManager' && str(p.ops) !== '') cli.getRange(i + 1, 5).setValue(Number(p.ops)); else if (cat === 'TV/Phone/Internet' && /landline/i.test(str(v[i][3])) && str(p.landline) !== '') cli.getRange(i + 1, 5).setValue(Number(p.landline)); } if (str(p.rent17b) !== '') { var lt = ss.getSheetByName('Long-term rental income'), lv = lt.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var j = 1; j < lv.length; j++) if (monthKey(lv[j][1]) === month) { lt.getRange(j + 1, 3).setValue(Number(p.rent17b)); break; } } if (str(p.subEur) !== '' || str(p.subRate) !== '') { var du = ss.getSheetByName('Direct USD costs'), dv = du.getDataRange().getValues(), n = recurAirbnbFlats_(ss).length || 1; var cur = recurringRead_(month).sub || {}; var eur = str(p.subEur) !== '' ? Number(p.subEur) : (cur.eur || 71); var rate = str(p.subRate) !== '' ? Number(p.subRate) : (cur.rate || 1.15); for (var k = 1; k < dv.length; k++) { if (str(dv[k][2]).toLowerCase() !== 'subscription' || monthKey(dv[k][1]) !== month) continue; du.getRange(k + 1, 4).setValue('Smoobu subscription, ' + eur + ' EUR/month split ' + n + ' ways, EUR/USD~' + rate); du.getRange(k + 1, 5).setValue('=(' + eur + '/' + n + ')*' + rate); } } SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return recurringRead_(month); } function recurringPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var r = recurringStep_(); hubBump_(['base']); if (r.skipped) { ui.alert('Recurring entries\n\n' + r.skipped); return; } var lines = []; Object.keys(r.added || {}).forEach(function (k) { lines.push(' ' + k + ': ' + r.added[k] + ' rows'); }); if (r.rent17b && r.rent17b.created) lines.push(' 17B rent income: ' + Math.round(r.rent17b.amount).toLocaleString() + ' EGP (from ' + r.rent17b.from + ')'); if (r.subscription && r.subscription.created) lines.push(' Smoobu: ' + r.subscription.eur + ' EUR ÷ ' + r.subscription.n + ' flats = $' + r.subscription.perFlatUSD + ' each'); ui.alert('Recurring entries — ' + r.month + '\n\n' + (lines.length ? 'Added to the P&L:\n' + lines.join('\n') : 'Nothing to add — the month already has them') + ((r.healed || []).length ? '\n\n🔧 Month cells repaired (were dates, broke their formulas):\n ' + r.healed.join('\n ') : '') + (r.droppedAccruals ? '\n\n🔄 ' + r.droppedAccruals + ' rent accrual(s) replaced by the confirmed WeShare payment' : '') + ((r.weshare || []).length ? '\n\n💳 Waiting in WeShare to confirm:\n ' + r.weshare.join('\n ') : '\n\n💳 No new WeShare rows (already there, or nothing due)')); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Recurring failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* Sheet menu — the same step on demand, with the numbers shown. */ function cleaningAccrualPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var r = cleanAccrualStep_(); hubBump_(['base']); if (r.skipped) { ui.alert('Cleaning accrual\n\n' + r.skipped); return; } var lines = Object.keys(r.accrued || {}).sort().map(function (m) { var a = r.accrued[m]; return ' ' + m + ': ' + a.n + ' submissions × ' + a.rate + ' = ' + Math.round(a.n * a.rate).toLocaleString() + ' EGP (rate from ' + a.from + ')'; }); ui.alert('Cleaning accrual — per submission\n\n' + (r.unchanged ? 'already up to date\n\n' : ('rows removed: ' + r.removed + ' · written: ' + r.wrote + '\n\n')) + (lines.length ? 'estimated months:\n' + lines.join('\n') : 'nothing to estimate — every open month is already paid') + '\n\nEach submission carries the last paid month\'s cost per submission. When a month\'s real payment is logged, these rows disappear and the actual is spread over that month\'s final submission count.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Cleaning accrual failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* OPEN THE NEW MONTH (Sarah 2026-08-05). The Monthly P&L is a fixed grid of flat × month rows that was extended by hand — so a month with no rows is invisible EVERYWHERE (Close, Statistics, the what-changed table, the cleaning accrual), which is exactly why August didn't exist on 5 Aug. This appends the missing months by COPYING the latest month's block: copyTo carries every column's formula with its relative references intact, so the new rows compute themselves — the same thing done by hand, minus the chance of missing a column. Existing months are never touched. */ /* ⚠️ The P&L's Month cells MUST be TEXT 'yyyy-MM'. Every cost column reads DATEVALUE(B&"-01"), so a real Date there concatenates as a serial number, DATEVALUE fails, and the #VALUE! propagates through Costs total → costs read as 0 and Net silently equals Revenue. That is exactly what happened to the first auto-opened month (2026-08, found the same day). This heals any month cell that is not plain 'yyyy-MM' text; historical rows are already text, so in practice it only ever touches a freshly written row. */ function monthColHeal_(sh, col) { if (!sh) return []; var last = sh.getLastRow(); if (last < 2) return []; var v = sh.getRange(1, col, last, 1).getValues(), fixed = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var cur = v[i][0]; if (cur === '' || cur == null) continue; if (typeof cur === 'string' && /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(cur.trim())) continue; // already correct var m = monthKey(cur); if (!m) continue; var cell = sh.getRange(i + 1, col); cell.setNumberFormat('@'); // '@' first, or Sheets re-parses it into a date cell.setValue(m); fixed.push(sh.getName() + ' row ' + (i + 1) + ' → ' + m); } if (fixed.length) SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return fixed; } /* Every tab whose Month column is matched by an exact-equality formula. A Date here breaks things silently and differently in each tab (2026-08-05): the P&L's cost columns go #VALUE!, and 'Long-term rental income' loses its FX VLOOKUP so 17B's whole row errors to zero. */ function allMonthHeal_(ss) { ss = ss || SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); return monthColHeal_(ss.getSheetByName(TAB.pnl), 2) .concat(monthColHeal_(ss.getSheetByName('Long-term rental income'), 2)) .concat(monthColHeal_(ss.getSheetByName('Direct USD costs'), 2)) .concat(monthColHeal_(ss.getSheetByName(TAB.fx), 1)); } function pnlMonthHeal_(sh) { return monthColHeal_(sh, 2); } /* ============================================================ * DRY-CLEAN: BACKFILL LAUNDRY THE HISTORICAL IMPORT MISSED (Sarah 2026-08-05). * May 2026 read 1,050 EGP against الكنترول's 3,135, and it looked like "Emad stopped logging in * May/June". He hadn't. The dry-clean book stores submission timestamps in TWO shapes — text * '2026-05-03' and spreadsheet SERIAL numbers (46154 = 12 May) — and whatever built the pre-cutover * snapshot could only read the text ones. Every serial-dated submission was silently skipped: its * laundry cost never reached the P&L, and it was missing from the submission counts that drive BOTH * the supplies allocation (RULE A5) and the cleaning-labor dcshare split. * This reconciles the two books directly: every PAID submission dated before the DC→WeShare floor * is matched against the laundry rows already in Cost line items on flat|date|amount (the snapshot * rows carry no submission id, so there is nothing else to match on), and only the unmatched ones * are written. Each existing row is consumed once, so duplicates are impossible; rows are tagged * "DC backfill #" and skipped on any later run. Submissions from the floor onward are left to * the WeShare pipeline — writing those would double-count a row still pending confirmation. * The affected months are FINAL — this restates them deliberately, on Sarah's decision. * ============================================================ */ function dcBackfillLaundryPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var FLOOR = '2026-07-18'; // from here the DC→WeShare sync owns it var dc = SpreadsheetApp.openById(DRYCLEAN_ID); var subSh = dc.getSheetByName('Submissions'), itSh = dc.getSheetByName('Items'); if (!subSh || !itSh) { ui.alert('Could not open the dry-clean Submissions/Items tabs.'); return; } var sv = subSh.getDataRange().getValues(), iv = itSh.getDataRange().getValues(); var total = {}, subApt = {}; for (var i = 1; i < iv.length; i++) { var sid = str(iv[i][1]); if (!sid) continue; total[sid] = (total[sid] || 0) + (Number(iv[i][7]) || 0); } for (var q = 1; q < sv.length; q++) { var qid = str(sv[q][0]); if (qid) subApt[qid] = normApt_(str(sv[q][1])); } var cli = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName('Cost line items'); /* HEAL first (2026-08-05): a row this tool wrote with a blank flat is attributed to NO flat — its cost vanishes from every apartment while still counting in the dcshare denominator, so part of that month's cleaning + supplies is allocated to nobody. Once the submission gets its apartment in the dry-clean book, re-running this repoints the row. */ var cv0 = cli.getDataRange().getValues(), healed = []; for (var h = 1; h < cv0.length; h++) { var hm = str(cv0[h][5]).match(/^DC backfill #(.+)$/); if (!hm) continue; var want = subApt[hm[1]]; if (want && normApt_(str(cv0[h][0])) !== want) { cli.getRange(h + 1, 1).setValue(want); healed.push(hm[1] + ' → ' + want); } } if (healed.length) SpreadsheetApp.flush(); // what the P&L already holds, as a consumable multiset keyed flat|date|amount var cv = cli.getDataRange().getValues(), pool = {}, tagged = {}; for (var c = 1; c < cv.length; c++) { if (str(cv[c][2]) !== 'laundry') continue; var k = normApt_(str(cv[c][0])) + '|' + dateStr(cv[c][1], TZ) + '|' + round2(cv[c][4]); pool[k] = (pool[k] || 0) + 1; var mm = str(cv[c][5]).match(/^DC backfill #(.+)$/); if (mm) tagged[mm[1]] = true; } var missing = [], noApt = []; for (var r = 1; r < sv.length; r++) { var id = str(sv[r][0]); if (!id || tagged[id]) continue; if (str(sv[r][3]).toLowerCase().indexOf('paid') < 0) continue; // RULE B5 — paid only var ymd = parseDCymd_(sv[r][2]); if (!ymd || ymd >= FLOOR) continue; var amt = round2(total[id] || 0); if (amt <= 0) continue; var apt = normApt_(str(sv[r][1])); /* NEVER write a row with no apartment (the ec4bac6e lesson): the P&L filters laundry by flat, so it would be invisible to every apartment yet still inflate the submission count. */ if (!apt) { noApt.push({ id: id, ymd: ymd, amount: amt }); continue; } var key = apt + '|' + ymd + '|' + amt; if (pool[key] > 0) { pool[key]--; continue; } // already in the P&L missing.push({ id: id, apt: apt, ymd: ymd, amount: amt }); } var aptWarn = noApt.length ? ('\n\n⚠️ ' + noApt.length + ' paid submission(s) have NO apartment and were skipped — fix the Apartment in the dry-clean book, then run this again:\n' + noApt.map(function (x) { return ' ' + x.ymd + ' · ' + x.id + ' · ' + Math.round(x.amount) + ' EGP'; }).join('\n')) : ''; if (!missing.length) { if (healed.length) { SpreadsheetApp.flush(); hubBump_(['base']); } ui.alert('Nothing to add — every paid submission before ' + FLOOR + ' already has its laundry row ✓' + (healed.length ? '\n\n🔧 Repaired the apartment on ' + healed.length + ' earlier backfilled row(s):\n ' + healed.join('\n ') : '') + aptWarn); return; } var byMonth = {}; missing.forEach(function (f) { var m = f.ymd.slice(0, 7); byMonth[m] = (byMonth[m] || { n: 0, egp: 0 }); byMonth[m].n++; byMonth[m].egp += f.amount; }); var summary = Object.keys(byMonth).sort().map(function (m) { return ' ' + m + ': +' + Math.round(byMonth[m].egp).toLocaleString() + ' EGP (' + byMonth[m].n + ' submissions)'; }).join('\n'); if (ui.alert('Backfill missing dry-clean laundry', missing.length + ' paid submissions are missing from the P&L — the historical import could not read their date format:\n\n' + summary + '\n\nThey will be added to Cost line items, tagged so they can never be added twice.\n\n⚠️ These months are CLOSED — the P&L will be restated, and the supplies / cleaning-labor splits will shift with the corrected submission counts.' + aptWarn + '\n\nContinue?', ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL) !== ui.Button.OK) return; var toAdd = missing.map(function (f) { var p = f.ymd.split('-'); return [f.apt, new Date(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2], 12, 0, 0), 'laundry', 'Dry-clean submission (app log) #' + f.id + ' — backfilled 2026-08-05, missed by the historical import', f.amount, 'DC backfill #' + f.id]; }); cli.getRange(cli.getLastRow() + 1, 1, toAdd.length, 6).setValues(toAdd); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); hubBump_(['base']); ui.alert('Backfilled ✓\n\n' + toAdd.length + ' laundry rows added:\n' + summary + (healed.length ? '\n🔧 apartment repaired on ' + healed.length + ' earlier row(s)' : '') + aptWarn + '\n\nRun Sync all, then check the months above — laundry rises, and the supplies / cleaning splits between flats correct themselves.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Backfill failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* Seed a new month's FX as CARRIED from the last known rate (Sarah's call, 2026-08-05). The P&L's FX column is an exact-month VLOOKUP, so a month with no rate silently books ZERO EGP costs and Net simply equals Revenue — which is how 2026-08 opened, invisibly. A carried row makes the month honest from day one; Source is marked "carried …" so isCurrentMonthSet stays FALSE and Today keeps asking for the real closing rate, and setFx overwrites this very row when you enter it. Inserted directly after the last month row, so the tab's trailing note stays at the bottom. */ function fxSeedCarried_(ss, month) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(TAB.fx); if (!sh) return null; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var lastRow = 0, lastM = '', lastRate = 0; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var m = monthKey(v[i][0]); if (!m) continue; if (m === month) return null; // already has a rate — never overwrite a real one lastRow = i + 1; if (m > lastM) { lastM = m; lastRate = num(v[i][1]); } } if (!lastRow || !lastRate) return null; // nothing to carry forward yet sh.insertRowAfter(lastRow); var r = lastRow + 1; sh.getRange(r, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); // TEXT — the P&L's VLOOKUP matches its text month sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[month, lastRate, 'carried from ' + lastM + ' — set the closing rate at month end']]); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return { month: month, rate: lastRate, from: lastM }; } /* Append month blocks by copying an existing one. copyTo carries every column's formula with its relative references intact; the Month cell is then forced to TEXT (see pnlMonthHeal_). */ function pnlAppendMonths_(sh, srcStart, n, lastCol, months) { var wrote = 0; months.forEach(function (mk) { var dst = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(srcStart, 1, n, lastCol).copyTo(sh.getRange(dst, 1, n, lastCol)); var mCells = sh.getRange(dst, 2, n, 1); mCells.setNumberFormat('@'); // text BEFORE the value, or Sheets parses it back into a date mCells.setValues(months.length ? Array.apply(null, Array(n)).map(function () { return [mk]; }) : []); wrote += n; }); if (wrote) SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return wrote; } /* The same work without any UI — runs inside Sync-all so a new month opens itself on the 1st. Heals first, appends only whole months up to today, never duplicates, never edits an existing row. */ function openMonthStep_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(TAB.pnl); if (!sh) return { skipped: 'no Monthly P&L tab' }; var healed = pnlMonthHeal_(sh); var lastRow = sh.getLastRow(), lastCol = sh.getLastColumn(); var ab = sh.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 2).getValues(), latest = '', have = {}; for (var i = 1; i < ab.length; i++) { var m = monthKey(ab[i][1]); if (!m) continue; have[m] = 1; if (m > latest) latest = m; } if (!latest) return { skipped: 'no months in the P&L', healed: healed }; var blockRows = []; for (var j = 1; j < ab.length; j++) if (monthKey(ab[j][1]) === latest) blockRows.push(j + 1); var srcStart = Math.min.apply(null, blockRows), n = blockRows.length; var nowM = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'), todo = []; var d = new Date(+latest.slice(0, 4), +latest.slice(5, 7) - 1, 1); while (true) { d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1); var mk = d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); if (mk > nowM) break; if (!have[mk]) todo.push(mk); } if (!todo.length) { var seedNow = fxSeedCarried_(ss, nowM); // heals a month opened before this rule existed return { latest: latest, opened: [], healed: healed, fxSeeded: seedNow ? [seedNow] : [] }; } var wrote = pnlAppendMonths_(sh, srcStart, n, lastCol, todo); var seeded = []; todo.forEach(function (mk3) { var s = fxSeedCarried_(ss, mk3); if (s) seeded.push(s); }); return { latest: latest, opened: todo, rows: wrote, healed: healed, fxSeeded: seeded }; } function openNewMonthPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var sh = ss.getSheetByName(TAB.pnl); if (!sh) { ui.alert('No Monthly P&L tab found.'); return; } var healed = pnlMonthHeal_(sh); // repair first — this alone fixes an already-broken month var lastRow = sh.getLastRow(), lastCol = sh.getLastColumn(); var ab = sh.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 2).getValues(), latest = '', have = {}; for (var i = 1; i < ab.length; i++) { var m = monthKey(ab[i][1]); if (!m) continue; have[m] = 1; if (m > latest) latest = m; } if (!latest) { ui.alert('Could not read any month from the P&L.'); return; } var blockRows = []; for (var j = 1; j < ab.length; j++) if (monthKey(ab[j][1]) === latest) blockRows.push(j + 1); var srcStart = Math.min.apply(null, blockRows), n = blockRows.length; var nowM = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM'), todo = []; var d = new Date(+latest.slice(0, 4), +latest.slice(5, 7) - 1, 1); while (true) { d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + 1); var mk = d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); if (mk > nowM) break; if (!have[mk]) todo.push(mk); } if (!todo.length) { var seedNow = fxSeedCarried_(ss, nowM); if (healed.length || seedNow) hubBump_(['base']); ui.alert('Nothing to open — the P&L already reaches ' + latest + ' (current month is ' + nowM + ').' + (healed.length ? /* §5.4 cash capture (Sarah 2026-08-02): "WeShare captures the cash, the P&L captures the bundle." Reconciled add/update/delete like the WeShare mirror; bundles before the cutover stay with the legacy WeShare-cash imports — no doubling either way. */ function syncNetBundles_() { var CUT = '2026-08-02'; var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var nb = ss.getSheetByName('Internet bundles'), cli = ss.getSheetByName('Cost line items'); if (!nb || !cli || nb.getLastRow() < 2) return { ok: true, none: true }; var v = nb.getDataRange().getValues(), desired = {}; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var flat = str(v[i][0]); if (!flat) continue; var ent = v[i][5] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(v[i][5], TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd') : str(v[i][5]).slice(0, 10); if (!ent || ent < CUT) continue; var csRaw = str(v[i][4]), csY = csRaw.slice(0, 10); if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(csY)) continue; } var cv = cli.getDataRange().getValues(), existing = {}, toDelete = [], adds = []; for (var r = 1; r < cv.length; r++) { var mm = str(cv[r][5]).match(/NetBundle #(\S+)/); if (mm) existing[mm[1]] = { row: r + 1, sig: str(cv[r][0]) + '|' + num(cv[r][4]) }; } Object.keys(existing).forEach(function (k) { if (!desired[k]) toDelete.push(existing[k].row); }); Object.keys(desired).forEach(function (k) { var d = desired[k]; if (existing[k] && existing[k].sig === d.flat + '|' + d.amount) return; if (existing[k]) toDelete.push(existing[k].row); adds.push({ k: k, d: d }); }); toDelete.sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; }).forEach(function (rw) { cli.deleteRow(rw); }); if (adds.length) { var startR = cli.getLastRow() + 1, dateFmt = ''; for (var fr = cli.getLastRow(); fr >= 2; fr--) if (cli.getRange(fr, 2).getValue() instanceof Date) { dateFmt = cli.getRange(fr, 2).getNumberFormat(); break; } adds.forEach(function (a, j) { var rw = startR + j; if (dateFmt) cli.getRange(rw, 2).setNumberFormat(dateFmt); cli.getRange(rw, 1, 1, 6).setValues([[a.d.flat, new Date(a.d.dateY + 'T12:00:00'), 'TV/Phone/Internet', a.d.desc, a.d.amount, 'NetBundle #' + a.k + ' auto']]); }); } SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return { ok: true, added: adds.length, deleted: toDelete.length }; } function addReading(p) { if (!p.flat || p.remaining == null || p.remaining === '') throw new Error('flat and remaining required'); } return out; } // ---- write helpers (inputs to the sheet; the sheet still does the math) ----- function setFx(month, rate) { if (!month || isNaN(rate)) throw new Error('month and rate required'); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(TAB.fx); // Month · EGP per USD · Source var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), sample = v.length > 1 ? v[1][0] : month, row = -1; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (monthKey(v[i][0]) === month) { row = i + 1; return { month: month, rate: rate }; } function markFinal(month, rate) { if (!month) throw new Error('month required'); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); if (rate != null && rate !== '' && !isNaN(parseFloat(rate))) setFx(month, parseFloat(rate));
