break; } } if (row < 0) { row = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(row, 1).setValue(normMonthVal(month, sample)); } sh.getRange(row, 2).setValue('final'); sh.getRange(row, 3).setValue(new Date()); // closed at sh.getRange(row, 4).setValue(fxUsed); // FX used /* Alerts spec A4 — the close is announced WITH its substance (net + split), Sarah+Khaled only */ try { var pt = readTabWithHeader(ss, TAB.pnl), Hh = pt.idx; var net = 0, kh = 0, sa = 0; pt.rows.forEach(function (r) { if (monthKey(r[Hh['Month']]) !== month) return; net += num(r[Hh['Net (USD)']]); kh += num(r[Hh['Khaled (USD)']]); sa += num(r[Hh['Sarah (USD)']]); }); tgHubNotify_('✅ شهر ' + month + ' اتقفل.\nالصافي $' + round2(net).toLocaleString() + ' — خالد $' + round2(kh).toLocaleString() + ' · سارة $' + round2(sa).toLocaleString() + '\nسعر الدولار ' + fxUsed + ' جنيه', { noEmad: true }); } catch (etg) {} return { month: month, status: 'final', fxUsed: fxUsed }; } function fxForMonth(ss, month) { // this month's rate, else the last prior (carried forward) var v = ss.getSheetByName(TAB.fx).getDataRange().getValues(), exact = null, carried = null, carriedM = ''; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var mk = monthKey(v[i][0]), rate = num(v[i][1]); if (!mk || !rate) continue; if (mk === month) exact = rate; else if (mk < month && mk > carriedM) { carried = rate; carriedM = mk; } } return exact != null ? exact : carried; } function normMonthVal(month, sample) { return Object.prototype.toString.call(sample) === '[object Date]' ? new Date(month + '-01T00:00:00') : month; } // ---- Smoobu (Source C) — STAGE 1: read-only probe, run smoobuTest() in the editor ---- // Credentials live in Script Properties (Project Settings → Script Properties): // SMOOBU_KEY = API key (Smoobu → Settings → For Developers) // SMOOBU_SECRET = API secret (needed for HMAC; legacy Api-Key works until 25 Sep 2026) var SMOOBU_BASE = 'https://login.smoobu.com'; function smoobuCreds_() { var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); return { key: (p.getProperty('SMOOBU_KEY') || '').trim(), secret: (p.getProperty('SMOOBU_SECRET') || '').trim() }; } function smoobuFetch_(path, params) { // GET only — the app is read-only against Smoobu var c = smoobuCreds_(); if (!c.key) throw new Error('SMOOBU_KEY missing in Script Properties'); // sort keys so URL and signature agree with a sorted server-side canonicalization var keys = Object.keys(params || {}).sort(); var qs = keys.map(function (k) { return k + '=' + encodeURIComponent(params[k]); }).join('&'); var url = SMOOBU_BASE + path + (qs ? '?' + qs : ''); var tried = []; // HMAC (doc §3.1): METHOD\nPATH\nQUERY_PARAMS\nTIMESTAMP\nNONCE\nBODY_HASH\nAPI_KEY // canonical QUERY_PARAMS variants tried in order: sorted qs · decoded qs · empty if (c.secret) { var bodyHash = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, '') .map(function (b) { return ('0' + (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join(''); var qsDecoded = keys.map(function (k) { return k + '=' + params[k]; }).join('&'); var canonQs = qs === qsDecoded ? [qs, ''] : [qs, qsDecoded, '']; for (var v = 0; v < canonQs.length; v++) { var ts = new Date().toISOString(), nonce = Utilities.getUuid(); var canon = ['GET', path, canonQs[v], ts, nonce, bodyHash, c.key].join('\n'); var sig = Utilities.base64Encode(Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature(canon, c.secret)); var r = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { headers: { 'X-API-Key': c.key, 'X-Timestamp': ts, 'X-Nonce': nonce, 'X-Signature': sig }, muteHttpExceptions: true }); if (r.getResponseCode() < 400) return { auth: 'hmac (canonical qs variant ' + v + ')', code: r.getResponseCode(), body: r.getContentText() }; tried.push('hmac v' + v + ':' + r.getResponseCode()); } } // legacy single-credential header forms var variants = [ { name: 'Api-Key', headers: { 'Api-Key': c.key } }, { name: 'X-API-Key', headers: { 'X-API-Key': c.key } }, { name: 'Bearer key', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + c.key } } ]; if (c.secret) variants.push({ name: 'Bearer secret', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + c.secret } }, { name: 'Api-Key secret', headers: { 'Api-Key': c.secret } }); var last = null; for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) { var rr = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { headers: variants[i].headers, muteHttpExceptions: true }); if (rr.getResponseCode() < 400) return { auth: variants[i].name + ' [tried ' + tried.join(', ') + ']', code: rr.getResponseCode(), body: rr.getContentText() }; tried.push(variants[i].name + ':' + rr.getResponseCode()); last = rr; } return { auth: 'ALL FAILED — ' + tried.join(', '), code: last.getResponseCode(), body: last.getContentText() }; } function smoobuTest() { // run manually in the editor; NO writes anywhere var out = []; var ap = smoobuFetch_('/api/apartments', {}); out.push('== /api/apartments [auth: ' + ap.auth + ', http ' + ap.code + '] =='); out.push(ap.body); var since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000); var res = smoobuFetch_('/api/reservations', { modifiedFrom: Utilities.formatDate(since, 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'), showCancellation: 'true', excludeBlocked: 'true', includePriceElements: 'true', pageSize: '3' }); out.push('== /api/reservations (last 30d, first 3) [auth: ' + res.auth + ', http ' + res.code + '] =='); out.push(res.body); Logger.log(out.join('\n\n')); return out.join('\n\n'); } // ---- Smoobu STAGE 2 — the sync. Upserts raw booking fields into Stays (+ apportionment // rows so the P&L sees new stays). The sheet keeps doing all the math. // Confirmed apartment.id → flat mapping (2026-07-20): var SMOOBU_FLAT = { '2765393': '8A', '2765383': '28A', '2765388': '17A', '2765398': 'Zayed 2000' }; // Stays raw-input columns (1-based). Everything else is formulas/manual — never touched. var STAYS_COL = { id:1, flat:2, portal:3, conf:4, status:5, booked:6, ci:7, co:8, adults:11, children:12, infants:13, guest:15, phone:16, payRoute:17, gross:18, netRec:23, other:25, otherType:26, source:32, notes:33 }; var SM_DIRECT_FIX_NOTE = 'direct cancellation — data-entry correction, no money kept'; /* ============================================================ * OTHER REVENUE LEDGER (Sarah 2026-08-06) * * WHY A LEDGER AND NOT A COLUMN. Other revenue kept breaking every rule we had, because a column on * the stay row cannot express what actually happens: * • one payment can carry money for TWO flats — Ehab's $40 was $20 for 8A and $20 for 28A; * • money can belong to a DIFFERENT booking than the row it sits on — Carol Li's $130 is a 50% * cancellation fee for a stay that never happened; * • it is earned on its own date, not on the stay's check-out — Ahed's $18.82 is June money * sitting on a July check-out. * And it was silently corrupting the accommodation rate: the apportionment divided All-in (which * includes Other) by the nights, so Carol Li's nightly rate read $45.47 instead of $40.06. * * THE RULE, verified to the cent against Sarah's own book on Carol Li, Ahed and Sajy: * nightly rate = (All-in − Other) ÷ nights, uniform across the stay * Other is never spread — it is recognised whole, in its own month * * A cancelled stay's kept money is ALWAYS Other revenue, never accommodation, and is apportioned * across the nights the booking would have occupied (Habib Abas 567.50 → Apr 324.29 + May 243.21, * which is 8/14 and 6/14). Recording it as accommodation would inflate ADR for a stay with no * nights — ADR is computed on earnings everywhere, so Other must stay out of it. * * This step only BUILDS the ledger. It changes no figure anywhere: the P&L still reads Stays!Y * until the switch is thrown separately, so the two can be compared side by side first. * ============================================================ */ var OTHER_REV_TAB = 'Other revenue'; var OTHER_REV_CATS = ['cancellation fee', 'extra service', 'damage reimbursement', 'test booking']; var OTHER_REV_HDR = ['Month', 'Date', 'Property', 'Amount (USD)', 'Category', 'Guest', 'Confirmation code', 'Stay ID', 'Collected via', 'Notes', 'Source']; /* Timing comes from Sarah's Airbnb reservation summary, which is the authority on when Other was earned — NOT the stay's check-out month. Only the exceptions are listed; everything else keeps its check-out month, which her sheet agrees with on 26 of 30 entries. Keyed 'confirmation code|flat'. An entry may split into several months (a kept cancellation fee spreads over the nights the booking would have used). `amount` overrides where it also differs — Ehab's internet is $20 charged but $19.27 received, because Stripe takes 3.65%. */ var OTHER_REV_FIXUP = { '135416242|28A': [{ month: '2026-04', amount: 19.27, note: 'net of Stripe 3.65% (charged 20.00)' }], '129825125|8A': [{ month: '2026-07', amount: 19.27, note: 'net of Stripe 3.65% (charged 20.00)' }], 'HMDXKXT8Y8|Zayed 2000': [{ month: '2025-06', note: 'earned in June; the stay checks out in July' }], 'HMHAMJYNS4|8A': [{ month: '2026-04', amount: 324.29, note: 'kept fee, 8 of 14 nights' }, { month: '2026-05', amount: 243.21, note: 'kept fee, 6 of 14 nights' }] }; /* In Sarah's book but never in the Hub. NOT here, and deliberately (Sarah 2026-08-06): Teaf Ali 101.00 and Mohamed Fengari 270.00. Both are cancelled rows carrying money in `Net — recorded`, and I had them down as kept fees. They are not. Fengari's row says so in the sheet — "wrong booking entry — cancelled", with its sibling noting "Smoobu 585.00 = this stay (315.00) + the cancelled 270.00 wrong entry — reconciles" — the same mistyped-and-re-entered pattern as Fergani's August pair. No money was ever kept on either, so neither is other revenue. */ var OTHER_REV_ADD = [ { month: '2026-05', flat: '17A', amount: 39.66, cat: 'extra service', guest: 'Farah Alajlan', conf: '128294297', note: "in Sarah's book, never in the Hub" } ]; /* A stable Ref per row, held in a 12th column, so the app can edit and delete without guessing at row numbers — the mistake that made the apportionment tab's Stay IDs drift out of alignment. */ var OTHER_REV_REF_COL = 12; function otherRevSheet_(ss) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(OTHER_REV_TAB); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(OTHER_REV_TAB); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, OTHER_REV_HDR.length).setValues([OTHER_REV_HDR]).setFontWeight('bold'); sh.setFrozenRows(1); } if (str(sh.getRange(1, OTHER_REV_REF_COL).getValue()) !== 'Ref') sh.getRange(1, OTHER_REV_REF_COL).setValue('Ref').setFontWeight('bold'); return sh; } function otherRevList_(month, flat) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = otherRevSheet_(ss), out = []; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var m = str(v[i][0]), f = str(v[i][2]); if (!m && !num(v[i][3])) continue; if (month && m !== month) continue; if (flat && f !== flat) continue; out.push({ ref: str(v[i][OTHER_REV_REF_COL - 1]), month: m, date: str(v[i][1]), flat: f, amount: round2(num(v[i][3])), cat: str(v[i][4]), guest: str(v[i][5]), conf: str(v[i][6]), stayId: str(v[i][7]), via: str(v[i][8]), note: str(v[i][9]), source: str(v[i][10]) }); } return out; } function otherRevSave_(p) { var lock = LockService.getScriptLock(); if (!lock.tryLock(10000)) throw new Error('busy — try again'); try { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = otherRevSheet_(ss); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), row = 0; if (p.ref) for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) if (str(v[i][OTHER_REV_REF_COL - 1]) === p.ref) { row = i + 1; break; } if (p.del) { if (!row) throw new Error('entry not found'); sh.deleteRow(row); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return { ref: p.ref, action: 'deleted' }; } if (!p.flat) throw new Error('which apartment?'); if (!p.month) throw new Error('which month?'); if (p.amount === null || p.amount === undefined || isNaN(p.amount)) throw new Error('amount is required'); if (p.amount < 0) throw new Error('amount cannot be negative'); if (OTHER_REV_CATS.indexOf(p.cat) < 0) throw new Error('pick a category'); /* A kept cancellation fee is spread over the nights the booking WOULD have used — Sarah's own treatment of Habib Abas: 567.50 → Apr 324.29 + May 243.21, which is 8/14 and 6/14. So the alert only ever asks "how much was kept?" and the split is derived, never typed. */ // a ZERO kept fee is one trace row in the check-out month — there is nothing to spread if (p.split && p.stayId && p.amount > 0) { var stSh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'), sv2 = stSh.getDataRange().getValues(), srow = null; for (var k = 1; k < sv2.length; k++) if (str(sv2[k][0]) === p.stayId) { srow = sv2[k]; break; } if (srow) { var ci2 = dateStr(srow[STAYS_COL.ci - 1], TZ), co2 = dateStr(srow[STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ); var ms2 = (ci2 && co2) ? monthSpan_(ci2, co2) : []; if (ms2.length > 1) { var cid2 = smDate_(ci2), cod2 = smDate_(co2), env2 = 0, per2 = []; ms2.forEach(function (m) { var n = nightsInMonth_(cid2, cod2, m); env2 += n; per2.push({ m: m, n: n }); }); var made = []; per2.forEach(function (q) { if (!q.n) return; var r2 = otherRevSave_({ month: q.m, date: q.m + '-01', flat: p.flat, amount: round2(p.amount * q.n / env2), cat: p.cat, guest: p.guest, conf: p.conf, stayId: p.stayId, via: p.via, note: (p.note ? p.note + ' · ' : '') + 'kept fee, ' + q.n + ' of ' + env2 + ' nights', user: p.user }); made.push(r2.ref); }); return { ref: made[0] || '', action: 'added across ' + made.length + ' months' }; } } } var ref = p.ref || ('OR' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 10)); var stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var note = p.note + (p.user ? (p.note ? ' · ' : '') + 'by ' + p.user + ' ' + stamp : ''); var line = [p.month, p.date || (p.month + '-01'), p.flat, round2(p.amount), p.cat, p.guest, p.conf, p.stayId, p.via, note, 'manual', ref]; if (!row) row = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, 2).setNumberFormat('@'); // Month/Date stay TEXT, as the P&L needs SpreadsheetApp.flush(); sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, line.length).setValues([line]); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return { ref: ref, action: p.ref ? 'updated' : 'added' }; } finally { lock.releaseLock(); } } function otherRevCat_(otherType, status) { var t = str(otherType).toLowerCase(); if (t.indexOf('cancel') > -1) return 'cancellation fee'; if (t.indexOf('test') > -1) return 'test booking'; if (t.indexOf('damage') > -1 || t.indexOf('reimburse') > -1) return 'damage reimbursement'; if (str(status).toLowerCase().indexOf('cancel') > -1) return 'cancellation fee'; return 'extra service'; // 'Services' and anything unlabelled } /* Build the ledger from Stays col Y + the fixups + the additions. Pure read — returns the plan. */ function otherRevPlan_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); if (!sh) throw new Error('no Stays tab'); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), rows = [], skipped = 0; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var r = v[i], flat = str(r[STAYS_COL.flat - 1]); if (!flat) continue; var amt = round2(num(r[STAYS_COL.other - 1])); if (!amt) continue; var conf = str(r[STAYS_COL.conf - 1]), co = dateStr(r[STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ); var guest = str(r[STAYS_COL.guest - 1]), id = str(r[0]); var cat = otherRevCat_(r[STAYS_COL.otherType - 1], r[STAYS_COL.status - 1]); var fix = OTHER_REV_FIXUP[conf + '|' + flat]; if (fix) { fix.forEach(function (f) { rows.push({ month: f.month, date: f.date || (f.month + '-01'), flat: flat, amount: round2(f.amount != null ? f.amount : amt), cat: cat, guest: guest, conf: conf, stayId: id, via: '', note: f.note || '', source: 'migrated (retimed)' }); }); } else { rows.push({ month: monthKey(co) || str(co).slice(0, 7), date: co, flat: flat, amount: amt, cat: cat, guest: guest, conf: conf, stayId: id, via: '', note: '', source: 'migrated' }); } } OTHER_REV_ADD.forEach(function (a) { rows.push({ month: a.month, date: a.month + '-01', flat: a.flat, amount: round2(a.amount), cat: a.cat, guest: a.guest, conf: a.conf, stayId: '', via: '', note: a.note, source: 'added' }); }); /* KEEP ANYTHING TYPED BY HAND. The build rewrites the whole tab from Stays, so without this a second run would silently delete every entry Sarah had since added — and going forward the manual entries ARE the point (damage reimbursements never appear in Smoobu at all). Only rows this tool wrote are regenerated; anything else is carried through untouched. */ var kept = 0, ex = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName(OTHER_REV_TAB); if (ex) { var e = ex.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var j = 1; j < e.length; j++) { var src = str(e[j][10]).toLowerCase(); if (!str(e[j][0]) && !num(e[j][3])) continue; if (src.indexOf('migrated') === 0 || src === 'added') continue; // regenerated below rows.push({ month: str(e[j][0]), date: str(e[j][1]), flat: str(e[j][2]), amount: round2(num(e[j][3])), cat: str(e[j][4]), guest: str(e[j][5]), conf: str(e[j][6]), stayId: str(e[j][7]), via: str(e[j][8]), note: str(e[j][9]), source: str(e[j][10]) || 'manual', ref: str(e[j][OTHER_REV_REF_COL - 1]) }); // keep its Ref so app edits still find it kept++; } } skipped = kept; // reported as "kept by hand" rows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.month === b.month ? (a.flat < b.flat ? -1 : 1) : (a.month < b.month ? -1 : 1); }); return { rows: rows, kept: skipped }; } function otherRevWrite_(ss, rows) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(OTHER_REV_TAB); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(OTHER_REV_TAB); } sh.clear(); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, OTHER_REV_HDR.length).setValues([OTHER_REV_HDR]).setFontWeight('bold'); sh.getRange(1, OTHER_REV_REF_COL).setValue('Ref').setFontWeight('bold'); if (!rows.length) { SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return 0; } // Month and Date as TEXT before the write — the P&L matches months as text, and an inserted date // format is exactly how 'yyyy-MM' turned into a real date in the apportionment tab. sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, 2).setNumberFormat('@'); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); var block = rows.map(function (r) { return [r.month, r.date, r.flat, r.amount, r.cat, r.guest, r.conf, r.stayId, r.via, r.note, r.source, r.ref || ('OR' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 10))]; // Ref survives a rebuild }); sh.getRange(2, 1, block.length, OTHER_REV_REF_COL).setValues(block); sh.setFrozenRows(1); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return block.length; } /* Menu: builds the ledger and PROVES it against the figures the P&L reads today. */ function otherRevenueBuild() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var P = otherRevPlan_(); // what the P&L reads today: Stays!Y by check-out month var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), old = {}, oldTot = 0; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var a = round2(num(v[i][STAYS_COL.other - 1])); if (!a) continue; var k = str(v[i][STAYS_COL.flat - 1]) + '|' + (monthKey(dateStr(v[i][STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ)) || ''); old[k] = round2((old[k] || 0) + a); oldTot = round2(oldTot + a); } var neu = {}, newTot = 0; P.rows.forEach(function (r) { var k = r.flat + '|' + r.month; neu[k] = round2((neu[k] || 0) + r.amount); newTot = round2(newTot + r.amount); }); var keys = {}, moves = []; Object.keys(old).forEach(function (k) { keys[k] = 1; }); Object.keys(neu).forEach(function (k) { keys[k] = 1; }); Object.keys(keys).sort().forEach(function (k) { var o = old[k] || 0, n = neu[k] || 0; if (Math.abs(o - n) > 0.005) moves.push(' ' + k + ': ' + o.toFixed(2) + ' → ' + n.toFixed(2) + ' (' + (n - o >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (n - o).toFixed(2) + ')'); }); var ok = ui.alert('Other revenue — build the ledger', P.rows.length + ' entries, total ' + newTot.toFixed(2) + ' USD' + (P.kept ? (' (' + P.kept + ' of them typed by hand and carried through untouched)') : '') + '.\n' + 'The P&L reads ' + oldTot.toFixed(2) + ' today, so the ledger adds ' + (newTot - oldTot).toFixed(2) + ' — Farah Alajlan 39.66, less 1.46 of Stripe fee on Ehab\'s internet.\n\n' + 'Flat/months that MOVE:\n' + (moves.length ? moves.join('\n') : ' (none)') + '\n\nNOTHING changes yet — the P&L still reads Stays!Y. This only writes the "' + OTHER_REV_TAB + '" tab so you can check it before the switch.\n\nBuild it?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var n = otherRevWrite_(ss, P.rows); hubBump_(['base']); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n"' + OTHER_REV_TAB + '" written: ' + n + ' entries, ' + newTot.toFixed(2) + ' USD.\n\n' + 'Check it, then run «Other revenue — switch the P&L over» to make it the source.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Other revenue build failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* ============================================================ * REVENUE — REBUILD ON THE NEW RULES (Sarah 2026-08-06) * * Three changes, and they only make sense together: * * 1. APPORTION ACCOMMODATION, NOT ALL-IN. The nightly rate is (All-in − Other) ÷ nights, so the * contribution is looked up from `Net — recorded` (col W) instead of `All-in` (col AD). Carol Li * is the proof: 961.35 ÷ 24 = $40.06/night, exactly Sarah's book, where dividing All-in gave * $45.47 because her $130 of other revenue was being smeared across every night of the stay. * * 2. THE PIECES MUST SUM TO THE WHOLE. Where a booking's nights do not fit its date envelope — * Smoobu merges non-contiguous blocks into one first-in→last-out row — the split is scaled so * the monthly pieces add back to the booking's own total. Yang Yang was recognising 856.90 in * one month from an 801.62 booking, and 31 nights of May out of a 29-night stay. * * 3. SEEDED WHERE THE SCALING ISN'T GOOD ENOUGH. For Yang Yang the invariant gets the total right * but lands May ~$190 high, so those two stays take Sarah's own month figures. Everything else * is computed. * * Cancelled stays contribute nothing here — their kept money is other revenue and lives in the * ledger, which is also what keeps ADR honest (ADR is earnings ÷ nights everywhere, and a stay with * no nights must not be able to move it). * ============================================================ */ var APPORT_SEED = { 'S0282': { '2025-04': 400.00, '2025-05': 400.00 }, // 17 nights + 16 nights, $400 each 'S0283': { '2025-04': 85.89, '2025-05': 421.80, '2025-06': 293.93 } // 3n · (11n + 4n) · 11n }; /* ONE STAY → its apportionment rows. The single place the rule lives, so the nightly sync and the full rebuild cannot drift apart. Returns [] for anything that must not contribute: cancelled, blocked, no money, no dates. `acc` is Net — recorded (accommodation ONLY — other revenue is in the ledger), and every piece is a share of it, so the rows can only ever sum back to what was actually paid. That invariant is the whole point: the Hub must never recognise revenue that isn't there. */ function apportRowsForStay_(v) { var flat = str(v[STAYS_COL.flat - 1]); if (!flat) return []; var id = str(v[0]), status = str(v[STAYS_COL.status - 1]).toLowerCase(); if (status.indexOf('blocked') > -1 || status.indexOf('cancel') > -1) return []; var acc = round2(num(v[STAYS_COL.netRec - 1])); if (!acc) return []; var ci = dateStr(v[STAYS_COL.ci - 1], TZ), co = dateStr(v[STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ); if (!ci || !co) return []; var statedN = Math.round(num(v[8])); var seed = APPORT_SEED[id]; if (seed) return Object.keys(seed).sort().map(function (m) { return { flat: flat, id: id, month: m, nights: '', total: statedN, acc: acc, amount: round2(seed[m]) }; }); var months = monthSpan_(ci, co); if (!months.length) return [{ flat: flat, id: id, month: monthKey(co) || str(co).slice(0, 7), nights: 0, total: statedN, acc: acc, amount: acc }]; var cid = smDate_(ci), cod = smDate_(co), envelope = 0, per = []; months.forEach(function (m) { var n = nightsInMonth_(cid, cod, m); envelope += n; per.push({ m: m, n: n }); }); if (!envelope) return []; return per.map(function (p) { return { flat: flat, id: id, month: p.m, nights: p.n, total: envelope, acc: acc, amount: round2(acc * p.n / envelope) }; }); } function apportRebuildPlan_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); if (!sh) throw new Error('no Stays tab'); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), rows = [], skipped = { cancelled: 0, blocked: 0, noMoney: 0, noDates: 0 }, scaled = [], seeded = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var r = v[i], flat = str(r[STAYS_COL.flat - 1]); if (!flat) continue; var id = str(r[0]), status = str(r[STAYS_COL.status - 1]).toLowerCase(); if (status.indexOf('blocked') > -1) { skipped.blocked++; continue; } if (status.indexOf('cancel') > -1) { skipped.cancelled++; continue; } var acc = round2(num(r[STAYS_COL.netRec - 1])); if (!acc) { skipped.noMoney++; continue; } var ci = dateStr(r[STAYS_COL.ci - 1], TZ), co = dateStr(r[STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ); if (!ci || !co) { skipped.noDates++; continue; } var seed = APPORT_SEED[id]; var statedN = Math.round(num(r[8])); if (seed) { Object.keys(seed).sort().forEach(function (m) { rows.push({ flat: flat, id: id, month: m, nights: '', total: statedN, acc: acc, amount: round2(seed[m]), basis: 'seeded' }); }); seeded.push(id + ' ' + flat); continue; } var months = monthSpan_(ci, co); if (!months.length) { // 0-night row (check-in = check-out) var m0 = monthKey(co) || str(co).slice(0, 7); rows.push({ flat: flat, id: id, month: m0, nights: 0, total: statedN, acc: acc, amount: acc, basis: 'zero-night → check-out month' }); continue; } var cid = smDate_(ci), cod = smDate_(co), envelope = 0, per = []; months.forEach(function (m) { var n = nightsInMonth_(cid, cod, m); envelope += n; per.push({ m: m, n: n }); }); if (!envelope) { skipped.noDates++; continue; } if (statedN > 0 && statedN !== envelope) scaled.push(id + ' ' + flat + ' (' + envelope + ' date-nights vs ' + statedN + ' booked)'); // Scale on the ENVELOPE so the pieces always add back to `acc`, whatever the nights column says. per.forEach(function (p) { rows.push({ flat: flat, id: id, month: p.m, nights: p.n, total: envelope, acc: acc, amount: round2(acc * p.n / envelope), basis: p.n + '/' + envelope + ' nights' }); }); } rows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.flat === b.flat ? (a.month < b.month ? -1 : 1) : (a.flat < b.flat ? -1 : 1); }); return { rows: rows, skipped: skipped, scaled: scaled, seeded: seeded }; } /* Write the apportionment block. Values, not formulas: the old rows carried a live VLOOKUP into Stays!AD, which is exactly how a stale Stay ID ended up pulling a different booking's money and how nights-in-month could exceed total nights. A computed figure that must reconcile should not be able to drift on its own afterwards — the rebuild is re-runnable, so recompute instead. */ function apportWrite_(ss, rows) { var sh = apportionSheet_(ss); if (!sh) throw new Error('no Revenue by month tab'); var last = sh.getLastRow(), cols = Math.max(7, sh.getLastColumn()); if (last > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, cols).clearContent(); if (!rows.length) { SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return 0; } sh.getRange(2, 3, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@'); // month stays TEXT SpreadsheetApp.flush(); // D/E/F keep the workings visible — nights in this month, nights the split is over, and the // accommodation being split. Sarah's rule is that a table of numbers has to add up on its face, // and D ÷ E × F = G is the whole calculation in one line. // The month is written in its OWN pass, after the rest has been committed. Formatting the column // and writing it as part of a wider setValues was not enough on this tab — the first rebuild put // real dates in every row (2022-04-01 instead of 2022-04), while the same pattern on the Other // revenue tab produced text. Rather than rely on it, write, read back, and heal. var block = rows.map(function (r) { return [r.flat, r.id, '', r.nights, r.total, r.acc, r.amount]; }); sh.getRange(2, 1, block.length, 7).setValues(block); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); var mcol = sh.getRange(2, 3, block.length, 1); mcol.setNumberFormat('@'); mcol.setValues(rows.map(function (r) { return [r.month]; })); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); if (sh.getRange(2, 3).getValue() instanceof Date) { // still coerced — force it mcol.setValues(rows.map(function (r) { return ["'" + r.month]; })); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } sh.getRange(1, 4).setValue('Nights in month'); sh.getRange(1, 5).setValue('Nights split over'); sh.getRange(1, 6).setValue('Accommodation (USD)'); sh.getRange(1, 7).setValue('Monthly contribution (USD)'); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return block.length; } /* Point the P&L at the ledger. Revenue gains it as a third source (apportionment + long-term rent + other revenue) and the Other line reads it instead of Stays!Y. Both must move together: the apportionment now carries accommodation ONLY, so without the third term Revenue would silently lose every penny of other revenue. Earnings = Revenue − Other therefore stays pure accommodation. */ function pnlRewireOther_(ss) { var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Monthly P&L'); if (!sh) throw new Error('no Monthly P&L tab'); var last = sh.getLastRow(), hdr = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String); var iRev = hdr.indexOf('Revenue (USD)') + 1, iOth = hdr.indexOf('Other revenue (USD)') + 1; if (!iRev || !iOth) throw new Error('Revenue / Other revenue columns not found on the P&L'); var revF = [], othF = []; for (var r = 2; r <= last; r++) { revF.push(["=SUMIFS('Revenue by month'!$G:$G,'Revenue by month'!$A:$A,A" + r + ",'Revenue by month'!$C:$C,B" + r + ")" + "+SUMIFS('Long-term rental income'!$E:$E,'Long-term rental income'!$A:$A,A" + r + ",'Long-term rental income'!$B:$B,B" + r + ")" + "+SUMIFS('" + OTHER_REV_TAB + "'!$D:$D,'" + OTHER_REV_TAB + "'!$C:$C,A" + r + ",'" + OTHER_REV_TAB + "'!$A:$A,B" + r + ")"]); othF.push(["=SUMIFS('" + OTHER_REV_TAB + "'!$D:$D,'" + OTHER_REV_TAB + "'!$C:$C,A" + r + ",'" + OTHER_REV_TAB + "'!$A:$A,B" + r + ")"]); } sh.getRange(2, iRev, revF.length, 1).setFormulas(revF); sh.getRange(2, iOth, othF.length, 1).setFormulas(othF); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return revF.length; } function revenueRebuild() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); if (!ss.getSheetByName(OTHER_REV_TAB)) { ui.alert('Build the Other revenue ledger first — the P&L is about to be pointed at it.'); return; } var P = apportRebuildPlan_(); // before / after, per flat-month, on the figure the P&L actually reads var before = {}, sh = apportionSheet_(ss), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var f = str(v[i][0]); if (!f) continue; var m = monthKey(v[i][2]) || str(v[i][2]).slice(0, 7); before[f + '|' + m] = round2((before[f + '|' + m] || 0) + num(v[i][6])); } var after = {}; P.rows.forEach(function (r) { after[r.flat + '|' + r.month] = round2((after[r.flat + '|' + r.month] || 0) + r.amount); }); var keys = {}, moves = [], bt = 0, at = 0; Object.keys(before).forEach(function (k) { keys[k] = 1; }); Object.keys(after).forEach(function (k) { keys[k] = 1; }); Object.keys(keys).sort().forEach(function (k) { var b = before[k] || 0, a = after[k] || 0; bt = round2(bt + b); at = round2(at + a); if (Math.abs(a - b) > 0.005) moves.push(' ' + k + ': ' + b.toFixed(2) + ' → ' + a.toFixed(2) + ' (' + (a - b >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (a - b).toFixed(2) + ')'); }); var ok = ui.alert('Revenue — rebuild on the new rules', 'accommodation rows: ' + P.rows.length + ' · seeded: ' + (P.seeded.join(', ') || 'none') + '\nskipped — cancelled ' + P.skipped.cancelled + ' · blocked ' + P.skipped.blocked + ' · no money ' + P.skipped.noMoney + ' · no dates ' + P.skipped.noDates + '\nnights rescaled to fit the booking: ' + (P.scaled.length ? P.scaled.join('; ') : 'none') + '\n\napportionment total ' + bt.toFixed(2) + ' → ' + at.toFixed(2) + ' (' + (at - bt >= 0 ? '+' : '') + (at - bt).toFixed(2) + ')' + '\n(it FALLS by design — other revenue moves out of here and into the ledger)\n\n' + (moves.length > 60 ? moves.slice(0, 60).join('\n') + '\n … ' + (moves.length - 60) + ' more' : moves.join('\n')) + '\n\nThis also points the P&L at the ledger. Apply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var n = apportWrite_(ss, P.rows); var rew = pnlRewireOther_(ss); hubBump_(null); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\napportionment rows: ' + n + '\nP&L rows rewired: ' + rew + '\n\nRevenue = accommodation + long-term rent + other revenue.\nEarnings = Revenue − Other = accommodation only.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Revenue rebuild failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* ============================================================ * THE AGREED CORRECTIONS (Sarah 2026-08-06) * Each line was settled against her Airbnb reservation book, one booking at a time. Her book wins * everywhere it disagrees with the Hub — it reconciled to the cent on 26 of 30 other-revenue * entries and on every month where none of the known defects applied. * Keyed on Stay ID. Re-runnable: it writes the end state, so running it twice changes nothing. * ============================================================ */ var STAYS_FIX = [ { id: 'S0162', set: { status: 'Confirmed' }, why: 'Airbnb, 2 nights, paid HSBC, then extended 13 nights direct — never cancelled. Restores 101.00 to 28A Aug 2025 (1,190.19 → 1,291.19, exactly her book).' }, /* REVERSED 2026-08-06. I read the sheet's own note — "wrong booking entry — cancelled" — and zeroed this row. Sarah: it is a REAL stay. When Habib Abas cancelled, Fengari took the dates and paid 270 by Instapay. NOT a duplicate of S0010: that is the separate November stay.' } ]; function staysFixPlan_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); if (!sh) throw new Error('no Stays tab'); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), byId = {}; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) byId[str(v[i][0])] = i + 1; var COL = { status: STAYS_COL.status, netRec: STAYS_COL.netRec, gross: STAYS_COL.gross, note: STAYS_COL.notes, portal: STAYS_COL.portal, payRoute: STAYS_COL.payRoute, ci: STAYS_COL.ci, co: STAYS_COL.co, nights: 9, other: STAYS_COL.other, otherType: STAYS_COL.otherType, noteSet: STAYS_COL.notes }; // note = append · noteSet = replace outright var plan = []; STAYS_FIX.forEach(function (f) { var row = byId[f.id]; if (!row) { plan.push({ id: f.id, missing: true, why: f.why, changes: [] }); return; } var cur = v[row - 1], changes = []; Object.keys(f.set).forEach(function (k) { var col = COL[k]; if (!col) return; var was = cur[col - 1], want = f.set[k]; var wasS = (k === 'ci' || k === 'co') ? dateStr(was, TZ) : str(was); var wantS = str(want); if (k === 'netRec' || k === 'gross' || k === 'nights' || k === 'other') { if (round2(num(was)) === round2(num(want))) return; wasS = String(round2(num(was))); } else if (wasS === wantS) return; else if (k === 'note' && wasS.indexOf(wantS) > -1) return; changes.push({ field: k, col: col, was: wasS, want: want }); }); plan.push({ id: f.id, row: row, flat: str(cur[STAYS_COL.flat - 1]), guest: str(cur[STAYS_COL.guest - 1]), why: f.why, changes: changes }); }); return { sh: sh, plan: plan }; } function staysApplyCorrections() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var P = staysFixPlan_(), lines = [], n = 0, missing = 0; P.plan.forEach(function (p) { if (p.missing) { missing++; lines.push(' ' + p.id + ' — NOT FOUND'); return; } if (!p.changes.length) { lines.push(' ' + p.id + ' ' + p.flat + ' ' + p.guest + ' — already correct'); return; } n += p.changes.length; lines.push(' ' + p.id + ' ' + p.flat + ' ' + p.guest); p.changes.forEach(function (c) { lines.push(' ' + c.field + ': ' + (c.was === '' ? '(blank)' : c.was) + ' → ' + c.want); }); }); if (!n) { ui.alert('Stays corrections\n\nNothing to change — every one is already applied.'); return; } var ok = ui.alert('Stays — apply the agreed corrections', n + ' cell(s) across ' + P.plan.length + ' stays' + (missing ? (' · ' + missing + ' NOT FOUND') : '') + '\n\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n\nEvery one was settled against your Airbnb reservation book.\n\nApply?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var wrote = 0; P.plan.forEach(function (p) { if (p.missing) return; p.changes.forEach(function (c) { var rng = P.sh.getRange(p.row, c.col); if (c.field === 'ci' || c.field === 'co') rng.setValue(smDate_(c.want)); else if (c.field === 'note') { var had = str(P.sh.getRange(p.row, c.col).getValue()); rng.setValue(had ? (had + ' | ' + c.want) : c.want); } else rng.setValue(c.want); wrote++; }); }); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); hubBump_(['base', 'stays']); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + wrote + ' cell(s) written.\n\nNow run «Revenue — rebuild on the new rules» so the ' + 'apportionment picks the corrections up.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Corrections failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } /* ============================================================ * OTHER REVENUE — THE THREE THINGS THAT MUST NOT BE MISSED (Sarah 2026-08-06) * * Other revenue is the one figure nothing else can derive, so if it is not entered it is simply * gone — and it was: $1,280 of kept cancellation fees, Farah's $39.66, Ehab's internet in the wrong * month and the wrong flat. Smoobu knows that *something happened* in two of the three cases and * nothing at all in the third, so the Hub asks rather than guesses: * * 1. an OTA booking was CANCELLED → "how much was kept?" (Smoobu never knows the amount) * 2. a price element with type:null → "confirm this extra" (stale after any booking change, * and may belong to another flat) * 3. a DIRECT stay with no payment route → "how was this paid?" (decides whether a fee applies) * * A DIRECT cancellation raises nothing — those are data-entry corrections. * Everything is matched against the ledger, so an answered alert stops appearing. * ============================================================ */ var REV_ALERTS_PROP = 'REV_ALERTS'; function otherRevKeys_(ss) { // conf|flat already in the ledger var sh = ss.getSheetByName(OTHER_REV_TAB), k = {}; if (!sh) return k; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var conf = str(v[i][6]), flat = str(v[i][2]); if (!conf && !flat) continue; k[conf + '|' + flat] = true; k[conf] = true; } return k; } /* Runs inside the sync. Cheap: one Smoobu pull it already makes, plus two sheet reads. */ function revAlertsStep_() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var have = otherRevKeys_(ss); var out = { cancellations: [], extras: [], noRoute: staysNeedPayRoute_(), builtAt: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), TZ, 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') }; // ---- 1 · OTA cancellations with nothing recorded against them var sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); if (sh) { var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var r = v[i], portal = str(r[STAYS_COL.portal - 1]); } } // ---- 2 · extras charged inside Smoobu (a price element with no type) try { var from = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Date.now() - 120 * 86400000), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var to = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(Date.now() + 365 * 86400000), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); if (rr.code >= 400) break; var jj = JSON.parse(rr.body); (jj.bookings || []).forEach(function (b) { var flat = SMOOBU_FLAT[str(b.apartment && b.apartment.id)]; if (!flat) return; var conf = str(b['reference-id'] || b.id); } catch (e2) {} return { cancellations: out.cancellations.length, extras: out.extras.length, noRoute: out.noRoute.length }; } function revAlertsRead_() { try { return JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(REV_ALERTS_PROP) || 'null') || { cancellations: [], extras: [], noRoute: [] }; } catch (e) { return { cancellations: [], extras: [], noRoute: [] }; } } /* Telegram wording — short, and it always names the money or the fact that the money is unknown. */ function revAlertsTelegram_() { var a = revAlertsRead_(), out = []; a.cancellations.forEach(function (c) { out.push('❓ ' + c.flat + ' — ' + (c.guest || 'guest') + ' cancelled (' + c.checkIn + ' → ' + c.checkOut + ', ' + c.nights + 'n). How much was kept?'); }); a.extras.forEach(function (e) { out.push('➕ ' + e.flat + ' — ' + (e.guest || 'guest') + ': "' + e.name + '" ' + (e.qty > 1 ? e.qty + ' × ' : '') + 'USD ' + e.amount.toFixed(2) + '. How was it paid?'); }); return out; } /* Smoobu channel → our Portal. Matched in the right ORDER, never by a loose substring: Smoobu calls a direct reservation "Direct booking", and the old test `indexOf('booking')` filed every one of them as Booking.com — which suppressed the payment-method prompt, stamped an OTA default account and applied Booking.com's payment charge to money that never went through Booking.com. 'direct' is therefore tested BEFORE 'booking', and 'booking' now has to be booking.com. */ function smoobuPortal_(b) { var cn = str(b && b.channel && b.channel.name).toLowerCase().trim(); if (!cn) return 'Direct'; if (cn.indexOf('airbnb') > -1) return 'Airbnb'; if (cn.indexOf('direct') > -1) return 'Direct'; if (cn.indexOf('booking.com') > -1 || cn === 'booking') return 'Booking.com'; return 'Direct'; // homepage/manual/anything else = we were paid directly } /* "How was this paid?" — a DIRECT booking has no default account, so the route must be chosen per booking, and until it is we do not know whether a fee was taken (Stripe 3.65%, Instapay none). Fergani's two August reservations arrived with no prompt at all, because the channel bug had filed them as Booking.com and the OTA default silently stamped HSBC on them. This is the alert that should have fired: every confirmed DIRECT stay whose payment route is still blank. Returned by props_alerts (Hub home) and repeated in the Telegram digest. */ function staysNeedPayRoute_() { try { var sh = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName('Stays'); if (!sh) return []; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), out = []; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var r = v[i]; if (str(r[STAYS_COL.portal - 1]) !== 'Direct') continue; if (str(r[STAYS_COL.status - 1]).toLowerCase().indexOf('confirm') < 0) continue; if (str(r[STAYS_COL.payRoute - 1])) continue; if (!num(r[STAYS_COL.gross - 1]) && !num(r[STAYS_COL.netRec - 1])) continue; // no money = nothing to route out.push({ id: str(r[0]), flat: str(r[STAYS_COL.flat - 1]), guest: str(r[STAYS_COL.guest - 1]), checkIn: dateStr(r[STAYS_COL.ci - 1], TZ), checkOut: dateStr(r[STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ), amount: round2(num(r[STAYS_COL.netRec - 1]) || num(r[STAYS_COL.gross - 1])) }); } return out.slice(0, 25); } catch (e) { return []; } } // OTA (Airbnb/Booking) account default per flat×channel from the editable 'Payment defaults' tab; Direct → '' (confirmed per booking). function otaDefaultMap_(ss) { var m = {}; buildPaymentDefaults(ss).forEach(function (d) { if (d.channel && d.channel !== 'Direct') m[d.flat + '|' + d.channel] = d.account; }); return m; } // One-time: fill col Q with the OTA default for any OTA booking whose route is blank (e.g. Azzaz). Direct left alone. function fillOtaDefaults() { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); if (!sh) throw new Error('no Stays tab'); var otaDef = otaDefaultMap_(ss), v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), n = 0; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var flat = str(v[i][1]), portal = str(v[i][2]), route = str(v[i][16]); if (!flat || portal === 'Direct' || route) continue; var acc = otaDef[flat + '|' + portal]; if (!acc) continue; sh.getRange(i + 1, 17).setNumberFormat('@'); sh.getRange(i + 1, 17).setValue(acc); n++; } SpreadsheetApp.flush(); return { filled: n }; } function weShareSyncJob() { return syncWeShare(false); } // trigger handler — always a real mirror write (never dry-run) function weShareInstallTrigger() { // run once in the editor to (re)install the WeShare mirror ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'weShareSyncJob') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('weShareSyncJob').timeBased().everyHours(1).create(); return 'installed'; } function dryCleanSyncJob() { return syncDryClean(false); } // trigger handler — always a real write (never dry-run) function dryCleanInstallTrigger() { // run once in the editor to (re)install the daily laundry sync // daily is plenty — laundry submissions are low-frequency; keeps trigger-runtime quota free for the Smoobu 10-min job. ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'dryCleanSyncJob') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); ScriptApp.newTrigger('dryCleanSyncJob').timeBased().everyDays(1).atHour(4).create(); return 'installed'; } // One Smoobu job for the trigger: reservations every run + blocks throttled to ~hourly (they change rarely, // and blocks do a full range pull — no need to hit the API for them every 10 min). function smoobuJob() { var res = { smoobu: null, blocks: null }; res.smoobu = smoobuSync(); var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), lastB = sp.getProperty('BLOCKS_LAST_SYNC'); if (!lastB || (Date.now() - new Date(lastB).getTime()) > 55 * 60 * 1000) { try { res.blocks = syncBlocks(false); sp.setProperty('BLOCKS_LAST_SYNC', new Date().toISOString()); } catch (e) { res.blocks = { error: String(e && e.message || e) }; } } return res; } function smoobuInstallTrigger() { // run once in the editor — ONE trigger runs reservations + blocks // every 10 min ≈ 20-40 min/day of trigger runtime — safely inside the consumer-account 90-min/day quota. // Do NOT go below 5 min: 1-min syncs would exceed the quota and Google silently disables triggers. ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { var h = t.getHandlerFunction(); if (h === 'smoobuSync' || h === 'smoobuJob' || h === 'syncBlocksJob') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); // drop old/separate ones ScriptApp.newTrigger('smoobuJob').timeBased().everyMinutes(10).create(); return 'installed (smoobuJob = reservations + blocks)'; } // daysBack > 0 → ignore the last-sync watermark and re-pull everything modified in that window // (healing pass). 0 / omitted → normal incremental behaviour. function smoobuSync(daysBack) { var lock = LockService.getScriptLock(); if (!lock.tryLock(5000)) return { skipped: 'sync already running' }; try { var sp = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); var last = sp.getProperty('SMOOBU_LAST_SYNC'); var fromD = daysBack > 0 ? new Date(Date.now() - daysBack * 86400000) : (last ? new Date(new Date(last).getTime() - 2 * 86400000) : new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000)); var from = Utilities.formatDate(fromD, 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); // pull every reservation modified since the last sync (2-day overlap for safety) var bookings = [], page = 1, pages = 1; do { var r = smoobuFetch_('/api/reservations', { modifiedFrom: from, showCancellation: 'true', excludeBlocked: 'true', includePriceElements: 'true', pageSize: '100', page: String(page) }); if (r.code >= 400) throw new Error('smoobu http ' + r.code + ' (' + r.auth + ')'); var j = JSON.parse(r.body); bookings = bookings.concat(j.bookings || []); pages = j.page_count || 1; page++; } while (page <= pages); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); var lastCol = sh.getLastColumn(); var headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(function (h) { return str(h); }); var smCol = headers.indexOf('Smoobu ID') + 1; if (smCol === 0) { smCol = lastCol + 1; sh.getRange(1, smCol).setValue('Smoobu ID'); lastCol = smCol; } var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var byId = {}, byKey = {}; // Smoobu id → row# · flat|ci|co → row# (for backfill linking) for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { var sid = str(v[i][smCol - 1]); if (sid) byId[sid] = i + 1; var key = str(v[i][1]) + '|' + dateStr(v[i][6], TZ) + '|' + dateStr(v[i][7], TZ); if (!byKey[key]) byKey[key] = i + 1; } var otaDef = otaDefaultMap_(ss); // OTA account defaults to auto-fill col Q var stat = { fetched: bookings.length, updated: 0, appended: 0, linked: 0, cancelled: 0, skipped: 0 }; bookings.forEach(function (b) { var flat = SMOOBU_FLAT[str(b.apartment && b.apartment.id)]; if (!flat || b['is-blocked-booking']) { stat.skipped++; return; } var id = str(b.id); var cancelled = str(b.type).toLowerCase().indexOf('cancel') > -1; var portal = smoobuPortal_(b); // A DIRECT "cancellation" is almost never a cancellation (Sarah 2026-08-06): Smoobu has no // delete, so a mistyped direct reservation is cancelled and re-entered. Every non-blocked // cancellation in the live window was one of these, three with an obvious replacement created // minutes later. Such a row must carry NO money — left alone it becomes a cancelled stay // holding a duplicate of the live booking's value (Fergani: 222.73 + 281.09 of phantom // revenue). The row is kept, zeroed and tagged, so the trace survives a re-sync. var directFix = cancelled && portal === 'Direct'; var row = byId[id] || byKey[flat + '|' + b.arrival + '|' + b.departure] || 0; if (row) { // ---- update in place (raw, non-formula cells only) var wasLinked = !!byId[id]; var rowF = sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, lastCol).getFormulas()[0]; var cur = sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0]; // Flat is part of the comparison: a booking MOVED between apartments (same dates, same status) // otherwise looked unchanged, so nothing updated and the row kept the old apartment forever — // the moved stay stayed in 28A while the booking that took its place also landed in 28A, and the // Week grid showed two overlapping stays. regenApportion_ keys on stayId, so once this fires it // deletes the old rows and rewrites them under the NEW flat; revenue follows the move. var before = str(cur[STAYS_COL.flat - 1]) + '|' + str(cur[STAYS_COL.status - 1]) + '|' + dateStr(cur[STAYS_COL.ci - 1], TZ) + '|' + dateStr(cur[STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ); function up(col, val) { if (rowF[col - 1]) return; if (str(cur[col - 1]) !== str(val)) sh.getRange(row, col).setValue(val); } function upDate(col, ymdStr) { if (rowF[col - 1]) return; if (dateStr(cur[col - 1], TZ) !== ymdStr) sh.getRange(row, col).setValue(smDate_(ymdStr)); } up(STAYS_COL.flat, flat); // ← was never updated: a move between apartments was silently ignored // Portal was never updated either, which is how the channel bug stayed invisible: Smoobu // names the channel "Direct booking", the old test asked indexOf('booking') and filed it as // Booking.com. Only NEW rows were affected (the update path never touched the column), so // Sarah's older direct stays kept the right value while Fergani's four came in wrong — no // payment-method prompt, an OTA default account, and Booking.com's 2.736% payment charge // taken off a direct booking. Writing it here self-heals the existing rows. up(STAYS_COL.portal, portal); up(STAYS_COL.status, cancelled ? 'Cancelled' : 'Confirmed'); if (b.arrival) upDate(STAYS_COL.ci, b.arrival); if (b.departure) upDate(STAYS_COL.co, b.departure); if (b['guest-name']) up(STAYS_COL.guest, b['guest-name']); if (b.phone) up(STAYS_COL.phone, b.phone); if (b.price != null) up(STAYS_COL.gross, directFix ? 0 : num(b.price)); if (directFix) { // zero the money, keep the row as a trace up(STAYS_COL.netRec, 0); if (str(cur[STAYS_COL.notes - 1]).indexOf(SM_DIRECT_FIX_NOTE) < 0) up(STAYS_COL.notes, (str(cur[STAYS_COL.notes - 1]) ? str(cur[STAYS_COL.notes - 1]) + ' | ' : '') + SM_DIRECT_FIX_NOTE); } if (b['reference-id'] && !str(cur[STAYS_COL.conf - 1])) up(STAYS_COL.conf, b['reference-id']); if (portal !== 'Direct' && !str(cur[STAYS_COL.payRoute - 1]) && otaDef[flat + '|' + portal]) // backfill OTA account into col Q up(STAYS_COL.payRoute, otaDef[flat + '|' + portal]); sh.getRange(row, smCol).setValue(id); fixDerived_(sh, row, portal, smCommission_(b), num(b.price)); // heal sync-created rows (net chain → All-in) if (!wasLinked) { stat.linked++; byId[id] = row; } var after = flat + '|' + (cancelled ? 'Cancelled' : 'Confirmed') + '|' + str(b.arrival) + '|' + str(b.departure); if (before !== after) { stat.updated++; if (cancelled) stat.cancelled++; regenApportion_(ss, str(cur[0]), flat, b.arrival, b.departure, cancelled); } } else { // ---- append new booking var newRow = sh.getLastRow() + 1; var tmplF = sh.getRange(newRow - 1, 1, 1, lastCol).getFormulasR1C1()[0]; var raw = {}; raw[STAYS_COL.id] = id; raw[STAYS_COL.flat] = flat; raw[STAYS_COL.portal] = portal; raw[STAYS_COL.conf] = str(b['reference-id']); raw[STAYS_COL.status] = cancelled ? 'Cancelled' : 'Confirmed'; raw[STAYS_COL.booked] = smDate_(str(b['created-at']).slice(0, 10)); raw[STAYS_COL.ci] = smDate_(b.arrival); raw[STAYS_COL.co] = smDate_(b.departure); raw[STAYS_COL.adults] = num(b.adults); raw[STAYS_COL.children] = num(b.children); raw[STAYS_COL.infants] = 0; raw[STAYS_COL.guest] = str(b['guest-name']); raw[STAYS_COL.phone] = str(b.phone); raw[STAYS_COL.gross] = directFix ? 0 : num(b.price); raw[STAYS_COL.source] = 'smoobu'; raw[smCol] = id; if (directFix) { raw[STAYS_COL.netRec] = 0; raw[STAYS_COL.notes] = SM_DIRECT_FIX_NOTE; } if (portal !== 'Direct' && otaDef[flat + '|' + portal]) raw[STAYS_COL.payRoute] = otaDef[flat + '|' + portal]; // OTA → default account (Direct stays blank for the confirm flow) for (var c = 1; c <= lastCol; c++) { if (raw[c] !== undefined) sh.getRange(newRow, c).setValue(raw[c]); else if (tmplF[c - 1]) sh.getRange(newRow, c).setFormulaR1C1(tmplF[c - 1]); } fixDerived_(sh, newRow, portal, smCommission_(b), num(b.price)); // build the derived chain + expected-net snapshot byId[id] = newRow; byKey[flat + '|' + b.arrival + '|' + b.departure] = newRow; stat.appended++; if (cancelled) stat.cancelled++; if (!cancelled) regenApportion_(ss, id, flat, b.arrival, b.departure, false); } }); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); sp.setProperty('SMOOBU_LAST_SYNC', new Date().toISOString()); sp.setProperty('SMOOBU_LAST_RESULT', JSON.stringify(stat)); // the 30-min trigger lands here too — only an actual change should force a payload rebuild if (stat.updated + stat.appended + stat.linked + stat.cancelled > 0) hubBump_(['base','stays']); return stat; } finally { lock.releaseLock(); } } function smDate_(s) { var p = str(s).slice(0, 10).split('-'); return new Date(+p[0], +p[1] - 1, +p[2], 12, 0, 0); } // ---- Smoobu BLOCKED days → Stays mirror. Own pull (arrival range, excludeBlocked=false), own rows (Source // "smoobu-block", status "Blocked", no price/formula/apportionment). Keyed "B"; add/update/delete. /* Sheet-menu backfill (2026-08-04): the routine mirror pulls 30 days back, so blocks older than that were never (re)fetched — and before v100 they were being deleted as they aged out. This widens the pull to ~13 months once, restoring the Stats booked/blocked history. Re-runnable: the mirror is keyed on the Smoobu block id, so nothing duplicates. */ function blocksBackfillPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var r = syncBlocks(false, 400), rep = r.report || {}; hubBump_(['stays']); ui.alert('Blocked days — history backfill\n\n' + 'reservations fetched: ' + rep.fetched + '\n' + 'active blocks found: ' + rep.blocks + '\n' + 'added: ' + rep.add + ' · updated: ' + rep.update + ' · removed: ' + rep.del + '\n' + 'older rows kept as history: ' + rep.keptPast + '\n' + 'cancelled blocks skipped: ' + rep.cancelledSkipped + '\n\nOpen Statistics → “Booked · blocked · free nights” to see the history.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Backfill failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e) + '\n\n(If it mentions authorization, run any menu item once and approve, then retry.)'); } } function syncBlocks(dryRun, daysBack) { var now = new Date(); var back = Math.max(30, parseInt(daysBack, 10) || 30); // &days=N widens the pull for a history backfill var from = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(now.getTime() - back * 86400000), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var to = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(now.getTime() + 365 * 86400000), 'UTC', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); var bookings = [], page = 1, pages = 1; do { var r = smoobuFetch_('/api/reservations', { from: from, to: to, excludeBlocked: 'false', pageSize: '100', page: String(page) }); if (r.code >= 400) throw new Error('smoobu http ' + r.code + ' (' + r.auth + ')'); var j = JSON.parse(r.body); bookings = bookings.concat(j.bookings || []); pages = j.page_count || 1; page++; } while (page <= pages); var desired = {}, cancelledSkipped = 0; bookings.forEach(function (b) { if (!b['is-blocked-booking']) return; if (str(b.type).toLowerCase().indexOf('cancel') > -1) { cancelledSkipped++; return; } // a cancelled/removed block is NOT active (root cause of the phantom Jul-4 block) var flat = SMOOBU_FLAT[str(b.apartment && b.apartment.id)]; if (!flat) return; desired['B' + str(b.id)] = { id: 'B' + str(b.id), flat: flat, ci: str(b.arrival).slice(0, 10), co: str(b.departure).slice(0, 10) }; }); var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), existing = {}; for (var i = 1; i < v.length; i++) { if (str(v[i][STAYS_COL.source - 1]) !== 'smoobu-block') continue; existing[str(v[i][STAYS_COL.id - 1])] = { row: i + 1, flat: str(v[i][STAYS_COL.flat - 1]), ci: dateStr(v[i][STAYS_COL.ci - 1], TZ), co: dateStr(v[i][STAYS_COL.co - 1], TZ) }; } var toAppend = [], toDelete = [], rep = { fetched: bookings.length, blocks: Object.keys(desired).length, cancelledSkipped: cancelledSkipped, add: 0, update: 0, del: 0, keptPast: 0, unchanged: 0 }; /* HISTORY IS FROZEN (Sarah 2026-08-04, the blocked-only-in-July finding): the mirror used to delete any block absent from the pull — and the pull starts 30 days back, so PAST blocks were erased as they aged out and the Stats history vanished. Now a row is only reconciled-away if it lies INSIDE the pull window; anything older stays as the historical record. */ Object.keys(existing).forEach(function (bid) { if (desired[bid]) return; if (existing[bid].co >= from) { toDelete.push(existing[bid].row); rep.del++; } else rep.keptPast++; }); Object.keys(desired).forEach(function (bid) { var d = desired[bid], ex = existing[bid]; if (!ex) { toAppend.push(d); rep.add++; } else if (ex.flat !== d.flat || ex.ci !== d.ci || ex.co !== d.co) { toDelete.push(ex.row); toAppend.push(d); rep.update++; } else rep.unchanged++; }); if (!dryRun) { toDelete.sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; }).forEach(function (rw) { sh.deleteRow(rw); }); var start = sh.getLastRow() + 1; toAppend.forEach(function (d, k) { var row = start + k; sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.id).setValue(d.id); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.flat).setValue(d.flat); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.portal).setValue('Blocked'); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.status).setValue('Blocked'); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.ci).setValue(smDate_(d.ci)); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.co).setValue(smDate_(d.co)); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.guest).setValue('Blocked'); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.gross).setValue(0); sh.getRange(row, STAYS_COL.source).setValue('smoobu-block'); }); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } return { ok: true, version: VERSION, dryRun: !!dryRun, report: rep, sample: toAppend.slice(0, 10) }; } // (blocks are run by smoobuJob on the Smoobu trigger — no separate trigger needed. Manual: props_sync_blocks.) function smCommission_(b) { // OTA commission from the price breakdown var c = 0; (b.priceElements || []).forEach(function (pe) { if (str(pe.type).toLowerCase() === 'commission') c += num(pe.amount); }); return c || num(b['commission-included']); } // Derived Stays columns — the sheet's own model (§3.4 notes + observed rows): // V (Net — calculated) is a live formula; W (Net — recorded) is pre-filled with the expected net when a // booking is recorded (house convention, e.g. S0145) and bank reconciliation corrects it later — Delta (X) // flags any drift. AD (All-in) = W + Y. So a synced row only earns revenue once W is set. // This touches ONLY rows the sync created (Source = 'smoobu') whose W is still empty — reconciled history is untouchable. function fixDerived_(sh, row, portal, commission, price) { function fill(col, x, isFormula) { // safe fill: empty cells only var rng = sh.getRange(row, col); if (rng.getFormula()) return; var v = rng.getValue(); if (v !== '' && v != null) return; if (isFormula) rng.setFormulaR1C1(x); else rng.setValue(x); } fill(9, '=RC8-RC7', true); // I Nights = out − in fill(10, '=IF(RC6="","",RC7-RC6)', true); // J Lead time fill(14, '=N(RC11)+N(RC12)+N(RC13)', true); // N Total guests var isSyncRow = str(sh.getRange(row, 32).getValue()) === 'smoobu'; // AF Source var wCell = sh.getRange(row, 23); // W Net — recorded if (!isSyncRow || str(wCell.getValue()) !== '') return; if (commission) sh.getRange(row, 19).setValue(commission); // S Commission (from Smoobu breakdown) var charge = 0, vat = 0; if (portal === 'Booking.com') { // T + U only apply to Booking.com sh.getRange(row, 20).setFormulaR1C1('=ROUND(RC18*0.024,2)'); // T payment charge — Smoobu 2.4% est (RULE C4) sh.getRange(row, 21).setFormulaR1C1('=ROUND(0.14*(N(RC19)+N(RC20)),2)'); // U 14% VAT on (commission + charge) charge = Math.round(price * 2.4) / 100; vat = Math.round(14 * ((commission || 0) + charge)) / 100; } else { sh.getRange(row, 20).setValue(''); sh.getRange(row, 21).setValue(''); // clear template leftovers } sh.getRange(row, 22).setFormulaR1C1('=RC18-N(RC19)-N(RC20)-N(RC21)'); // V Net — calculated (portal-generic) var net = Math.round((price - (commission || 0) - charge - vat) * 100) / 100; wCell.setValue(net); // W expected-net snapshot → All-in goes live var ad = sh.getRange(row, 30); // AD All-in = W + Y (ensure it exists) if (!ad.getFormula() && str(ad.getValue()) === '') ad.setFormulaR1C1('=RC23+N(RC25)'); } // Revenue-apportionment rows: one per stay-month; the P&L reads these. Regenerated per touched stay. function apportionSheet_(ss) { var shs = ss.getSheets(); for (var i = 0; i < shs.length; i++) { var h = shs[i].getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.min(8, shs[i].getLastColumn() || 1)).getValues()[0].join('|'); if (h.indexOf('Stay ID') > -1 && h.indexOf('Monthly contribution') > -1) return shs[i]; } return null; } /* Re-apportion ONE stay, on the same rule as the full rebuild — they share apportRowsForStay_, so there is exactly one definition of how revenue is split and the nightly sync can never quietly reintroduce the old one. The end state is COMPUTED and the block rewritten, rather than rows being inserted and deleted in place. deleteRow/insertRowBefore is what let stale rows survive: the old rows carried a live VLOOKUP and a hand-maintained nights count, so a Stay ID that shifted underneath pulled a different booking's money, and nights-in-month could exceed the booking's own nights. Rewriting is also idempotent, which every writer here has to be. `flat/arrival/departure/cancelled` are kept in the signature for the existing callers but are no longer trusted — the Stays row itself is read, so a caller passing stale values cannot mislead it. */ function regenApportion_(ss, stayId, flat, arrival, departure, cancelled) { var sh = apportionSheet_(ss); if (!sh || !stayId) return; var stSh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'); if (!stSh) return; var sv = stSh.getDataRange().getValues(), stayRow = null; for (var i = 1; i < sv.length; i++) if (str(sv[i][0]) === str(stayId)) { stayRow = sv[i]; break; } var want = stayRow ? apportRowsForStay_(stayRow) : []; var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), keep = []; for (var j = 1; j < v.length; j++) { if (!str(v[j][0]) && !num(v[j][6])) continue; if (str(v[j][1]) === str(stayId)) continue; // this stay's rows are rebuilt keep.push({ flat: str(v[j][0]), id: str(v[j][1]), month: monthKey(v[j][2]) || str(v[j][2]).slice(0, 7), nights: v[j][3], total: v[j][4], acc: num(v[j][5]), amount: num(v[j][6]) }); } want.forEach(function (w) { keep.push(w); }); keep.sort(function (a, b) { return a.flat === b.flat ? (a.month < b.month ? -1 : 1) : (a.flat < b.flat ? -1 : 1); }); apportWrite_(ss, keep); } /* ============================================================ * THE SAFETY NET (Sarah 2026-08-06) * * regenApportion_ keeps the sync honest, but it only fires when the SYNC changes a booking. Money * also moves by other routes: Sarah edits Net — recorded in the sheet, a correction tool runs, a * status is flipped by hand. None of those re-apportion anything, and a silent mismatch is exactly * the failure mode that hid $781.67 of phantom Yang Yang revenue for a year. * * So every sync re-derives what each stay OUGHT to contribute and compares it with what the tab * actually holds. Anything that disagrees is rewritten. It is a comparison of computed against * stored, not a diff of intentions — it cannot be fooled by a write that silently did nothing. * ============================================================ */ function apportHealStep_(dry) { var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(); var stSh = ss.getSheetByName('Stays'), sh = apportionSheet_(ss); if (!stSh || !sh) return { skipped: 'tabs missing' }; var sv = stSh.getDataRange().getValues(), want = {}, order = []; for (var i = 1; i < sv.length; i++) { var id = str(sv[i][0]); if (!id) continue; var rows = apportRowsForStay_(sv[i]); if (rows.length) { want[id] = rows; order.push(id); } } var av = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), got = {}; for (var j = 1; j < av.length; j++) { var gid = str(av[j][1]); if (!gid) continue; if (!got[gid]) got[gid] = []; got[gid].push({ month: monthKey(av[j][2]) || str(av[j][2]).slice(0, 7), amount: round2(num(av[j][6])) }); } function sig(rows) { return rows.map(function (r) { return r.month + ':' + round2(r.amount).toFixed(2); }).sort().join('|'); } var bad = []; Object.keys(want).forEach(function (id) { if (sig(want[id]) !== sig(got[id] || [])) bad.push(id); }); Object.keys(got).forEach(function (id) { if (!want[id]) bad.push(id); }); // should contribute nothing if (!bad.length) return { checked: order.length, healed: 0 }; if (dry) return { checked: order.length, wouldHeal: bad.length, stays: bad.slice(0, 40) }; var keep = []; Object.keys(want).forEach(function (id) { want[id].forEach(function (r) { keep.push(r); }); }); keep.sort(function (a, b) { return a.flat === b.flat ? (a.month < b.month ? -1 : 1) : (a.flat < b.flat ? -1 : 1); }); apportWrite_(ss, keep); hubBump_(['base']); return { checked: order.length, healed: bad.length, stays: bad.slice(0, 40) }; } function apportionHealPrompt() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); try { var d = apportHealStep_(true); if (d.skipped) { ui.alert('Apportionment check\n\n' + d.skipped); return; } if (!d.wouldHeal) { ui.alert('Apportionment check ✓\n\n' + d.checked + ' stays checked — every one reconciles to what was paid.'); return; } var ok = ui.alert('Apportionment — heal', d.checked + ' stays checked.\n' + d.wouldHeal + ' do not match what they should contribute:\n\n ' + d.stays.join(', ') + '\n\nRewrite them on the agreed rule?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO); if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) { ui.alert('Nothing changed.'); return; } var r = apportHealStep_(false); ui.alert('Done ✓\n\n' + r.healed + ' stay(s) rewritten of ' + r.checked + ' checked.'); } catch (e) { ui.alert('Apportionment heal failed\n\n' + (e && e.message || e)); } } function monthSpan_(arrival, departure) { var out = [], ci = smDate_(arrival), co = smDate_(departure); for (var d = new Date(ci); d < co; d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1)) { var m = d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); if (out.indexOf(m) < 0) out.push(m); } return out; } function nightsInMonth_(ci, co, ym) { var y = +ym.slice(0, 4), mo = +ym.slice(5, 7); var ms = new Date(y, mo - 1, 1, 12), me = new Date(y, mo, 1, 12); var s = ci > ms ? ci : ms, e = co < me ? co : me; return Math.max(0, Math.round((e - s) / 86400000)); } function json(obj) { return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(obj)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); }
